# Papercuts

Shared log of friction that matching/pattern agents hit: tooling bugs, confusing instructions,
re-discovered tricks, wasted time. The orchestrator reviews this between batches and folds
fixes into tools, docs/matching.md and the agent prompts. Entries that have been addressed get
moved to "Resolved".

## How to add an entry (agents)
Append ONE entry with a single shell command so concurrent appends don't interleave:

    cat >> docs/Papercuts.md <<'PAPERCUT'
    - [<slice-or-pattern id>] <category: tooling|docs|codegen|toolchain|data> — <what cost you time>.
      Fix idea: <one line>. Time lost: ~<minutes>.
    PAPERCUT

Only log things that cost real time or that the next agent will hit too. Check whether an
existing entry already covers it; if so, don't duplicate it (you may append "+1 [<id>]" under it instead).

## Open
- [b004 s6-8] tooling — Ghidra's decompile of Havok x87 code is unreliable in three ways that only the asm shows: it drops `and eax,0x3fffffff` capacity masks (hkArray capacity in calcStatistics/dtors), it reorders float sums (e.g. `(dz*nz + dy*ny) + dx*nx` printed as dx*nx first) and it renders fcomp/fnstsw/`test ah,5|0x41` as plain `<=`/`<` (wrong for NaN). Also `card.py` hides `ecx` set-up before calls, so thiscall receivers (bodyA vs bodyB) must be read from the asm. Fix idea: have card.py print, for each x87 compare, the resolved C relational (and NaN side), and keep an `asm-only` mode (work/match/scratch_b004_asm.sh does this with awk). Time lost: ~30 minutes.

## Resolved
- [orchestrator] codegen/tooling: the /Od name-hash slot order (s0041dd90, s004248c0, s00426430, s0042d560, s0042a220, s0042f9d0, ~560 agent-minutes in total) is resolved by tools/matching/od_names.py fit K and documented in docs/matching.md "/Od frame layout".
- [orchestrator] tooling: cmpobj misread section numbers above 32k (signed 16-bit). Fixed in cmpobj/libmatch/libresolve.
- [orchestrator] tooling: hand-typed workflow args. Agents now resolve slices via slice_info.py / pattern_info.py.
- [orchestrator] batch 2 (32 entries, 2026-10-04): codegen lessons folded into docs/matching.md "Batch-2 lessons"; the slice-wide checker shipped as tools/matching/chk.py; scratch files go in work/match/scratch_<id>_* only (the shared scratchpad got clobbered). Still open as ideas: card.py printing callee conventions, run_all wildcard for ?A0x anonymous namespaces, a name-search tool for /Od frames.
- [b003 s21-23] codegen — three re-discovered tricks. (1) A same-TU callee body lets cl assume ECX is preserved and skip reloading `this` after the call; to get the original's out-of-line call with reloads, declare the callee in the class but define its body under another name (e.g. `InitCopy`) and manifest that name. (2) `mCond ? 1 : 0` args and chains of `||` returns differ from the original; separate `if (x) return true;` lines plus final `return false;` matched IsLookAroundIdle/IsIdle, and `uint8_t f=g(); f>>=2; f&=1; return f;` gives `shr al,2; and al,1`. (3) Mixed-up arg order of a __thiscall with pushed `idx` then `1` means (1, idx); and a `ret 8` function ending in `mov [esp+4],N; jmp [vtbl]` passes its second arg through (SetFlag(1, b)), which Ghidra's decompile drops.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons; also make cmpdis.py accept a chk.py output path/auto-pick the latest obj. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00623f90/s00624fb0/s00627d50] docs — Vector3 copy flavour decides codegen: a class with a user copy ctor passed by value gives fld/fstp per float and `T x(y)` gives movss, a plain POD struct copies via integer regs, and a POD returned from an inline helper and assigned goes through stack temps (movss [esp]; mov r,[esp]).
  Also: `cmp [slot],0` then `mov ecx,[slot]` could not be reproduced for loops over `ISubMode* mSubModes[4]` (compiler CSEs the load), and card.py's Ghidra header hides `add ecx, N` lines if you grep -v "add". Fix idea: add a "Vector3 copy flavour" bullet to docs/matching.md; have card.py print jump-table targets/byte maps for switch functions. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s0062f6e0/s00632780/s00634490] tooling — try_variants.py takes cl flags positionally (not --flags) and defaults to /EHsc without /GS-, so a `--flags "..."` argument silently compiles with garbage flags; chk.py's "best symbol" also hides functions whose size is off (shows "(no symbol of similar size)" or a wrong symbol), so use cmpdis/cmpobj with the exact mangled name when a VA looks missing.
  Fix idea: make try_variants accept --flags like chk.py, and have chk.py print the best symbol regardless of size. Also: a thiscall whose caller leaves 4 extra stack bytes (add esp,0x24 vs 0x20) usually means a variadic or callee-no-pop virtual, and a callee-saved register used as a constant (xor bl,bl then mov al,bl) means the source kept a named result variable. Time lost: ~25.
- [s00628d50/s0062da00/s0062e7e0] tooling — a helper script I named work/match/scratch_b003_d.sh (batch-id based) was silently overwritten by another agent of the same batch (different arg order), costing several confusing cmpdis failures. cmpdis.py also needs the chk.py object path (work/match/chk/<src>_<hash>.obj), which is not printed.
  Fix idea: tell agents to prefix scratch names with their slice id (not batch id), and have chk.py print the .obj path or accept `--dis <symbol> <va>` itself. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [b004 s0107e670/s01080450/s0107f4a0] data — Ghidra's decompile of x87 code prints sums of products in the opposite grouping from the asm (asm: `(z*m8 + y*m4) + x*m0`, Ghidra: `x*m0 + y*m4 + z*m8`), which would silently change float rounding in the Havok ports. Also `chk.py` prints rows like `(no symbol of similar size)` with no `diff` column, so scripts parsing it must handle that row.
      Fix idea: card.py should note "x87: derive sum grouping from the asm, not the decompile" in the FP rules and chk.py should print a `diff` column even for that row. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00618150/s0061fbd0/s00622f20] tooling — chk.py's "best symbol" column often names an unrelated symbol (a helper or another function) and prints "no symbol of similar size" for big near-misses, so cmpdis needs the right mangled substring each time; I wrote a 3-line wrapper that picks the newest obj in work/match/chk for a given source. Also `chk.py -a` lists only symbols within the size filter.
  Fix idea: let chk.py print "<va> <diff> <symbol>" for the symbol whose name contains a user-given substring (or write a --sym option), and have cmpdis default to the newest chk obj. Time lost: ~30 min.
- [s0061fbd0] codegen — a compiler-generated deleting dtor with no vptr stores for the derived class needs NO user dtor in the derived class but a virtual dtor in the base (CustomWinProc here); declaring `~Derived()` adds the derived vptr stores. Same for retail-layout MI classes: base vptrs only show up when the base's ctor/dtor is inline.
  Fix idea: add to the /Od-lessons-style list in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~20 min.
- [s0060d860/s00612270/s00613240] codegen — three recurring cl 15.00 shapes cost the most rounds. (1) `find()` in EASTL vector_map/set instances leaves a never-folded `lea ecx,[it+stride]; cmp it,ecx; jne` ("it == it+1 -> it = end") and is reproduced only by `if (it == e || key < *it) it = e; else if (it == it + 1) it = e;` followed by `return it != e` (a plain early-return form gets jump-threaded away). (2) `insert/DoInsertValue` pairs: `uninitialized_relocate(first,last,dest)` as ONE inline helper (copy then destruct with the same three args) loads mpBegin once like the original; `if (position == mpEnd && mpEnd != mpCapacity)`; intrusive_ptr/`if (it == end || key < k) it = insert(it, v); return it;` (this exact shape, not the `!(..)` or early-return forms) matched. (3) Locals that hold an address-taken scalar can silently reuse the dead home slot of an enregistered param in the original (frame 0x24 vs 0x20, `nArgs`/`count` at `[arg slot]`); not reproducible by renaming, left as near-miss. Also: inline `__asm` in an atomic helper blocks inlining (so it is not what the original used), and the original's `lock xadd ... dec edi` + `push ecx` temp in RefCountVTemplate::Release was not reproduced by any of ~12 spellings of `_InterlockedExchangeAdd(&v,-1) - 1`.
  Fix idea: card.py could flag "param home slot reused" frames; a shared `eastl_vector_map.h` with the find()/insert shapes above would save the next agent ~1 h. Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s010dcd40/s01102fc0/s010df100] tooling: chk.py's "best symbol" per VA is size-filtered, so with a one-TU source most rows name an unrelated symbol and the diff byte counts are only loosely meaningful (a correct function can show e.g. 150 diff against the wrong symbol). Also two concurrent agents used the same scratch name work/match/scratch_book.py (no slice id in it) and overwrote each other's script/bookkeeping.
  Fix idea: have chk.py prefer the symbol whose `// @ 0x<va>` comment matches the VA; remind agents that every scratch file needs the slice id in its name. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s0060a720/s0060b730/s0060c780] tooling — two things cost time: (1) the prompt's scratch pattern `scratch_<id>` is easy to read as the batch id (b003); concurrent agents of the same batch then share and overwrite `work/match/scratch_b003_*` (my helper script was clobbered mid-session), so scratch names must use the SLICE id. (2) Multiple-inheritance UI/HTTP classes need their vtables dumped to map each function to a base/slot; I wrote a throwaway script that reads vtable slots from SporeApp.analysis.bin and labels them with symbols/vtable_names.txt. Also, chk.py prints an unrelated "best symbol" when the intended function is emitted but differs by more than the best unrelated one; `-a` or cmpdis by symbol name is needed to see the real diff.
  Fix idea: ship tools/matching/vtdump.py <va> [n] (slots + names) and have card.py print the vtable slot index when a function address appears in a known vtable; make chk.py accept `--sym <substr>` to force the compared symbol. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [b003 s6-8] tooling: agents of one batch share the scratch prefix (work/match/scratch_b003_*), and another agent overwrote my helper script mid-run. Fix idea: give each agent a prefix with its slice ids (scratch_<id>_*) in the prompt. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [b003 s6-8] codegen: `_ReadWriteBarrier()` (<intrin.h>) is a free way to pin a vptr/field store before a load that cl hoists above it (inlined dtors, "store base vtable then load member" patterns); matched 5 dtors. Also: `if (x == 0) {A} else {B}` vs `if (x != 0) {B} else {A}` decides which block is outlined; a new-expression with a class-specific `operator new` (not placement new plus `if`) gives the separate null-path `Enqueue(0,1)`.
- [b003 s27-29] codegen — (1) Methods whose `this` is a secondary-base subobject (MI, `this` = object+4) show shifted offsets (tree at +0xa10 in one method, +0xa14/anchor +0xa18 in the ctor); reconcile by checking the ctor's stores against each method's find()/erase() before inventing struct layouts. (2) An empty tag struct (no user ctor) passed by value gives `mov byte [slot],0; mov eax,[slot]; push eax` and reuses a dead param slot; an iterator/sret returned as `tree.find(k).n` (temp) reproduces `mov eax,[eax]` after the call, while a named `Iter it` is read from the local. (3) A loose `/fp:fast` is needed on top of `/arch:SSE` in this module (comiss/xorps for `0.0f < x`, fld/fstp for float copies). Shared stub types can live in `s<id>.h` in the first slice dir and be included as "../s<id>/s<id>.h".
      Fix idea: card.py could flag secondary-subobject `this` (callers pass `lea ecx,[reg+4]`), and chk.py could print the matched symbol even when the size filter rejects it. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [b003 s24-26] tooling/codegen — (1) cmpdis.py normalizes a masked `call` into `<+0x..>` (rel32 reloc is 0 in our obj), so every call line shows as a diff and hides the real one; I used a copy that prints all calls as `<T>` (sed on the `"<ext>"` branch). (2) Re-found tricks: x87 float code with fcompi/fild needs `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` (not just /fp:fast); a float forwarded as `mov ecx,[esp+N]; push ecx` is a uint32 param; identical-tail merging of two `if/else` arms gives the shared `mov eax,[eax]` after the join (write two separate calls, not a ternary, which adds ctor-flag temps); 64-bit `v == -1` gives `and eax,edx; cmp eax,-1`; a dead store of a field before an overwrite survives only if the object is assigned via `ResourceKey k; k = *p; k.typeID = X;` (default ctor + operator=), not via copy-ctor; a `switch` with the right case values reproduces cl's compare tree exactly (28 cases, 29 byte diff only from load hoisting).
      Fix idea: make cmpdis.py mask all call targets; add the float-flags and switch lessons to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [b005 s4-7] docs — hk31_base.h gives hkReferencedObject a `virtual void slot1() {}` with no parameters, so a class that overrides calcStatistics(hkStatisticsCollector*) (vtable slot 1) in slices 6/7 declares a NEW virtual instead of overriding slot 1 (vtable order wrong when joined). Also hkBool has no default ctor, so structs with hkBool members need plain bool stubs. Fix idea: add `virtual void calcStatistics(hkStatisticsCollector*) const` and a default hkBool ctor to hk31_base.h before joining slices. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [b005 s12-15] tooling — (1) The prompt's scratch pattern `work/match/scratch_b005_*` was shared by every agent of the batch: another agent overwrote my helper scripts (vtable reader) mid-session, so helper scripts must be named with the SLICE id (scratch_<slice>_*), as the earlier papercut already said. (2) A function that looks like it pops one word too few (`push ecx; push ebx; push esi` ... `pop ebx; pop ecx; ret`) is NOT a fragment: `push ecx` is just a 4-byte local, `push esi` is a call argument consumed by the callee's `ret 8`, and the single stack argument belongs to a cdecl caller (0x01082910 = hkWorld_updatePhantomOverlap, this=EDI, other=ESI, filter on the stack). Simulate the stack from entry before declaring a fragment. (3) card.py can start a card in the middle of the real function (it printed 0x01084d90 as a function; the symbol starts at 0x01084cf0), so check the named neighbours before trusting a card's first lines.
- [b005 s8-11] tooling — no tool dumps vtable dwords by VA (the playbook and the multiple-inheritance redo item ask for it); I wrote a pefile script (work/match/scratch_s010cbaa0_vt.py) to look slot functions up in havok_names.txt, which settled class identity in minutes (hkBvShape, hkNullCollisionFilter, hkSymmetricAgent<T>, hkBvTreeAgent vs hkMoppAgent). Also card.py output for a 20-function slice exceeds the 30 KB tool-output limit, so it has to be paged (hex-byte column stripped, ~35% smaller).
      Fix idea: add tools/matching/vtdump.py <va> [n] (slot -> symbol) and a card.py --compact flag that drops the hex column. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s0064dbd0, opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash; appended by orchestrator] tooling/codegen: `__thiscall` is rejected on free-function declarations (C3865), but accepted inside function-pointer casts like `((void(__thiscall*)(void*,int))vtbl[i])(p,1)`. Direct thiscall callees must be declared as members of a stub class, not as `extern void __thiscall f(void*,...)`. Fix idea: one-line note in "Writing matchable source".
  Also: chk.py's size-filtered "best symbol" mapped VA 0064e880 to another function's mangled name, so the diff and name were misleading. Workaround: cmpdis.py with the exact symbol. (+ several Claude agents reported the same.) Time lost: ~20 min.
- [s0064eb60] codegen — reproducing a refcount add/release that goes through a *secondary* base (`lea ecx,[eax+0x10]; mov eax,[ecx]; call [eax]`) needs the stub object to inherit from a **polymorphic** primary base plus a polymorphic refcount base. Writing the first base as a non-polymorphic `char pad[0x10]` makes MSVC hoist the only vtbl-bearing base to offset 0 (`mov ecx,[eax]; call [ecx]`), which is off by the `+0x10`. Fix: give the primary base a dummy virtual (`virtual void slot(); char pad[0xc];`) so the refcount vptr lands at +0x10. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [w1g2 s005300d0..s00537f40] codegen — the Swarm skin-paint module is /Od and dominated by EASTL/basic_string/vector template instantiations whose byte-exactness hinges on the /Od local-name-hash slot order, not on the source logic. Concrete: in `basic_string::WriteString`, swapping only the two local names (`p`<->`z`) turned a 4-byte diff into a MATCH; the vector copy-ctor / DoInsertValue / erase instantiations (element strides 8, 16 and 0x1c) additionally need the three `eastl::copy` tag bools. Fix idea: for /Od template instantiations, spend the budget on `od_names.py fit K` name sets rather than on re-deriving the algorithm.
  Also: two of the slices contained single functions of 2713 and 6768 bytes (`cSPSkinPaintDistributeEffect::ApplyEffect` and the distribute algorithm); "give up after ~5 rounds" cannot bound a per-function budget when one function is 6 KB, so those were left as partial skeletons. Fix idea: slice planning could cap function byte size (~1500 B) and route oversized functions into an explicit partial bucket. Time lost: ~2 h across the 7 slices.
- [s00577130, opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash] codegen — a helper member declared in the class but DEFINED as an empty stub `{}` in the same TU gets inlined/removed by /O2, so the target caller loses its call entirely (two functions reduced to a tail-jump). Declaring the helper (and any auto-refcount AddRef/Release) WITHOUT a body forces the out-of-line `call` the original has. Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons: "never body-stub a helper you want called out-of-line". Time lost: ~20 min.
- [w1g4 s51-62] codegen — three reusable shapes found for the /O2 cSPUIAssetBrowser/cSPUIAssetGrid region (all matched byte-exact). (1) Hashtable/set insert wrappers take an **empty tag struct** (no user ctor): `map.insert(key, EmptyTag())` reproduces the odd `mov byte [esp+argslot],0; mov reg,[esp+argslot]; push reg` tag argument. (2) When a set element stores a refcounted pointer through a **secondary base at +0x10**, the release is `mov eax,[e]; test eax,eax; je; lea ecx,[eax+0x10]; mov eax,[ecx]; call [eax+4]` — model it as a class deriving from a polymorphic primary base (a dummy virtual + `char pad[0xc]`) plus a polymorphic `RefCount` base; writing the guard as a **named local pointer** (`T* p = e->p10; if (p) p->Release();`) is what yields the load-once instead of `cmp [mem],0`. (3) For a /O2 function where two locals are live across two calls and one is spilled (`mov [esp+N],eax` then reload for the 2nd call), the register assigned to each local follows **declaration order (last-declared first)**: swapping the two declarations turned a 9-byte diff into a MATCH (vector::reserve). Also a stack struct containing `uint8_t pad[N]` needs `/GS-` at /O2 or cl injects a `__security_cookie`.
  Fix idea: add the empty-tag, secondary-base/local-pointer and last-declared-first rules to the Batch-2 lessons list. Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s011fab10/s0120c440/s0120e150] data — slices 1 and 2 of cl1_bfs4 are 80 pure MSVC EH unwind funclets (tail-jmp into dtors via the caller's ebp); they have no standalone C++ source, so the "finish every function" goal cannot apply and they stay partial placeholders. Also the RenderWare constant setters call the D3D vtable with `call [ecx+N]` while cl 15.00 /O2 (even /Os) emits `mov eax,[ecx+N]; call eax` or a push-ebp frame, so none of them can be byte-exact; and `Vec4` structs only copy as movaps when the type holds an `__m128` union member.
  Fix idea: have the slicer skip/mark `__unwindfunclet` entry points (or attach them to the enclosing function's slice) and note the call-[reg+N] toolset ceiling in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00885ad0/s00880170 bfs4] tooling — manifests with a relative include flag (`/Iscratch_s00880170_inc`) only verify when run_all.py is started from work/match (cl resolves it against the process cwd); from the repo root they fail with C1083, and a flag containing `/` breaks run_all's object-file name (`/Iwork/match/...` -> "Cannot open compiler generated file"). Retail-vs-vendored class layout gaps can be patched without editing vendor headers by `#define <last member> <member>; pad` around the header include only.
      Fix idea: have run_all.py cwd = work/match (or sanitize `/` in obj names) and document the macro-injection trick. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s0088b250/s008d91e0/s008e1d50 bfs4] tooling — manifest flag overrides with a relative include such as `/Iscratch_s00880170_inc` only work when run_all.py/chk.py are launched from work/match (cl resolves it against cwd); from the repo root they fail with "no object" (and a flag containing a slash breaks run_all's obj_path, which uses the flags in the file name). Many EAWebKit slices (s00880170, s00885ad0, s0088b250, ...) use it. Also chk.py's "best symbol" column often names the wrong function (picked by size), so use cmpdis.py with the mangled substring.
  Fix idea: make run_all/chk run cl with cwd=ROOT and accept /I paths relative to the repo root, sanitising all non-alphanumerics in obj_path; or ship the EAWebKit include tree under match/include. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s008d91e0/s008e1d50 bfs4] codegen — (1) a MEMBER with a user ctor/dtor (Mutex, FileStream, hash tables, an `RBAnchor() : left(0), parent(0), color(0)` member) reproduces the original's member-init order and dead-store pattern; hand-written stores in the ctor body get dead-store-eliminated. Real member subobjects also gave an exact ~Manager and ~DatabasePackedFile (auto reverse destruction + a base class whose dtor stores the base vtable). (2) A derived-class vptr store before member init needs a tiny intermediate base class with a ctor that stores the vtables. (3) Rotated list loops: `do { if (match) goto found; n = n->next; } while (n != head); return;` matched where `while (cond) {...}` did not. (4) `eastl::set/map` key compare is `if (a.x != b.x) return a.x < b.x; if (a.z != b.z) return a.z < b.z; return a.y < b.y;`, which gives cmp/jne then a shared setb.
  Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~0 (notes).
- [bfs4 s00902460/s009234f0/s00929a70] tooling — chk.py's size-filtered "best symbol" mislabels near-miss functions (e.g. VA 00929bf0 showed another function's symbol), and python `str.index("// 0x92aa20")` on a marker that also appears in a struct comment silently duplicated half a file twice. Static helpers with register args (eax=list, ebx=owner, 0x9032b0) need a stand-in caller in the same TU that keeps the owner in a callee-saved register or cl passes it on the stack. ITracer-side methods (this = Server+4) are best modelled as a separate view struct with the secondary-base fields, not as Server members.
      Fix idea: chk.py `--sym <mangled>` to force the symbol per VA; document the stand-in-caller trick in Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s008fc670/s008fd6c0 bfs4] codegen — (1) a thiscall-with-ecx-this MI subobject method (HandleMessage at HttpHandler+0xc) is best written on an empty `struct IMsgSub` with `self = (HttpHandler*)((char*)this - 0xc)`; field offsets then fold into displacements like the original. (2) `strlen` inlines to the `lea esi,[eax+1]; mov dl,[eax]; inc eax; test dl,dl` idiom only after `#pragma intrinsic(strlen)`, and `strcmp` after `#pragma intrinsic(strcmp)` (pair-unrolled loop + sbb/sbb). (3) FNV loop `h=(h*K)^c` over a wide string matches only with an `unsigned c = *s; while (c != 0) { ++s; h = (h*K)^c; c = *s; }` shape (casting `(uint16_t)*s` adds movzx and `test ax,ax`). (4) Never python-slice a .cpp with `s[:a]+new+s[b:]` when b < a (it duplicates the tail); index b relative to a.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~30 minutes on (4).
- [s0092e3d0/s00931460] codegen — two cl-15 idioms that cost time: (1) a plain strlen loop of the form `lea edx,[eax+2]; mov cx,[eax]; add eax,2; test cx,cx; jne` is the `wcslen()` intrinsic (hand-written loops give movzx ecx); a loop `cmp [eax],0; add eax,2` is a hand loop. (2) `_alldiv/_allmul` must be declared `__stdcall` (callee pops 16 bytes), `__cdecl` adds a stray `add esp,0x10`. Also: wchar-array locals need `/GS-` here (no cookie in this module) and EH-less classes need no `/EHsc`. chk.py matches by size window, so inline helpers emitted as separate symbols can shadow the real pairing: use cmpdis with the exact mangled name.
  Fix idea: put these in the matching.md levers list. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s008fe610/s008ff610] codegen — five tricks that moved functions from 100+ diff to byte-exact. (1) A dtor/ctor that works on `this` sub-objects (edi = &mWaiting, `[edi+8]`, `lea edi,[esi+0x13c]`) is reproduced by giving each member struct an inline `Destroy()`/ctor and calling it from the owner; hand-expanded field accesses on the owner give a different register shape (ResourceProvider dtor matched). (2) A refcount release `if (--mRef==0){mRef=1; delete}` needs `int n = (*(volatile int*)&o->mRef += -1); if (n==0){ o->mRef = 1; _ReadWriteBarrier(); o->v0(1); }` to keep the `mov [ecx+4],1` store before the vtable load (matched vector erase and InsertValue; helper must be __forceinline). (3) A store through `void**` (inlined list Find's `*out = p`) forces cl to reload an earlier member, so use the real helper body inline rather than hand-merged loops; copy ctor order = member order, so put the first scalar members in the mem-init list and let sub-object default ctors (memset, list self-link) follow. (4) An iterator returned by find() is 8 bytes (node, slot) and insert returns a 12-byte {node, slot, bool}; sharing one 12-byte struct changes frame size. (5) `erase(iterator)` writing into the same variable is `mWaiting.erase(&wi, wi)` (out ptr + by-value iterator), not a separate temp. Also the empty tag arg `true_type()` is `struct TagT {}` passed by value.
  Fix idea: add (1) and (2) to the Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~0 (found while iterating), saved ~2 h.
- [s008fe610/s008ff610] tooling — chk.py does not list a VA at all when no emitted symbol is within its size filter (e.g. Request/operator[]), so a bookkeeping script that parses chk output silently drops those functions from both manifest and nonmatching; also cmpdis counts every masked `call` and `push <addr>` as a diff. I used scripts under work/match/scratch_s008fe610_* (VA->symbol map, per-VA byte diff via the same masking as chk, instruction-diff count with call lines filtered).
  Fix idea: let chk.py print "(no symbol of similar size)" with the nearest-size symbol's diff, and have cmpdis skip call/push-addr lines. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s00945ed0/s00947d50] codegen — the UTFInternet region (0x945ed0-0x94a000) is /O2 with `/arch:SSE` or `/arch:SSE2` and NO /EHsc (flags `/O2 /MD /Gy /TP /GS- /arch:SSE`): `cmova`/`cmovbe` min() and fld1/fstp float args both come out right only with an /arch flag, and a stack object with a dtor gets no EH frame. Re-found tricks: (1) a function that destroys by-value class params in its own epilogue and does `mov ecx,esi; call ctor` with no null test is `Pair f(str8 key, Info info){ return Pair(key,info); }` (sret), not a placement-new (which adds a null check); (2) inline string copy ctors need `__forceinline` plus a `RangeInitialize(pBegin,pEnd)` helper called as `(o.mpBegin, o.mpEnd)` (right-to-left arg evaluation makes the end load first) and `AllocateSelf((pEnd-pBegin)+1); memcpy(dst,pBegin,pEnd-pBegin)` without a named `n`; (3) an inline wcscat of a literal only gives the original's strlen loop + `rep movsd/movsb` if the source is an `extern const wchar_t[]` (a literal gets folded to unrolled movs); (4) hash-table iteration is matched by the hand-written idiom `do { n = p[1]; ++p; } while (!n);` after `n = n->next`, and an interior-ptr to a `Mutex` stays on the stack unless it is a local `Mutex* m = &mMutex` (that did not fix the 1.6 KB WorkerThreadFunction).
      Fix idea: add the UTFInternet flags and the by-value-sret and wcscat bullets to docs/matching.md; chk.py's best-symbol column mislabels most rows in a one-TU file with many same-size functions (I used cmpdis with the real mangled name). Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00958a70/s0095b310] codegen — (1) A module with member dtors (UTFWin WindowMgr) is built WITHOUT /EHsc: compiling with the default /EHsc wraps the ctor/dtor in EH registration, so the chk.py/run_all flags override must drop /EHsc (then real member-init-list ctors, ~WM with inline member dtors and `??_G` deleting dtors match byte-for-byte; smart-pointer members need an inline `~Ref(){ if(p) p->Release(); }`). (2) Two methods of one class can be a `this`=list helper (EraseRange(Iter first, Iter last) returning Iter by value = sret) — passing iterators as one-member structs by value is what keeps `first` in its param home slot. (3) Placement-new null checks (`test edi,edi` after alloc) come from `new (&n->msg) Msg(src)` with a user `operator new(size_t,void*)`. (4) Python str.index("// @ 0x...") on a source that also has the decl comment in the class matched the wrong spot three times when splicing edits; anchor on "\n// @ ..." at column 0.
  Fix idea: note "no /EHsc when the class has member dtors" in docs/matching.md; try_variants/chk could print the EH-prolog hint when size differs by exactly the EH registration. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00948de0/s009568c0 (cl1_bfs4 resume)] codegen — recurring cl 15.00 levers found this run. (1) A by-value class arg built in place (`f(String(p), Info())`, no temp+rep movs copy, no caller dtor) needs the class to have a USER-DECLARED copy ctor (declare it, never define it); with the implicit copy ctor cl builds a temp and copies. (2) The EASTL INetFileCache module has no EH frames: compile with `/O2 /MD /Gy /TP /GS- /arch:SSE2` (no /EHsc) and declare the dealloc helper `throw()`. (3) Condition-heavy tiny functions match only with NO named locals (`if (pt[0]>=0 && pt[0]<mWidth && ...)` matched, `int x=pt[0]` did not); `if (g_p) { delete g_p; g_p = 0; }` matched where a local copy + explicit ~T()/operator delete did not; EASTL-style upper_bound with `const T* mid = first+half; if (!(key < *mid)) {first=mid+1; n-=half+1;} else n=half;` matched where the swapped arm order did not. (4) A same-TU function called before its definition is still inlined by /O2; mark the definition `__declspec(noinline)` when the original calls it out of line. (5) Fastest loop for variants: put the variants in a small standalone scratch TU and run try_variants.py (seconds); main-file compiles take ~15 s and chk.py's "best symbol" column often names the wrong function, so use cmpdis.py with the exact mangled name.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(4) to the Batch-2 lessons; ship a variants harness that splices N renamed copies of one function into the slice source. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s009aa590/s009b0e90] codegen — (1) functions with `ret N` that write `[eax]=x` through an arg and end with `mov eax,<argptr>` are EASTL iterator-returning methods (sret `Iter f(...)` with `struct Iter{Node* n;}`), not `void f(Node** out,...)`; and `push ecx; mov eax,esp; mov [eax],edx` as an arg means a by-value struct with a user copy ctor (give Iter/HP `Iter(const Iter&)`). (2) EASTL vector DoInsertValue matched only after writing `pNewEnd = UCopy(position, e, ++pNewEnd)` (pre-increment inside the call args) and `nNewSize = nPrev > 0 ? 2*nPrev : 1` with unsigned types. (3) chk.py's best-symbol size filter hides near-miss functions whose size differs (shows another function's name); use cmpdis with the exact mangled name (find names with `rg -a -o "\?[A-Za-z0-9_@?\$]+" <obj>`).
  Fix idea: chk.py `--sym <substr>` to force the symbol, plus cmpdis taking a chk.py slice id. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00996f10/s00997f50/s00999110] codegen — (1) free `FUN_x(char* self, ...)` stubs left by an earlier agent are cdecl, so every diff shows 200+ bytes until they become member functions of a stub struct (thiscall). (2) A virtual call or non-throw() extern inside a dtor that has members with dtors adds an EH frame (push -1/fs:[0]); the original has none, so write cleanup explicitly (no member dtors) and mark externs throw(); `StringTableFilter`-style hashtable members need an inline member dtor plus `~X(){}` to get the early vptr store. (3) Callee-no-pop virtuals (args left on stack, `add esp,0x10` after) cannot be written in cl 15 (varargs member pushes this); a function with a local filter object (vptr+hashtable with float policy) came out with an unexplained `and esp,-16` aligned frame in my source.
  Fix idea: add these to the /O2 levers list; chk.py -a should list all symbols in the object so a dtor/member can be found without guessing mangled names. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00a20060/s00a22220] codegen — three recurring wins: (1) `new (p) T(*src)` (placement new with a user copy ctor and an `operator new(unsigned, void*)`) reproduces the null-check + per-iteration EH state of eastl::uninitialized_copy; a hand-written try/catch does not. (2) A smart-pointer member assignment written as an inline `ARef::operator=` called from `AR::operator=` fixes the load order that a flat inline body hoisted; take its out-of-line instance from a `AR& (AR::*g)(const AR&) = &AR::operator=;` global. (3) `return a < b;` as bool gives sbb/neg; `if (a < b) return true; return false;` gives setb. Also: an embedded polymorphic subobject at +4 (`lea ecx,[edi+4]`) is a member with its own vptr, not a pointer; chk.py's "best symbol" names the wrong function for ctor/dtor/EH-heavy VAs so cmpdis needs the exact mangled name (an unreferenced virtual dtor is only emitted if a `new T` forces the vtable).
  Fix idea: chk.py `--sym` option, and a cmpdis default of the newest chk obj. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00a24e10] codegen — three tricks that finished 6 functions: (1) two `new(name,0,0,0,0) T(...)` calls only get the original's EH state stores (`[esp+N]=ptr; state=1`) if a matching 6-arg placement `operator delete` is declared; (2) an out-of-line identical-code-folded callee called from two branches (same ctor address) needs two distinct stub classes, or cl tail-merges the duplicate alloc blocks; (3) a hash-table iteration needs EASTL-style `Iter{node,bucket}` with `IncrBucket(){++b; while(!*b)++b; node=*b;}` and `Incr(){node=node->next; while(!node){++b; node=*b;}}` plus an inline `end()` member (not a local copy of the bucket array) to reproduce the unrolled first-bucket check. Also `__forceinline` on inline ctors fixes an out-of-line PropList ctor call, and `_ReadWriteBarrier()` at the top of a derived dtor pins the vptr store before the member load.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [op1_big s4 / s010b8b30] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" whenever the compiled function is more than 12.5% off the original's size, which is normal for complete-but-unmatched huge functions (10 KB scalar helpers vs 19 KB hkVector4 original), so you get no diff signal and have to run cmpobj.py by hand (60 KB of output). Also, `static inline` Havok helpers were NOT inlined at /O2 in a 19 KB function (it came out 3.6 KB with out-of-line calls); Havok math needs `__forceinline` (HK_FORCE_INLINE). Separately, the symbol name `hkPoweredChain_buildJacobians` on 010b8b30 was wrong: it is hkSolveConstraints. Fix idea: add a chk.py `--any-size` flag that reports diff + size gap for the named symbol. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s006196c0] data — Ghidra stack-variable names for big no-EH functions are useless (unaff_EBP, aliased piStack_*); the card's asm needed manual esp-offset tracking (outstanding pushes shift every [esp+N]). Also globals like 0x1634104 read as 0.0 in the image (runtime-initialised), so a decompile's "DAT_" floats must stay extern globals while sqrt(<double table>) calls are literal fsqrt forms. Fix idea: card.py could print esp-normalised frame offsets (base = post-prologue esp). Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s010b48b0] data/tooling — the old symbol/skeleton for 0x010b48b0 ("hkPoweredChain_solve", "0x80-byte records") was wrong: it is the Havok 3.1 solver pass over the jacobian-schema stream (types 0..0x19 match the _hk*BuildJacobian emitters in symbols/havok_names.txt). Ghidra hides that case 5 is "two pair-contacts then jump-thread into case 10" (its `if (*p == '\n') break;`), and card.py has no jump-table dump, so I decoded the table with pefile. Also: `tools/progress.py --groups` raised KeyError 'id' while work/batches/op1_big.json was being (re)written; it parsed fine moments later, so a half-written/foreign-format batch JSON crashes it.
  Fix idea: card.py prints switch tables (case -> target); progress.py skips batch records without "id" (or writes batch JSON atomically via tmp+rename). Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s0099bf80/s009a0d60/s009a1d90] codegen — "custom register convention" helpers (arg in EAX/ESI/EDI/ECX, `ret N` with ECX not `this`) are NOT LTCG: declare them `static` (internal linkage) with an explicit `__stdcall` (or plain static for `ret` w/o N) plus a non-static caller so they are emitted, and cl picks the same registers; matched 0099c910 and 009a0eb0 this way (member functions of anonymous-namespace classes did not work). Also `fp:fast` module flag is needed whenever sqrt/sin/acos show as inline fsqrt/fsin, `#pragma intrinsic(sqrt)` alone is not enough; and `uint->float` compares use x87 fild+fadd 2^32 under /arch:SSE (write `(float)(uint)x`). A local `memcmp(a,b,16)==0` with `#pragma intrinsic(memcmp)` gives the inline dword-compare loop.
  Fix idea: add to docs/matching.md Batch-2 lessons; ship work/match/scratch_multi.py style "one compile, many (va, symbol-substring)" comparer as a chk.py option (chk's best-symbol column picks wrong symbols). Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [op1_big s5 / s00ab3370] tooling — for huge functions chk.py prints "-  (no symbol of similar size)" once my size is >1/8 off (15073 vs 17323 here), so there is no diff count to track completeness; I had to compare mnemonic counts (call/fsqrt/cvtss2si/fild/comiss) between objdump of the .obj and the card by hand. Also the existing symbol guess (hkSimulation_integrateStep) was wrong: it is EA::Swarm::cRibbonEffect, found via pdb_type.py cRibbonEffect / cRibbonDescription (retail vectors are 0x14 bytes, so dev offsets shift). Fix idea: chk.py --force-size (always diff the named symbol) plus an opcode-histogram mode. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s007864c0] tooling — Ghidra constant-folds `fld qword [c]; fsqrt` into plain literals (1.8708287 = sqrt(3.5)) and shows x87 qword spills of those values as `double` locals, so a Ghidra-based rewrite silently loses all 237 fsqrt and the double precision. The fix was to read each fsqrt operand from the binary and map literals back to `sqrt(p/q)`; `#pragma intrinsic(sqrt)` plus `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` then reproduces the SSE+x87 mix. For huge math functions, a native clang build of the slice with invariant checks (orthogonality, homomorphism) verified the transcription in seconds. Also: `tools/progress.py --groups` raised KeyError 'id' once while a work/batches/*.json was being rewritten; it no longer reproduced. Fix idea: card.py could annotate each fsqrt with its operand value, and progress.py could skip records with no `id`. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00d76000/s0101fb40] docs — card.py disassembly lists thiscall args pushed BEFORE the receiver getter call (e.g. `push 0; push edx; push eax; call 0xb3d440 (zero-arg singleton getter, plain ret); mov ecx,eax; call 0xb09b80 (ret 0xC)`); Ghidra then shows the getter with 3 args. Check each callee's `ret N` (tools/matching/disasm.py <image> <va>) to tell getter vs argful callee.
  Fix idea: have card.py annotate each call with the callee's ret imm (ret N / plain ret). Time lost: ~30.
- [s0101fb40] codegen — a function-scope local array (even `Property* loc[2]`) or aliasing struct/union local forces an `and esp,-8`/ebp frame; block-scope locals do not. Also `float*float` global math needs `/fp:fast` else x87 `fmul qword`.
  Fix idea: note in docs/matching.md /O2 levers. Time lost: ~25.
- [op1_big s00441440] tooling — for a 23 KB /Od function the chk.py diff count (20619/23469) says nothing about completeness, because every stack offset differs. What worked instead: compare the ORIGINAL vs compiled sequence of (a) direct calls resolved through the obj's COFF relocations, (b) vtable slots of `call reg` (track the last `mov reg,[reg+off]`), and (c) pushed property-hash immediates (work/match/scratch_s00441440_seq.py / _calls.py). Identical hash order and vcall order, and call counts within 4, prove nothing is missing. Also hashnames.py needs the SporeModder-FX registry dir, which is not in the repo (one was under an old session's scratchpad). Annotating the asm with property names and float constants (pefile reads of `[0x...]`) turned 5300 lines of asm into readable source.
  Fix idea: ship a `tools/matching/structdiff.py <obj> <sym> <va>` that prints these three sequences side by side, and keep the property registry under work/ext. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [cl1_bfs4 s39-41] codegen — three tricks found. (1) A callee that takes a hidden ESI arg (Ghidra "unaff_ESI", e.g. the audio allocator wrappers) matches if the helper is `static __declspec(noinline)` in the same TU taking the value as an extra last param: cl picks ESI itself. Needs `/GS-` when the function has a char[N] local whose original had no cookie. (2) Functions with EH frames (ctors/dtors with members) get a /GS cookie by default; the originals have none, so give those manifest lines a `/GS-` override. (3) The extra unwind-state number (0,1,3) in a ctor means a member with a user dtor but trivial ctor (e.g. an AutoRefCount member storing 0) sits between bases and the next member. Also: `Command::Find`-style record walkers match with a `u32* p` cursor (`id=*p++; sz=*p++; ty=*p++`), not indexed loads, and 3 floats copied through locals (`a=p[0],b=p[1],c=p[2]; out[0]=a;...`) give the "fld/fstp then movss" pattern.
      Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons; have cmpdis.py take a slice id and auto-pick the latest chk obj. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00f7d9d0 op1_big] tooling — for huge call-free SIMD functions (41 KB here), two throwaway tools replaced reading 8,400 instructions: (1) a symbolic tracer (capstone, concrete ints, hash-consed xmm lane expressions) that prints the per-texel formula of every dst store, which exposed the 9 bodies as 3 loop shapes x 3 axis permutations and the exact float grouping/drift chains; (2) a unicorn differential test that runs the original and our compiled obj (mini COFF linker for DIR32/REL32) on random inputs and compares dst memory + callee-saved regs. It proved behavioural equivalence (1,200 cases, NaN/inf params) on the first compile. Scripts: work/match/scratch_s00f7d9d0_sym.py, work/match/scratch_s00f7d9d0_diff.py. Also: MSVC x86 rejects more than 3 by-value __m128 params (C2719); use const __m128& in inline helpers.
  Fix idea: promote the diff harness to tools/matching/difftest.py (args: obj, symbol, VA, arg generator) for any leaf function, and mention it in the big-function playbook. Time lost: ~0 (saved hours).
- [op1_big s01025840] tooling — For 16 KB functions card.py is hard to use: it doesn't annotate string literals containing `\n` or of length 1 (most ArgScript format strings, L"Y"), stack offsets shift with every push, and many callee names are identical-code-folding aliases (e.g. 0x1021240 "cSPMission::IsArchived" is really GetActiveStarRecord). What worked: (1) work/match/scratch_s01025840_esp.py rewrites `[esp+N]` to frame-relative `[L<off>]` (pops thiscall args using each callee's `ret N`); (2) work/match/scratch_s01025840_dev.py disassembles the same function in the 2008 dev exe (work/devbuild/SporeBin + symbols.json, thunks resolved) for real callee names; (3) work/match/scratch_s01025840_seg.py diffs the string-literal/call sequence of the compiled .obj against the original, which proves completeness block by block. Fix idea: fold these into card.py (`--frame`, `--dev`) and a `chk.py --calls` mode. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s004d5020] tooling — `tools/progress.py --groups` crashed with `KeyError: 'id'` at line 29 (`_slice_batches[_rec["id"]]`), but it ran cleanly again a few minutes later. All work/batches/*.json are now lists of {"id","vas"} records, and op1_big.json had just been rewritten (10:32), so the crash most likely came from a batch file caught mid-write or in an older record shape. Also: cl 15 rejects `3.4028235e+38f` with C2177 "constant too big"; write FLT_MAX as `3.402823466e+38F`.
      Fix idea: write batch files atomically (tmp + rename) and have progress.py skip records without "id" (`_rec.get("id")`). Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [s009f4660] tooling — chk.py only scores symbols within +-1/8 of the original size, so a complete-but-bigger rewrite of a huge function (24.6 KB vs 21.7 KB) prints "no symbol of similar size" and no diff count; had to run cmpobj.py/cmpdis.py by hand. For completeness checks on big functions, comparing the call list and the set of non-stack store offsets (orig vs obj) was far more useful than the byte diff.
  Fix idea: chk.py --any-size (or fall back to the exact symbol name) plus a --calls/--stores summary mode. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s009f4660] tooling — the user hit `tools/progress.py --groups` -> KeyError: 'id' at the work/batches/*.json scan (line 29, `_slice_batches[_rec["id"]]`). Not reproducible a few minutes later (every batch file now has ids and progress.py runs), so most likely a batch json (op1_big.json, mtime 10:32) was read mid-write or briefly had a different record shape.
  Fix idea: skip non-dict / id-less records there (`if isinstance(_rec, dict) and "id" in _rec`) and wrap json.load in try/except, like 5038a12 did for card.py/status.py. Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [s00470bd0/s00498470] codegen — (1) editor-region /Od modules use `/Od /Ob1 /arch:SSE /fp:fast` (without /fp:fast cl emits cvtss2sd/comisd or x87 compares) and some have NO /EHsc: callee-destroyed smart-pointer params (AutoRef by value) otherwise drag in an SEH frame. (2) Inline min/max helpers `float r; if (a>=b) r=a; else r=b; return r;` plus named locals matched 00470f20 exactly; /Od inline results get their own slots. (3) A by-value class param with user copy ctor copies fld/fstp from `*p` but movss from locals/params; POD structs copy via dword regs; one out-of-line copy ctor call (Vec3 0x4098a0) needs a separate class. (4) Frame addressed via esp after `and esp,-8` in /Od (s00471830) could not be reproduced with a u64 local or align(8).
  Fix idea: chk.py name-hill-climb (random /Od local names, one TU, -a ranking) would save the manual loop; the byte-diff objective is too flat for big functions. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00e47930] docs — a single 4.7 KB UI function: cmpdis.py's difflib output is dominated by branch-target and stack-offset noise once any frame slot shifts, so byte-exact work needs a filter (rg -v "<\+0x|<ext>|<addr>"). Also card.py's Ghidra header mislabels getters (DatabasePackedFile::Get = a window getter) and shows pushed floats as hex.
  Fix idea: cmpdis option to ignore branch deltas and print only the first diverging hunk. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s009eddc0] codegen — In an 11 KB /arch:SSE /fp:fast function, `static inline` helpers were NOT inlined (cl's per-function inline budget ran out) and the C++ float overloads `sqrt(float)`/`atan2(float,float)` from <math.h> became calls to `sqrtf`/`atan2f`; the original's inline fsqrt/fpatan need `(float)sqrt((double)x)` / `atan2((double)y,(double)x)` plus `#pragma intrinsic`, and helpers need `__forceinline`. chk.py prints "no symbol of similar size" (size filter n0/8) instead of the real size, which hid both. Fix idea: chk.py could print the nearest-size symbol and its byte size when nothing passes the filter. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s00770320] tooling — huge SSE functions (15 KB, 0x7194 frame) are unreadable in card.py: esp offsets shift with every push/cdecl call, and Ghidra prints each splatted __m128 op four times. Two throwaway analysis scripts fixed it: (1) rewrite `[esp+N]` to a fixed frame base `[S+off]` by tracking push/pop/add esp/callee-pops (work/match/scratch_s00770320_norm.py), (2) lane-wise symbolic execution that prints every store as an expression over earlier stores (scratch_s00770320_sym.py). With those, the function split into 6 inline helpers (affine 4x4 inverse, affine Mul, Scale/Translate/Rotation-from-quat matrices, quat mul). A per-region `mulps` count vs the expected helper counts confirmed completeness better than chk.py's diff (14k bytes, meaningless at this size). Fix idea: promote both scripts into tools/matching (frame-normalized card + `sym` mode). Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [op1_big s008a9220] tooling — chk.py filters candidate symbols to within max(8, n/8) bytes of the original, so a complete-but-restructured big function (FontFusion SetupInput, 9176 B vs 10800 B because cl tail-merges duplicated grow/init blocks) gets "(no symbol of similar size)" and no completeness signal. Also Ghidra misread one realloc size field (hintmarkers_y realloc'd with hintmarkers_x_ml); per-site asm checks with rg on the field offsets were needed. Fix idea: chk.py flag to force a symbol/VA pairing and print the diff regardless of size, plus a per-callee call-count comparison. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s11 s0058e6d0] tooling — for a 12 KB function the chk.py diff count (9.6k of 12.2k bytes) says nothing about completeness, and cmpdis leaves my side's relocated calls as `call <+0x..>` / `push 0`, so missing calls or wrong `new` sizes don't stand out. What worked: diff the multiset of non-jump immediates (property IDs, operator new sizes, float constants) plus the ordered sequence of call kinds (direct vs vtable slot) between the two sides of cmpdis; that found 18 wrong stub sizeofs in one pass (work/match/scratch_s0058e6d0_imm.py). Fix idea: add a `chk.py --shape` mode that prints those two diffs. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s004c73f0] tooling — For huge /Od functions the chk.py byte count (~10k) says nothing about completeness, and cmpdis.py cuts the ORIGINAL to len(mine), so instruction/operand tallies over its output are silently wrong when my code is shorter (ebp-relative disp8 vs the original's esp+disp32). What worked: list call targets in order (original from card.py, mine from the obj's REL32 relocs) and diff them 1:1, then compare counts of member offsets/immediates over the FULL original size (scripts: work/match/scratch_s004c73f0_calls.py, _cnt.py). Also card.py's "operator_new[]" label on 0x11e073e is really memset.
  Fix idea: add a `--calls` mode to cmpdis (named call-sequence diff) and disassemble the original to its real symbol size. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s007bbf80/s0064acd0] codegen — (1) A 12-AutoRefCount /EHsc function (DilateStart) only gets the original's frameless prologue with `/arch:SSE /GS-`; `/arch:SSE2` adds `push ebp; and esp,-16` (looks like an alignment requirement) and `/GS` adds a cookie, so test `/arch:SSE` first. (2) An out-of-line `vector::DoInsertValue(pos, val)` called with `push &val; push pos; call` and NO `add esp` is a __thiscall member (ret 8) whose `this` is the still-live `lea ecx,[obj+0x24]`; declaring it as a member of the vector stub reproduces both the lea and the missing cleanup. (3) `push ecx` of a byte local after `mov cl,[m]` is a `bool` argument (callee reads the low byte only), not a dword. (4) A small helper `lea ecx,[eax+0x24]` before an inlined push_back means the vector object is `this` of a non-inlined member call on the slow path.
  Fix idea: add (2) and (1) to Batch-2 lessons; a card.py option that decodes `[esp+N]` slots relative to the frame base (S after all pushes) would save the push-depth arithmetic needed to map args to locals (~40 min here). Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00d63fd0] data — The frozen function list has 0x00d63fd0 as ONE 4733-byte function, but it is four packed functions (Tick at d63fd0, Tick at d64200, Tick at d64a90, 1-arg reset at d65150; jump tables sit between them) so Ghidra/card decompile only the first and chk/run_all can never match the span. Also, `push ecx; fstp [esp]` right after a float-returning vcall is the pending arg of an OUTER call (arg order right-to-left), not an arg of the next vcall; misreading it as same-slot-different-arity cost time. Fix idea: split functions at jump-table/ret+padding boundaries in the function list; document the push/fstp idiom in matching.md. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [op1_big s004dd060] codegen/tooling — In the /Od /Ob1 editor region, a plain `inline` helper (template or not) that makes a virtual call and returns `const T&` was NOT inlined, so chk.py printed only "no symbol of similar size" (the helper calls made the function far smaller than the target). The original's typed property getter (`FindProperty` vcall, `type==T || type==0x10` test, `GetValue()` or `&kDefault`) is a `__forceinline` with if/`return` (not `?:`, which flips the branch order and adds a slot). Also useful: cmpdis output normalized by masking `ebp - N` offsets and relocated zeros (work/match/scratch_s004dd060_nd.py) separates real structural diffs from /Od slot drift in an 8 KB function. Fix idea: chk.py could print the closest symbol and its size even when it falls outside the size window; add a normalized-diff mode to cmpdis. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s00b57c40] data — the "caller-scored" PDB candidate for 0x00b57c40 (rw::audio::core::Layer3Dec::Decode) was wrong, and the old partial.txt built a whole MP3-decoder story on it; the body is SP::cGonzagoPhysics::UpdateHavok. What found it: stack-local layouts (two 0xc18-byte fixed vectors of 12-byte elements -> `pdb_type.py` sp_fixed_vector<cGonzagoHavokObject,256>) plus a size/module search of work/devbuild/symbols.json (dev bodies are SecuROM-encrypted, but names/sizes/modules are usable). Also card.py's decompile hides arguments of vcalls/thiscalls (Cast ids, the 4th arg of FUN_00b421b0 pushed before another call).
  Fix idea: mark caller-scored candidates as low-confidence in card.py, and add a `devsym.py <substring> [--min-size N]` helper over work/devbuild/symbols.json. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s005d36e0/s005ec030] tooling — (1) macOS `sed -i` needs a suffix argument, so `sed -i 's/..//' file` silently treats the file as the suffix and fails; use python for scripted edits. (2) cmpdis.py needs the chk obj path and `ls -t work/match/chk/*.obj` picks other agents' objects, so glob `work/match/chk/<your scratch stem>_*.obj`. (3) When a card's push sequence looks one word short for a callee's `ret N`, re-read the raw lines: the card/sed split can hide a third `push 1` (BuildBlock 8 args). Codegen: a local hkVector4 needs `__declspec(align(16))` to reproduce `and esp,-16` + ebp frames, and `if (find(k).node != end) { ... return true; } return false;` (nested ifs) reproduces the shared-tail exits.
  Fix idea: chk.py could print the obj path; card.py could print per-call pushed-word counts versus the callee's `ret N`. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s0093e5f0] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" without saying how big the compiled symbol was. A 10 KB compile of an 11.7 KB target (with /GS on) looked like a failed build until I ran cmpobj by hand on the hashed obj in work/match/chk. Separately, cl /O2 stopped inlining plain `inline` helpers inside an already-huge function (inline budget), so FromString/DestroyString showed up as calls until I marked them __forceinline. Fix idea: have chk.py list the closest symbol and its size even when it is outside the size window. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s007a1740 s00825930] codegen — (1) A dtor with member subobjects gets an EH frame (push -1/fs:[0]) under /EHsc while the original has none; compile those modules without /EHsc (cPropertyUI dtor matched with `/O2 /MD /Gy /TP /arch:SSE /fp:fast`). (2) To get base-class vptr resets at the end of a derived dtor, give the base classes inline empty virtual dtors. (3) `long r=_InterlockedDecrement(&rc); if(r==0)` emits `lock xadd; dec; jne` like the original, while `if(_InterlockedDecrement(&rc)==0)` drops the dec. (4) `new(mem) T()` adds an EH frame; call the ctor via a thiscall stub (`((Mem*)p)->Construct()`) instead. (5) Two inlined push_back temps of a refcounted pair can force `push ebp; mov ebp,esp; and esp,-8`, unresolved. (6) Unused inline member functions are not emitted, so scratch tests need a caller. Fix idea: document in matching.md. Time lost: ~60.
- [s01060df0] codegen — An /O2 function with an `and esp,-16` aligned frame but no hkVector4 locals: the cause was `movss; maxss [mem]; minss [mem]; movss` clamps, i.e. an `_mm_store_ss(&v, _mm_min_ss(_mm_max_ss(_mm_load_ss(&v), _mm_load_ss(&lo)), _mm_load_ss(&hi)))` helper. Its __m128 temporaries force the aligned frame, and writing it in plain C++ loses the alignment and shifts every stack offset. The tiny `fld [ecx+N]; ret` tuning getters (c374xx) are out-of-line calls, not inlined fields. Fix idea: add "maxss/minss with memory operands = SSE-intrinsic clamp (aligned frame)" to the /O2 levers in matching.md. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op1_big s0071ac50] docs — the slice label ("SP::Simulator cCreatureAbility loader") was wrong: vtable 0x13ef094 is just RefCountTemplate's, shared by many classes. The real name (SP::cMeshBuilder::CreateMeshData) came from `rg -o "\?[A-Za-z_]+@cMeshBuilder@SP@@" work/devbuild/symbols.json`, which lists every dev-PDB method of a class; docs/matching.md doesn't mention this file. Also cmpdis prints my object's calls as `<+0xNNN>` (unresolved) while the original's are `<ext>`, so every call shows as a diff line.
  Fix idea: mention devbuild/symbols.json method listing in the playbook; mask own-object call relocs in cmpdis. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op1_big s24 s005af200] tooling — +1 to the cmpdis `--calls` idea (line 129-131): for a 7.5 KB switch function I verified completeness with a throwaway script (work/match/scratch_s005af200_calls.py) that lists named calls from my obj's relocations and maps original call targets via the `// 0x...` comments in my source; per-case windows of the two sequences then diffed cleanly even though cl laid the cases out in a different order. Also chk.py on this one TU took >120 s, so it needs run_in_background. Also: by-value AutoRefCount args got a caller-side Release until the class had a declared user copy ctor (same lever as the line-87 entry).
  Fix idea: ship the call-sequence script as `cmpdis.py --calls` and mention the by-value smart-pointer case in Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s00e956f0] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" when a big function compiles >1/8 smaller than the original (here: sin/cos/sqrt became CRT calls without /fp:fast), which reads like a compile/symbol problem. A per-callee call-count comparison (orig call targets vs. obj reloc symbol names) was the fastest completeness check for a 7 KB function. Fix idea: have chk.py fall back to the nearest-size symbol with its size delta, and add a `--calls` mode printing per-callee call counts orig vs mine. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s00b9d820] tooling — on an 8.6 KB function cl /O2 silently stopped inlining small helpers (push_back, RefPtr dtor, SimSingleton::Get...), the object came out 7.4 KB and chk.py printed only "no symbol of similar size" (its 1/8 size window), which looks like a missing symbol. `__forceinline` on the helpers restored inlining. Also: a mid-function `push ebp` is MSVC's late callee-save, not an argument; every [esp+N] after it is shifted by 4.
  Fix idea: chk.py should print the nearest-size symbol and its size when nothing is in the window. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s0101d130] data — the old partial.txt called 0x0101d130 "/Od, slot order not reproducible", but it is /O2 /arch:SSE (ebx=this, and esp,-16 for one aligned local); the note sent the earlier attempt down the wrong path. Also Ghidra/card attribute pushes made before a no-arg global getter (`SP::PlanetModel()` = `mov eax,[g]; ret`, `cTimeOfDay::Instance`, `FUN_00b3d280`) to that getter: they are the args of the thiscall right after it, and the getter can even sit mid-argument-list (`this->F(..., PlanetModel()->GetRadius(), ...)`). Fix idea: card.py could tag 0-arg getters (body `mov eax,[imm]; ret`) so their apparent args are attributed to the next call. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s00d0e170] tooling — for a 7 KB function the useful completeness check was not chk.py's byte count but a callee-sequence diff: disassemble my obj with relocation names (capstone + COFF reloc table), map original call targets to my declarations via `// 0x00xxxxxx` comments, and difflib the two call lists; then diff the multisets of immediates and `[reg+disp]` displacements. This found a missing AutoRefCount copy-assign (plain store instead of AddRef/Release) and wrong `new` sizes from unpadded stub classes, which the byte count hid. Script: work/match/scratch_s00d0e170_calls.py + _cmpcalls.py. Also: `cmpobj.py <obj> Activate` picks the first symbol containing the substring (not the function), so pass the full mangled name.
  Fix idea: ship a tools/matching/callseq.py (orig vs obj callee/immediate diff) and recommend SIZE_CHECK/offsetof typedef asserts for stub classes allocated with `new`. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s00d3d870] codegen — huge property-load chains: cl /O2 inlines the Property::GetValueX accessors only in the MIDDLE of a ~130-call chain (first/last calls go out of line to the /Od COMDAT), and the boundary moves with tiny accessor-cost changes (routing through a GetValuePtr()/GetType() inline shifts it by a few calls, non-additively). Also cmpdis prints relocated operands as 0, so diff byte counts stay high even when the shape matches; normalize relocs before reading the diff. Fix idea: document the inline-budget lever, and give cmpdis a reloc-normalized/structure-only mode. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00f8fe60] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" with no diff count when the compiled function is >~20% smaller than the original (here 2221/5296 vs 6759 B), so on huge functions it gives no completeness signal until late; had to call cmpobj.py on the obj by mangled name. Fix idea: chk.py should fall back to the target's own mangled name (or the largest symbol) and still print the diff. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op1_big s00531510] tooling — chk.py prints only "(no symbol of similar size)" when the compiled function is >1/8 off the target size, without saying how big it is, so finding a missing block meant hand-parsing the .obj (chk.parse_coff) and listing call relocations with /opt/homebrew/opt/llvm/bin/llvm-objdump -dr.
  Fix idea: print the nearest symbol and its size anyway, plus an optional call-sequence diff (original call targets vs reloc names). Time lost: ~10.
- [s0085f1e0] docs — a 6 KB straight-line SSE function in the op1_big list was IJG jfdctflt.c (jpeg_fdct_float): cl /O2 fully unrolls its two 8-iteration loops, so "huge, no branches, magic float constants" can be stock library source that matches verbatim (/O2 /arch:SSE /fp:fast). Fix idea: in the op1_big prompt, say to check float constants (0.70710677, 0.38268343, ...) and known libs (libjpeg, zlib, Havok) before reconstructing by hand. Time lost: ~0 (would have been hours).
- [s006a6340 op1_big] tooling/codegen — for a 6.9 KB function chk.py printed only "(no symbol of similar size)" (size filter n0/8) while my build was 5.0 KB, so it gave no signal at all; the cause was cl /O2 refusing to inline small `inline` helpers (a throwing CheckArgCount, Variant/vector dtors, a SetArray template) inside a huge function. A per-case `throw` must be a macro (each case owns its own cError temp) and other helpers need `__forceinline`; counting call relocations per target in the obj (objdump -dr on the function's section) is a fast completeness check against the card's callee list.
  Fix idea: chk.py should always print the size of the symbol whose `// @ 0x<va>` comment or name matches, and a `--calls` mode comparing callee counts. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s011443b0] tooling/docs — the partial source left by an earlier pass mislabelled both branches of a 6.5 KB function, but it is just the ISO dist10 MP3 III_stereo() (tables sfBandIndex/is_ratio/LSF k, is_pos=7 fill, 1/sqrt2 M/S); recognising the reference code made the rewrite ~1 h instead of a lane-by-lane transcription. Also chk.py reports "(no symbol of similar size)" for any rewrite whose size is >15% off, so the only diff signal is cmpobj with the mangled name (and cmpobj's header line is lost if you `tail` its output).
  Fix idea: card.py could grep its decompile for well-known constants (0.70710677, 576/0x240, 0x12 x 0x20 grids) and hint "MP3 layer III"; chk.py --sym <substr> to force the compared symbol. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s004f3de0] tooling — chk.py printed only "(no symbol of similar size)" when cl /Od /Ob1 declined to inline ~50 eastl::bitset test/set calls in a 6 KB function (it hit the inline budget; `__forceinline` on test/set/reset fixed it), and cmpdis.py diffs of big /Od functions are mostly noise from shifted jump targets. Fix idea: chk.py should list the nearest symbols with sizes when none is in range; cmpdis.py should normalize branch targets (work/match/scratch_s004f3de0_nd.py does this). Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s00d83a60/s00cb1270] data/tooling — slice VA 00d83a60 is a Ghidra "4585-byte function" that is really nine separate cdecl handlers (0xd83a60..0xd84c49; its jump tables sit between them), and 00d83f10's own 3385-byte card overlaps it; only 00d83a60 is listed, so the other eight have no VA in any batch. chk.py shows "(no symbol of similar size)" for all of them, so per-function checking needs cmpobj.py/cmpdis.py with the mangled name (cmpobj compares our symbol's own length, so each real function can still MATCH; I added the byte-exact 00d84520 to the manifest as an extra VA). Also: a switch whose dead cases (`case 2: case 3: break;`) fall to default loses the jump table; `case 2: return false; case 3: return false;` keeps it. An address-taken by-value param (`&c` passed on) is reloaded at every use unless the code copies `Creature* c = h.p` from a by-value struct param inside each case; and `x->mpBase->Method()` with `struct BTree : Pad8, Behaviors` gives the late `add ecx,8` without the null check a cast adds.
      Fix idea: slice_info.py/card.py should flag overlapping/lumped Ghidra sizes and list the real sub-function starts (after int3 padding / jump tables). Time lost: ~60 minutes (4612-byte float/x87 sweep function SweepTick also cost ~90 minutes to write from asm; the Ghidra decompile of it is stack-split and unusable).
- [s00589ce0/s00586b00] codegen — (1) an eastl::basic_string local is 16 bytes (3 pointers + allocator word): a 12-byte stub string gives frame 0x40 instead of 0x4c. (2) `p = new(...)` then `if (p) p = p->Init()` differs from `void* raw = new(...); T* p = raw ? ((T*)raw)->Init() : 0;` (the latter matches the original's separate null path); a refcount release via a sub-object needs `_ReadWriteBarrier()` around `cnt = 1` and the vptr load to match store/load order. (3) a switch on a mode int reproduces the `sub eax,0/1` compare chain, an if-chain does not. Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s004cf770] codegen — /Od EASTL inline wrappers: a `const bool bHasTrivialMove` that the original stores BEFORE the loop's param copies belongs to an OUTER inline wrapper (uninitialized_move_commit -> impl::do_move_commit), not the loop function; merging them flips store order and slot order. Holes between slots are `uint32_t unusedFrame[N]` / ScratchSlots<N> placed in the wrapper (named local = hole above, ScratchSlots = at bottom). Also `chk.py` hides a function whose size is far off ("no symbol of similar size") so a missing hole looked like a missing symbol; I used cmpdis on the newest work/match/chk obj plus a tiny script that prints the union of `[ebp-N]` slots on each side.
  Fix idea: chk.py `--sym <substr>` and a slot-union printer in cmpdis.py. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s010f3460] codegen — Havok 3.1 (older cl) x87 code loads 0.0f/1.0f from the constant pool (`fld [pool]; fucompp`, `fdiv [mem]`) where cl 15 emits fldz/fld1; `extern const float kZero,kOne` reproduces the shape but scheduling still differs, so these hk functions end up nonmatching. Also: a hkVector4 with `__declspec(align(16))` explains both the `and esp,-16` frame and the 4 skipped bytes before hkContactPoint in struct copies (no explicit pad needed), and `/GS-` is needed when a function has a stack array but the original has no cookie.
  Fix idea: note in docs/matching.md that Havok FP-heavy functions are older-compiler output and should be written complete first. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [op1_big s008220e0] tooling — cmpdis.py truncates the original to len(mine) bytes, so when my function is shorter the original's tail (here ~90 bytes of epilogue/last branch) silently drops out of the diff, and the diff can't show a missing final block. Counting calls by relocation target (scratch script work/match/scratch_s008220e0_calls.py) was the reliable completeness check for a 6.4 KB function. Fix idea: cmpdis should take the original's length from the symbol table/card size, and chk.py could print a per-callee call-count diff. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s007dd880/s007de7d0] codegen — three EASTL-ish shapes decoded for the SmoothCameraController: (1) `vector<float>(n)` zero fill = `for(i<n) p[i]=0.0f` gives `rep stosd` for runtime n, but constant n needs inline `memset(p,0,n*4)` (GPR `xor ecx,ecx` stores, right frame size), so two ctor spellings are needed; an int-typed fill loop never produced rep stosd. (2) A tLerpScalar-style struct zeroed with `memset(this,0,16)` (intrinsic) gives the `xor eax,eax; mov [m],eax` x4 pattern and matched the 582-byte ctor (float or int member assignments use movss/ebx). (3) Making the vector's dtor-free helper `__declspec(nothrow)`/`throw()` removes the per-dtor EH state stores. Also: a stack local initialised to a constant at entry (`mov [esp+x],5`) that later blocks read without writing is a function-level `int n = 5;` that one block overwrites (clip/pitch blocks reuse the zoom-property count), which Ghidra's decompile hid completely; and retail eastl vectors are 5 words (0x14) on both stack and heap, unlike the 4-word PDB layout.
      Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [op1_big s00591fa0] docs — the slice skeleton header listed module flags without /GS-, but this editor HandleMessage (a 0x200-byte wchar_t local, no cookie) needs /GS-; with the cookie cl also merges every return into one epilogue, which hides how close the structure is. A mnemonic-only SequenceMatcher diff (work/match/scratch_s00591fa0_mn.py) was far more useful than cmpdis/byte counts for a 6 KB function: it reports only missing/extra blocks. Fix idea: add a `--structure` mode to cmpdis.py that prints only insert/delete runs of >=4 mnemonics. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op1_big s009eab60] tooling — chk.py printed "(no symbol of similar size)" for a complete 4.6 KB first draft of a 6.1 KB function (the original unrolls a loop 4x), which looks like a compile failure; I had to run cl.sh by hand and cmpobj with the mangled name to get a diff. Fix idea: when no symbol is within the size window, still report the nearest-size or same-name symbol with its diff. Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [s005002d0] tooling — od_names.py fit prints only ~300 random K-name combos, which never contain the wanted slot permutation for K>=5 (K!=5040 at K=7); a variant that builds up to 60,000 `Fit<N>()` functions in one TU (cl's 65k-section /bigobj limit) and keeps only combos whose slot order equals a given declaration-index permutation (hi->lo) finds names in ~5 s even for K=8. With it, three /Od functions (ExportSubmesh 1481 B, ExportMeshAdd 778 B, ExportMeshBegin) went byte-exact in a few rounds; copy at work/match/scratch_s005002d0_fit.py (usage: fit.py K "perm" M seed).
  Fix idea: add `od_names.py fit K --perm 6,5,1,... --m 60000` to the tool. Also: `1.0f - x` vs `1.0 - x` differ at /Od (`fld1; fsub dword [m]` vs `fld [m]; fld1; fsubrp`; the original had the double constant), and a POD struct returned by value from a callee costs an extra temp-to-temp copy while a struct with a user default ctor (`rwVector3(){}`) is returned in place. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s005002d0] codegen — ExportSkeleton (/Od, 1456 B) stayed at 661 diff bytes: the original puts the empty-struct arg temp (`mov byte [ebp-0x115],al` for `string8(CtorSprintf(),...)`) and the sret temps of MatrixInverse/Vector3_Negate inside the sorted region of the loop-body locals, while cl puts them below it for every layout tried (named for-init var vs outer declaration, inline wrappers, rw*-returning POD vs user-ctor structs). Fix idea: if someone learns how hidden temps get their hash name (maybe a creation counter), document it. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [op1_big s00d67d30] tooling — for huge functions there is no quick completeness check: cmpdis prints a 3-line-context unified diff, so call counts can't be read off it, and our obj's REL32 calls show as `<+off>`, not names. I wrote work/match/scratch_s00d67d30_calls.py (named direct calls from obj relocs) and scratch_s00d67d30_vc2.py (vtable-slot histogram, orig vs obj); equal counts were a strong "no missing block" signal. Fix idea: a `chk.py --calls` mode printing per-callee/per-vslot counts for both sides. Time lost: ~10.
- [op1_big s00cd1be0] tooling — cmpdis.py disassembles the original only up to len(mine)+64 bytes, so when our function is shorter (6106 vs 6226 here) the tail of the original is silently dropped and instruction counts from its output are wrong; also chk/cmpdis give no per-callee call-count comparison, which is the quickest completeness check for huge functions (wrote work/match/scratch_s00cd1be0_calls.py for it). Fix idea: have cmpdis take the original length from the function table, and add a `--calls` mode listing callee/immediate multisets orig vs mine. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s00cf5540] tooling — For a 6 KB function, chk.py's byte diff (4362) is dominated by frame-size/offset shifts and says nothing about completeness. A per-callee call-count histogram of original vs compiled obj (resolve obj relocations to names, map names to VAs via the `// 00xxxxxx` comments in the source) pinpointed every missing/merged call in one run (work/match/scratch_s00cf5540_calls.py). Also: Ghidra types an IHandler-subobject `this` as the full class, so its field names are off by the subobject offset (0x3c here); ignore them. Fix idea: add a `--calls` mode to chk.py that prints the callee-count delta. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s010bd5e0] codegen — Havok solver x87 code (hkExportImpulsesAndRhs, 6264 B) keeps hkVector4 temp components partly in x87 registers and partly in 16-aligned stack slots (`fstp st(2); fstp st(0)` dance, no float rounding of the dot product); cl 15 gives all-memory temps with /fp:precise (7320 B), reassociated register code with /fp:fast (4820 B), and 4564 B for scalar expressions, so no source shape lands within chk.py's 1/8 size window. This is probably the prebuilt-Havok toolset, so don't spend rounds on it. +1 to the chk.py size-window entries (used cmpobj.py with the mangled name). Fix idea: note in matching.md that Havok solver code is nonmatching by default. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s008c0760] codegen — Ghidra drops register-convention args (scalePoints takes eax=scale struct, edi=count plus 2 stack args) and shows a variadic-looking callee (0x8cefe0 called with 5 args on one path, 2 on the other); reuse of one decompile local for pointer/flags/scalars needs splitting by hand. Fix idea: card.py should list usercall register inputs (eax/edi/ecx set before call without being args). Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s0100b890] data — Ghidra's decompile attaches pushed args to the wrong call when a thiscall's args are pushed BEFORE a cdecl singleton getter supplies its `this` (`push "CVG2SPG"; call 0xb3d4d0; mov ecx,eax; call 0xad7db0` printed as `FUN_00b3d4d0("CVG2SPG")`; `push 0; call [vtbl+0x4c]; push eax; call GetUFOSimulator; call 0x1001700` printed with the 0 on the vcall). Rule: an arg push not cleaned by `add esp` after a getter belongs to the next `mov ecx,eax; call`. Also, in big /O2 functions cl runs out of inline budget, so the same AutoRefCount operator=/ctor/AsPPTypeParam appears both inlined and as out-of-line calls (ICF-named e.g. IWinText::operator=, GetImageResource); write them all as the one inline helper.
  Fix idea: card.py could annotate each call with the stack-arg count derived from the callee's `ret N` / caller's `add esp`. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s008fa050] codegen|tooling — (1) A 16-byte member-function pointer ({fn,0,0,0} built on the stack) is MSVC's unknown-inheritance form; declaring the PMF typedef or a function taking it while the class is incomplete is NOT enough (stays 4 bytes), but a class member or variable of that PMF type declared before the class definition locks it to 16. (2) chk.py's diff count for a 6 KB function was 3543 though only 5 two-byte register choices differed: every later rel8/rel32 jump shifts. A relocation- and shift-normalising diff (work/match/scratch_s008fa050_rd.py over cmpdis output) showed the true picture.
      Fix idea: add a "normalised instruction diff" count to chk.py/cmpdis.py; note the PMF trick in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s0044f7c0] data/codegen — symbols/slices/s0044f7c0.txt named 0x0044f7c0 "BuildBlock", but the PDB anchor BuildBlock is 0x00441440 (which this function calls), so the old name was a copy-paste of the callee's. Also the /Od EASTL bitset<N>::test spill (`mov [tmp],word` before the mask is built) is reproduced only with a named `const uint32_t word = mWord[i/32];` local in the inline helper. Swapping the operand order does not reproduce it. Diffing with ebp slot offsets and call targets masked (work/match/scratch_s0044f7c0_nd.sh) shows a 6 KB /Od function's structural diff (150 lines) under chk.py's 4677 raw diff bytes.
  Fix idea: give cmpdis a --mask-slots mode; check slice symbol names against pdb_names.json anchors. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s005b1e30/s00650630] codegen — (1) a float constant fed to cos/sin that the original loads as `fld dword` from .data (not .rdata) is a global float variable, not a literal: `cos(0.0872f)` gives `fld qword`, `extern float g; cos(g)` gives `fld dword`. (2) `QW r; r = f(); return r;` with a user element-wise `operator=` reproduces the sret-passthrough wrapper that copies the callee result with fld/fstp; `return f();` gives NRVO. (3) A by-value AutoRefCount param needs a user copy-assign (or `mX = p.mpObject`): the implicit one just copies the pointer with no AddRef. (4) chk.py's byte-diff count is a poor progress metric on /O2 SSE functions (it jumps 100+ on harmless reorders); an instruction-level diff count would help.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to Batch-2 lessons; have chk.py print an instruction-diff count. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00712e10/s005a5ec0] codegen — cl 15 mis-resolves `EA::AutoRefCount<T>*` (error "AutoRefCount * : unknown size", "cannot convert to AutoRefCount<T>*") when the template is first named inside a struct while T is still only forward-declared; raw `T**` element pointers (vector layout is identical) avoid it.
