Also: a ctor that reloads the shared prime_rehash_policy floats from memory means they were declared extern; in the original they are literal-pool constants (movss from pool, 1.0f/2.0f) and the empty bucket array is `extern void* arr[2]` used as an immediate address. macOS `sed -i` needs a suffix arg, so use the Edit tool.
      Fix idea: add an "incomplete-T AutoRefCount" note to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s005e3280/s006a9470] codegen — (1) /fp:fast is needed on top of /arch:SSE for float members compared/loaded as comiss/ucomiss (without it cl emits fld/fucompi); (2) cl canonicalises `dt + member` to `member + dt`, so the original's `movss xmm0,[dt]; addss xmm0,[member]` operand order could not be forced by any spelling (temp copy, +=, const float&); (3) a dead third `eastl::string` temp whose dtor folded away (begin/end/cap into 0x1667bac) still keeps its 3 stores and a frame slot in the original, but cl drops the stores for a plain local; (4) retail cPropertyManager layout differs from the PDB (Factory subobject at +4, embedded property list at +0x3c, name table at +0x148), so Factory::WriteResource is a member of the +4 subobject (`this-4` is the manager) and takes 4 stack args (ret 0x10).
      Fix idea: card.py could print `ret N` arg counts next to the PDB signature so mismatched PDB prototypes are obvious. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00908c00] docs — 0x00900000-0x0090ffff contains expat 2.0.1 (XML_NS, XML_DTD, XML_CONTEXT_BYTES=1024; XML_ParserStruct offsets match exactly), built with LTCG: static helpers use custom register conventions (processXmlDecl via ecx/edx/eax) and partly inline, so byte-exact is out of reach but the real lib/xmlparse.c (libexpat tag R_2_0_1) gives complete source in minutes instead of hours of asm reading. Fix idea: note third-party libs (expat, Havok 3.1 agents sharing hkPredGskAgent3 skeleton) and their versions in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op1_big s00c2b170/s00ce6af0] tooling — for big /O2 functions the chk.py diff count can't show whether the function is complete. Two quick checks proved it was: (1) the ordered list of E8 call targets, original vs the obj's COFF relocation symbol names (work/match/scratch_s00c2b170_calls.py), and (2) the ordered list of pushed property-hash immediates (work/match/scratch_s00ce6af0_seq.py). Both matched exactly. Also: without /fp:fast an /arch:SSE module's float math comes out as x87 (0 mulss vs 56), so check the mulss/comiss counts before blaming the source. Previous partials for both slices were placeholders ("~200 rules omitted", "represented structurally") and had to be rewritten from scratch.
  Fix idea: put the call/hash sequence diff into chk.py as a flag (e.g. --seq). Time lost: ~20.
- [op1_big s00588570/s009c32c0] tooling — for huge functions the byte diff says nothing about completeness, and cmpdis masks call targets as `<+0x..>`/`<ext>`, so missing or duplicated calls are invisible. A 40-line script (work/match/scratch_s00588570_calls.py: walks the obj's COFF relocations at each E8 call and counts the target symbol names) compared against the card's callee list showed at once which helpers cl had not inlined (a paint-mode helper, HandlePtr::reset, the quaternion helper) and confirmed every EASTL call count matched.
      Fix idea: add `chk.py --calls` that prints the per-symbol call counts of mine next to the original's per-address callee counts. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s00b21e60/s007391f0] codegen — three re-found tricks. (1) A function returning a struct by value (pair<iterator,bool>, iterator) takes the sret pointer as FIRST stack arg: declaring `ClsIter __thiscall InsertValue(...)` matched an rbtree DoInsertValue whose "out" pointer was written after `++size`, while an explicit out-param version never did. (2) An inlined rbtree/vector dtor is `~Tree(){ Nuke(anchor.parent); }`: call `tree.~Tree()` explicitly and cl loads the root before `lea ecx`; calling `tree.Nuke(root)` gives the wrong push order. (3) `if (a == p) a = 0;` on an AutoRefCount needs both `operator==(T*)` and `operator=(T*)` inline (cmp [mem] then reload) to match. Also: an unused inline function defined inside a class is never emitted, so scratch variants must be defined out of class, and `rg -h` prints help (use --no-filename).
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s01164200/s00f6c940] codegen — huge "4x repeated block" functions were cl's own loop unrolling of tiny per-cell loops (`((n-4)>>2)+1` trip count, remainder loop sometimes split into rep stosd + float loop); prior partials hand-factored them as "inlined ~40x". cl only unrolls when the index is bumped inside the call arg (`SetCell(m, idx++, ...)`), not with `for (...; ++j, ++idx)`, and static inline helpers get outlined at /O2 unless __forceinline. Also chk.py hides >12% size misses, so measure the obj symbol size directly.
  Fix idea: add an "unrolled loop shape" bullet to docs/matching.md /O2 levers. Time lost: ~15 min.
- [s00d43e30/s00577e10] data — Ghidra's decompile of big /O2 functions misattributes args pushed BEFORE a no-arg global getter call (e.g. `push 1; push id; call AchievementsController; mov ecx,eax; call AutoTest`) to the getter, and swapped the switch field of ContinueLoading (+0xa0 mState vs +0xa4 mPreviousMode, so the existing partial skeleton was wrong). Also chk.py's `--flags` without /fp:fast silently emits `__CIsin/__CIsqrt` calls where the original has inline fsin/fsqrt; grep the obj's relocations (objdump -dr) for `_CI` to pick /fp:fast.
  Fix idea: card.py could annotate "args consumed by next thiscall" when a call has no `add esp` and the following call uses its eax as ecx. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s01330060] codegen — a dynamic initializer that builds each element of a global POD array (Simulator::Attribute tables, `$E<n>`) in a stack temp and `rep movsd`-copies it cannot be reproduced with an inline ctor: cl 15 /O2 folds the whole `T arr[] = { T(...), ... }` to static .data and leaves an empty initializer (factory-by-value variant folds too). Probably an out-of-line ctor inlined by LTCG. Recorded as nonmatching with the full table. Fix idea: note in docs/matching.md that attribute/property tables are data-complete but not byte-matchable. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s007ada40/s0049fbd0] codegen — (1) a user-declared dtor adds derived vptr stores and a /GS cookie; for a dtor that stores only base vptrs and uses the old non-cookie SEH, leave the dtor implicit, call `p->~T()` from a helper under `#pragma auto_inline(off)`, give the bases `virtual ~B(){}`, and use manifest flags `/GS-` (the default flags add the cookie, so a dtor with fs:[0] SEH never matches without it). (2) chk.py's "best symbol" column mislabels functions when sizes are close, and its default-flag output hides that /GS- is needed. (3) usercall DXT helpers (eax/esi/edi args) and MMX leaf functions cannot match: cl wraps __m64 intrinsics in an ebp frame with `and esp,-8`.
  Fix idea: let chk.py print the mangled symbol per VA; document /GS- for dtors with fs:[0] SEH. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s00aabc20/s0116c750] data — Both "largest" functions were mislabeled, and one is two functions. 0x00aabc20 (11424 B) is two packed cdecl streamers: Stream_Quad_V4F_N4F_C4B_T2F at aabc20 and Stream_Quad_V4F_N4F_C4B_T4B at aad240 (the second prologue sits at aad240). chk.py then reports "no symbol of similar size", so diffs come from cmpobj per function. An earlier agent had read the "uninitialized particle cursor" [esp+0x54] as a Ghidra bug, but it is [esp+0x60]=effect->mParticles before `add esp,0xc`. 0x0116c750 is not a terrain brush: it is ISO dist10 MPEG Layer III III_stereo with the LSF extension (576 lines, sfBandIndex/tan/io^n tables), and 01164200 is its MPEG-1 twin. The dev build's symbols.json (module + size) plus disassembling work/devbuild/SporeBin/SporeApp.exe named both at once. Fix idea: split packed spans in the function list (prologue after ret+int3), and tell big-function agents to first look up the module neighbours in work/devbuild/symbols.json and disassemble the dev twin. Time lost: ~30 min.
- [op1_big s00503c50/s00b24740] docs — two "huge" functions (5.4 KB each) were byte-exact on the first compile once recognised: 0x503c50 is the textbook Mahovsky-Wyvill Pluecker ray/AABB test (8 sign cases, block-scoped xa..zb), 0x00b24740 is 109x Simulator::RegisterNounType + NounCreateMap[noun]=Create (ModAPI NounClassFactories.h). A ~30-line script folding the card asm into symbolic expressions (slots -> names, [ebp+reg+off] with constant reg -> o[i]/d[i]) made the whole tree readable in 170 lines; the earlier "skeleton only" partial was a wasted pass.
  Fix idea: give card.py an --expr mode that folds movss/subss/mulss/comiss chains into expressions, and tell big-function agents to first look for a repeated/table shape or a known algorithm. Time lost: ~0 (saved).
- [s0069a9a0] codegen — with `/arch:SSE` alone the whole TU compiles to x87 (fld/fdivr); the originals' SSE movss/divss code only appears with `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` (sqrt/fmod still go through x87 there). Cost ~20 min of size mismatches. Also: a `__forceinline` helper containing `goto` is not inlined (use a macro), and a mixed x87/SSE function that keeps a Vector3 in stack memory with integer `mov` copies means struct-by-value temps (return Vector3 from an inline Normalized()).
  Fix idea: note in Batch-2 lessons that `/arch:SSE` modules need `/fp:fast` to emit SSE. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s00e07e70,s010e9ce0] tooling — cmpdis shows calls in our object as `call <+0x..>` (unresolved REL32 to an external) while the original shows `call <ext>`, so every call line counts as a diff and diff counts on call-heavy functions (~250 calls) are dominated by noise; also card.py names are misleading for some callees (0x00bfc5f0 printed as cGameData::SetGameDataOwner2, used as GetPlayerTribe). Separately, `tools/progress.py --groups` now crashes in runpy'd status.py with FileNotFoundError when a concurrent workflow deletes a slice's partial.txt between glob and open (the user-reported KeyError 'id' is already fixed in progress.py). Fix idea: normalize both sides' external calls to `call <ext>` in cmpdis; wrap status.py's per-file open in try/except OSError. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s00b318d0] tooling — chk.py pairs each VA with the best-scoring symbol of similar size, so small same-size functions in one object get cross-paired (RegisterDefaultPauseGates shown for four different VAs) and the real per-symbol result is hidden; also `export FLAGS` does not persist between Bash calls. Fix idea: let chk.py accept `va=symbol` pairs, or print `-a` per-symbol table by default. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s00b318d0] codegen — original module has no /EHsc (no fs:[0] frames even with member dtors) and `(__int64)float` compiles to `_ftol2` here but inline `fistp qword` there; flags /O2 /MD /Gy /TP /arch:SSE /GS- (no /fp:fast: it reorders fmul chains) matched 8 of 22. Fix idea: note in matching.md that missing EH frames mean dropping /EHsc, and record which flag gives inline fistp. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00c2d220] codegen — a 4.4KB steering function calls helpers that return a float in xmm0 (FUN_00c29d80 ends `movss xmm0,[esp+0xc]; ret`), a convention MSVC cannot express, and ecx-set calls cleaned by the caller (`add esp,8`) imply variadic thiscall members; for huge functions cmpdis diffs are dominated by stack-slot shifts so byte-exact is unrealistic. Fix idea: card.py could flag callees returning in xmm0 / caller-cleaned thiscalls. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op1_big s00cfbc10] data — In /O2 code cl reuses the stack slots of already-pushed args after a cdecl call returns: `push eax; push ecx; call 4a9b40; fldz; fstp [esp+4]; ...; add esp,4; push edx; push eax; call 472b80; add esp,0xc` means the leftover dword (now 0.0f) is the THIRD arg of the second call, not part of the first. Count pushes/pops per call (`add esp,N` vs callee `ret N`) before trusting Ghidra's arg lists; the same balance check showed that a vcall slot (+0xac) taking 0 args explains 1-, 2- and 4-push sites. My calls-histogram script (work/match/scratch_s00cfbc10_map.py, maps obj call symbols to VAs via `// 0x...` comments) confirmed 326/326 calls. Fix idea: card.py could print, per call, the pushes since the previous call and the callee's `ret N`. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s007efd10/s00d35190] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" both when the compile emits nothing useful and when the candidate is >12.5% off in size (e.g. a big inline helper cl declined to inline), and -a lists nothing in that case, so it looks like a broken compile. Fix idea: when no candidate passes the size filter, print the nearest-size symbol with its size. Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [s00b148c0] data — Ghidra's field names for cTerrainCameraController (PDB layout) are misleading at retail offsets >0x2f0 (e.g. mLocalInputState/kKeyboard* are really keyboard-ramp floats at 0x364-0x374); and call args that look like Ghidra "extra args" can be pushed before a vcall whose result is `this` of the next call (cDistGrid vf+0xc -> GetHeightAt(pos)). Fix idea: card.py could print callee arg counts from `ret N` so push-before-vcall patterns are obvious. Time lost: ~15 min.
- [s005d27e0/s00579e20] codegen — two levers cost most of the time: (1) an EASTL map helper that sits inline in the original (clear(), ~map(), find()) must be a `__forceinline` member of a small stub struct that owns the anchor fields (clear() and an explicit `~VMap()` call matched 31b0 and 35c0 exactly; the same code inlined by hand in the caller gave 285/214 diff bytes), and (2) a struct returned by value (`Bits128 GetMask()`) gives sret, so cl may hoist loads above the stores through the sret pointer; with `Bits128* out` it cannot, which is why 0057aaa0 only matched once GetMask/GetDefaultMask returned by value. Also: a function's own address-taken parameter (`&key` passed to map::operator[]) lives in the arg slot, so use the param directly instead of a local copy.
  Fix idea: add both to docs/matching.md EASTL/struct-return notes. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [op1_big s010a7720/s010e8860] tooling — `tools/progress.py --groups` crashed once in tools/status.py:26 with FileNotFoundError on match/slices/s00503c50/partial.txt: a concurrent agent deleted that partial.txt between the glob and the open(). It passed on rerun. The earlier `_rec["id"]` KeyError in progress.py was already guarded when I looked, and every work/batches/*.json record now has "id", but chk.py's slice_vas() still indexes `s["id"]` without a guard. Also: s010e8860 (castRayWithCollector) is the same inlined castRayInternal as s010e9ce0 (castSphere), so copying that helper made it complete in minutes. Fix idea: wrap the status.py open() in try/except OSError and use s.get("id") in chk.py. Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [op1_big s00f4d9c0/s01112950] tooling+codegen — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" when our function is >1/8 off in size, which hides compile results for large partial functions (first compile of a 5 KB function was 4.4 KB because /GS cookies and missing /arch:SSE). Also: under /fp:precise every sqrtf/sqrt (even of a double expression) becomes a __CIsqrt call; the inline x87 fsqrt in Havok/terrain code needs /fp:fast. Fix idea: have chk.py always report the closest-size symbol with its byte size, and add the sqrt/fp:fast note to matching.md. Time lost: ~15.
- [op1_big s00fb50d0] tooling — unnamed retail functions in SP code can often be named by their callee set: disassembling dev-build functions (work/devbuild/SporeBin/SporeApp.exe + work/devbuild/symbols.json, same module) and filtering by distinctive callees (atexit + cTerrainMap::GetVector4) found BuildRibbonVerts in seconds, even though retail rewrote its body. Also chk.py has no "keep the obj" flag, so cmpdis needs a hand-rolled cl.sh call. Fix idea: a tools/matching/devcallees.py "<retail va>" that ranks dev functions by shared named callees, and chk.py --keep-obj. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op1_big s00e11ab0] data — symbols/pdb_names.json "how": "anchor" names can be wrong outright: 0x00e11ab0 is labelled vector<slot_vector_entry<cGroupMovement>>::DoInsertValue but is cSPUIMinimapWin::HandleMessage (a message switch calling RemoveIcon/AddIcon on this-0x214). Check the body against the name before recovering types for it.
  Fix idea: have card.py flag anchor names whose callees/neighbours belong to a different class. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s006f0890/s00a2e650] codegen — (1) a fixed_hash_map ctor's allocator is a *temporary* built in the base-init expression (`Base(nb,...,FixedAlloc(buf,size,buckets))` with an inline ctor and const& param) and gives the right locals and EH state; a by-value helper return adds copies. (2) `it = m->erase(it)` vs `m->erase(it)` changes whether the sret slot is shared with the find() result; hashtable_iterator needs a user copy ctor for the `sub esp,8; mov [eax],..` by-value pattern. (3) cl hoists `ebp/edi` pushes differently when a fixup `flags |= 0xff` precedes arg loads; not solved. Fix idea: add (1)-(2) to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [cl1_part s42-43 s0056b680/s005bccc0] codegen — (1) At /Od, named locals of one function get slots in one hash order regardless of scope depth, but inline-callee params and reference temps (e.g. `wfind(first,last,const char& value)` called with `'|'`) are allocated below all named locals; a missing 2-4 byte hole between them is a `ScratchSlots<N>()` at the call site (partcount Describe matched with `<1>` before the PListRef local, `<1>` before the Format call and `<25>` before the by-value arg call). (2) A `char` ref temp landed at -0x59 where the original has -0x5a (2-byte near-miss in two functions); unused named locals are dropped, so they do not shift it. (3) `RefCountVTemplate::Release` that matched is `__forceinline` with `virtual ~RefCountV(){}` and `delete this;` (a virtual `Destroy(int)` call schedules the `mov [cnt],1` after the vtable load). (4) Hill-climbing on diff counts fails when frame offsets are globally shifted; fix the frame size/holes first, then names. (5) python `str.replace` of a variable name inside a function must skip `.name`/`->name` member accesses (use `(?<![.>\w])name\b`). Fix idea: od_names.py could print the per-N bucket order instead of one global rank. Time lost: ~60 min.
- [s006470b0/s0045f5e0] tooling — cmpdis.py needs the chk.py object, but `ls -t work/match/chk/*.obj | head -1` picks another concurrent agent's newest object; use `ls -t work/match/chk/<scratchname>_*.obj`. Also the /O2 first block of a repeated "if (obj) x=obj+12 else 0" smart-pointer assignment is tail-duplicated by cl whenever the source has a null-branch, so the original's single shared block is not reproducible with plain if/else or volatile.
  Fix idea: chk.py could print its .obj path (or take `--dis <sym>`). Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [cl1_part s56-57] codegen — four tricks that cost time. (1) /O2 modules that use SEH (`push -1; push handler; mov eax,fs:[0]`) need `/GS-` in the flags override, otherwise cl adds the security-cookie prolog and every diff looks wrong. (2) A callee that builds a temp (`call f; ...; call dtor`) with the EH state set only AFTER the call is a function returning the struct by value (hidden out pointer first, returns it in eax): `vec->Add(MakeTmp(a))`, not `T tmp; Add(MakeTmp(&tmp,a))`. A `V()` temp passed by const-ref to an insert reproduces the zero-store + explicit state set, and an empty-but-user-declared `~T(){...}` is needed to keep the EH frame when the dtor call itself is inlined away. (3) `x - x` folds to 0 when written with one variable; to keep the original's `mov edx,esi; sub edx,esi` write the second operand as a member re-read (`ByteDiff(it->e, ee)`), and swapping members through an inline `static __forceinline void SwapPtr(T*& a, T*& b)` fixed a load-hoisting diff that inline temps did not. (4) An int by-value Matrix3 arg built through `Matrix3(const Matrix3& m){ Assign(m); }` (inline wrapper, out-of-line Assign) gives `sub esp,0x24; mov [slot],esp; ... mov ecx,[slot]; call`, while an out-of-line copy ctor gives `mov edx,esp`.
  Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons; chk.py could print the per-VA best symbol even when the size differs by >10% (it printed "no symbol of similar size" for a smaller complete function). Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [cl1_new s39 / s009e2490] codegen — a 4 KB gait-table builder with inline eastl::vector push_back/clear: original has no EH frame (drop /EHsc even though temps have dtors via Vec members) and needs `/O2 /arch:SSE /fp:fast` (inline `fld qword;fsqrt` of float-valued double consts). Unsolved: the original default-constructs each `Slot s[N]` member array TWICE with non-unrolled `ecx=N-1; jns` / `ecx=N; jne` loops; every spelling tried (member init, ctor-body loop, `s()`, `s[k]=Slot()`) gives one fully unrolled init. Also chk.py's newest-obj-in-work/match/chk is shared across agents, so `ls -t | head -1` picks another slice's obj; use the `<name>_*.obj` glob.
  Fix idea: chk.py could print the obj path it wrote. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00490a70] codegen — /Od slot order for big functions (25 locals): `od_names.py pick N` (evenly spaced ranks) gave 45 diff bytes at once, then a coordinate-descent over neighbouring pool names (compile ~6 candidate renames in parallel via threads, score with chk.py) reached 0 in two passes; also hole sizes found by `ScratchSlots<N>` placed per push_back/ctor site, a by-value param spill = inline wrapper calling the out-of-line body (Matrix3 copy ctor = inline `{ Assign(m); }`), and a default-arg temp (`EdList(const AllocTag& t = AllocTag())`) puts the tag byte in the named-local region. Fix idea: add an `od_names.py search` mode (generate role->name variants, parallel chk, hill-climb) and mention the default-arg/inline-wrapper tricks in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s0082cd30] codegen — a 1-byte tag struct with a user ctor (`struct ForceTag{bool v; ForceTag(bool x):v(x){}}`) passed by value reproduces `mov byte [m],0; mov eax,[m]; push eax` for rbtree DoInsertValue's bool arg, and a `volatile bool` gives movzx instead; /O2 UI code with array locals needs `/GS-` (extra __security_check_cookie calls otherwise); retail stack object sizes (cSPUIDeserializeHelper 0x270 in Read's frame) can differ from the 2008 PDB (0x274). Fix idea: note in docs/matching.md batch-lessons. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s0044d4f0/s005570d0] codegen — three levers cost most of the time: (1) an in-class intrusive_ptr::operator=(T*) containing Release is NOT inlined at /Od /Ob1 but the original inlined it: `__forceinline` fixed it (matched 2961-byte ctor); (2) float compares against a `fld dword [rdata]` constant with `fcompi` mean a float LITERAL (0.5f), not an extern global (g_Eps gave comiss and a 200-byte diff); read the constant from the image with pefile first; (3) `x = Corner(1)` with an sret-by-value decl adds a copy, while the original used the out-pointer form `Corner(&local, 1)` returning eax; declare both forms. Also `if (c) return false; else return true;` gives the doubled `jmp end`. Fix idea: add to docs/matching.md /Od notes. Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s0079b020 / s006553b0] toolchain — manifest default flags lack /GS-, but the 2008 EASTL code has no security cookie: a function with locals + EH compiled with the default flags emits `mov eax,[__security_cookie]; xor eax,esp` and never matches. Add `/GS-` to the manifest flag override (found only after reading the cmpdis diff). Also macOS `sed -i` needs `-i ''` (a bare `-i 's/..'` silently treats the script as the backup suffix and edits nothing).
  Fix idea: put /GS- in DEFAULT_FLAGS or document it in the Agent playbook lessons. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s00cecf10] codegen — a big property-loading SetValues (macro: `if (g && g->Get(id,&p) && p->type==13) f=*p->GetFloat()`) matches except for two things: cl picks `mov edi,9; lea ebp,[edi+4]` where the original has `mov ebp,13; lea edi,[ebp-4]`, and it drops the `test edi,edi` before the temp-string delete. `memcpy(b,lit,N-1)` (not a byte loop) is needed to keep the literal in rdata instead of folding immediates; `/fp:fast` is needed for `x*1000.0f` to be mulss; `/GS-` and no dtor (explicit Free) avoid EH frames.
- [s00780b70] tooling — chk.py drops candidates whose size differs from the original by more than n0//8, so a complete but shorter function prints "no symbol of similar size" with no hint of its size; I had to parse the .obj by hand to see 3175 vs 3680 bytes. Fix idea: print the best symbol's size even when outside the window. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s00780b70] codegen — math.h in C++ mode gives float overloads of sqrt/fabs (wrapper calls, extra COMDATs); cast the argument to double and use #pragma intrinsic(sqrt, fabs, tan). Fix idea: add a note to the playbook. Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [s005f9920/s00b181d0] codegen — three re-discovered tricks. (1) A local `struct { void* p; bool b; }` (EASTL pair<iterator,bool> out-param) made cl 15.00 emit a stack-realigned frame (`push ebp; mov ebp,esp; and esp,-8`) and a /GS cookie even without arrays; `struct { int v[3]; }` of the same size removed it (bisecting by deleting lines took ~40 min). (2) Inline `fsqrt` needs /fp:fast plus a float wrapper `float sqrtf_(float x){ return (float)sqrt((double)x); }` with `#pragma intrinsic(sqrt)`; a `* 0.5f` then stays a `fmul dword`. A first store to `vtbl+4` that survives before the second store (cGameData ctor) comes from an inline base-class ctor, so model the base as a member struct with its own inline ctor. (3) `fixed_vector` on the stack = {begin,end,cap=begin+0x400,pad[2],allocator=0} (0x18 bytes) then the 0x400 buffer, and cap == the frame end, which looks like a pointer to the return address in the asm.
  Fix idea: add to docs/matching.md Batch-2 lessons; chk.py could print the function's own mangled name for each VA instead of the nearest-size symbol (it mapped 00b18f90 to an unrelated Write). Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s01047850] docs — a 4 KB function with ~7 inlined copies of one "create visual" block plus garbled Ghidra output in the second half (trade-route lines) needed the raw asm to reconstruct; card.py output is 2000 lines. Fix idea: a card.py --no-decompile or --range option. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op2_big s00bd40a0] tooling — `tools/difftest/equiv.py s00bd40a0` dies before testing anything: `NameError: name 'gen_input' is not defined` (equiv.py:981), so agents can't self-check "complete" claims with the differential tester. Fix idea: restore/import gen_input (or add a smoke run of equiv.py to CI of the tools). Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [op2_big s00bd40a0] codegen — a `rep stosd` with a non-zero dword value inside an /O2 SSE module (grid relabel fill) is not a loop cl rewrote: it comes from the `__stosd` intrinsic with an index-based guard/count (`int end = w + col; if (col < end) __stosd(&g[r*n+col], label, end - col);`). A pointer-based fill(first,last) gives `sub; sar 2` instead. Fix idea: add to matching.md /O2 levers. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op2_big s00b60d80] codegen — A 10 KB init function matched everywhere except 41 inlined `vector<intrusive_ptr>::push_back` blocks (temp held in ecx and reloaded after each AddRef instead of esi). Cause: DoInsertValue was only declared; with the real EASTL DoInsertValue body in the TU (still called out of line) cl's escape analysis keeps the temp in esi and reloads only after the DoInsertValue call. Byte-exact after that one change. Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons ("give out-of-line EASTL callees their bodies; declaration-only changes caller regalloc"). Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [cl1_new s01114370] codegen — hkMoppAabbCastVirtualMachine::cast (4.2 KB x87 bytecode VM): the original has no fs:[0] frame, so no /EHsc despite the hkOstream local; `and esp,-16` frame comes from __declspec(align(16)) locals (hkVector4); a repeated subexpression like `(a+b)+(a+b)` must NOT go through a named float temp, or cl stores/reloads it (-250 bytes).
  Fix idea: note "Havok x87 modules: /GS- and no /EHsc" in Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [cl1_part 80-81] tooling — chk.py's "no symbol of similar size" hides member functions whose first compile is far off in size (e.g. an out-of-line inline helper); use -a, and note cmpdis.py needs the newest obj under work/match/chk (glob by mtime). Also Ghidra mislabels the locals of x87-heavy stdcall frames (reads ints as call args); trust the asm slots.
      Fix idea: have chk.py print the best symbol regardless of size filter when none is in range. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op2_big s00ae0e30] codegen/tooling — In 8 KB /O2 functions cl's per-function inline budget runs out: tiny inline helpers (cTransform::SetOffset 0x571d40, GetOrientation 0xad7b70, a Quaternion copy 0x572600) are called out of line late in the original, and in my rebuild small RAII dtors / helpers went out of line instead (fixed with __forceinline). Also `dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz` on loose floats never reproduces the original (x²+z²)+y² order under /fp:fast, but `(a - b).SquaredLength()` on a Vector3 class with a user ctor does. A vtable-slot+direct-call sequence diff (work/match/scratch_s00ae0e30_cmp.py, maps callees through `// 0x...` comments in the source) proved completeness quickly; +1 to the s01025840 entry asking for `chk.py --calls`.
  Fix idea: note the inline-budget effect and the SquaredLength form in Batch-2 lessons; ship the call-sequence diff as a tool. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s005a0510/s004942b0 op2_big] tooling/data — (1) difftest's symbol resolver prefers names from symbols/ over call-order alignment, so naming a callee after a COMDAT-folded or misattributed symbol silently binds it to the wrong VA ("resolution conflicts": cSPUILayout::cSPUILayout -> 0xdd1ca0, a 6-byte getter; SPUIHelpers::GetImageFromLayout/CreateImageWindow -> other helpers). Give callees unique names unless the symbol file's VA is the one you call. (2) The dev PDB symbols in work/devbuild/symbols.json carry full mangled signatures; grepping them by parameter shape (`YA_NPAVcSPEditorBlock@2@AAUcSPVector3@@...`) named a "FUN_" function in one step. (3) SP::cString is 0x14 bytes in retail (frame size was off by 8 until fixed); a quick ordered call-target/vtable-slot/field-offset comparison script (work/match/scratch_s005a0510_seq.py) caught a 4-byte struct-offset bug that compiled fine.
  Fix idea: have difftest prefer the aligned VA when the named VA's size/shape is implausible; mention devbuild/symbols.json signature search in the playbook. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s0044c690/s005c53c0] tooling — no way to see an /Od diff with stack-slot offsets and call targets normalized away, so a 3.6 KB /Od function shows ~1500 diff lines of pure frame-layout noise. I wrote work/match/scratch_s0044c690_norm.py (cmpdis variant: `ebp - 0x..` -> `ebp-S`, every call -> `<call>`, relocated addrs -> `<addr>`) and the real structural diffs (missing copies, wrong inline wrappers, int vs movss vs fld copies) became visible. Fix idea: add a `--norm-slots` flag to cmpdis.py. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s0044c690] codegen — at /Od /arch:SSE, user *operator=* bodies (`x = v.x`) compile to fld/fstp, user *constructors/copy-ctors* to movss, and trivial struct copies to int moves; a different pick among these explains most "Vector3 copy flavour" diffs. Inline helper `if (a) return n; else if (b) return 1; return 0;` leaves the dead `jmp` seen in the original (plain if/else assignment does not). Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s005f16d0/s004974e0] codegen — three things cost time. (1) A local `wchar_t buf[0x20]` plus `/GS` adds a cookie and changes frame size; the card's `sub esp,N` with no cookie xor means add `/GS-` to the manifest flags, and a 16-byte `cString` (not 12) fixes the remaining frame delta. (2) Stack-passed args: `add esp,N` after a call that is smaller than the pushes means the leftover pushes belong to a LATER call (e.g. `RemapTypeId(1 arg)` then a 3-arg vcall that reuses two already-pushed words; a by-value Matrix3 copy left on the stack is the callee-popped arg of the next thiscall). (3) MSVC compiles an override that only lives in a secondary-base vtable with `this` = that subobject (`lea edi,[esi-0xc]`), so declare the override normally in the MI class instead of faking a thunk; a null check that survives a prior deref (`item ? &item->secondBase : 0`) and an out-of-line vs inlined message ctor in two arms of one switch were not reproduced.
      Fix idea: card.py could annotate each call with its callee-pop size (add esp,N) and flag leftover pushes. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s0055beb0/s004ae3b0 cl1_part] codegen — three /Od shapes that cost the most rounds. (1) An AutoRefCount released before an out-param call (`cmp [r],0; je; mov ecx,[r]; mov [t],ecx; mov [r],0; call [vt+8]` placed BEFORE the arg pushes) is a plain member `Reset(){ if(mp){T* p=mp; mp=0; p->Release();} }` called as a statement, not `operator=(0)` (that one carries the dead `if(p) p->AddRef()` junk, seen in Shutdown) and not an inlined `operator&` (that would sit between the pushes). (2) `ScratchSlots<N>()` only changes the frame at function scope; inside nested/for/case scopes it does nothing, so use a named unused `uint32_t hole[N]` there. Holes were 4/9/10 dwords here. (3) Names inside one function only matter if the frame size already equals the original: chk.py scores all candidate renames equal until the frame size is right, so fix the frame first (compare `sub esp,N`), then brute-force names. Also: an `AsInterface(int)` switch with 0xee3f516e needs `uint32_t` (signed puts the negative case first in the compare tree), and BSD `sed -i 's/..'` eats the script as the backup suffix (use python or `sed -i ''`).
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons; ship tools/matching brute-name search (random role->name assignment compiled in one object, ranked by chk.py -a) as a real tool. Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [op2_big s0055aac0] codegen — /Od /Ob1 inline decisions for a recursive inline (eastl::vector<Constraint> dtor, recursive through ~Constraint) are not per-callee: in one function cl 15 inlines some call sites and calls others out of line, and which ones depends on the total site count and surrounding code (a test TU with N identical `q.push_back(Constraint(...))` temps inlines none for N<=8, sites 1-2 for N=10, 1-7 for N=12, 4-19 for N=20, 4-25 for N=25; adding plain statements before them makes all out of line). The original cheat declines the early (query/test) sites and inlines the 20 stress-loop ones; I found no source shape that reproduces that, so the frame (0x574 vs 0x50c) stays off although every named local matched. Fix idea: someone with time could map cl's /Ob1 budget rule (scratch harness: work/match/scratch_s0055aac0_exp.sh); until then treat mixed inline/outline of one recursive callee as a non-goal. Also: BSD sed has no \b, so `sed -i '' 's/\bname\b/x/g'` silently does nothing on macOS; rename with python re. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [cl1_part 84-85] codegen — (1) `wcscmp` expands to the original's unrolled 2-wchar inline loop only with `#include <string.h>`, `#pragma intrinsic(wcscmp)` and a `const wchar_t*` signature (an own `extern "C"` ushort declaration is a real call). (2) An `/Od` local that is a 0x1d8-byte struct needs `uint32_t rest[]` not `char rest[]` or /GS adds a cookie; `IRef** operator&() { return GetAddr(); }` (inline wrapper over an out-of-line call) reproduces the arg temp slot. (3) `chk.py` only lists symbols within 1/8 of the target size, so a very different first draft shows "no symbol of similar size"; compile with a scratch copy of cmpdis against the latest `work/match/chk/*.obj`. (4) A 64-bit `unsigned long long flags` local plus a fixed_vector local with `__declspec(align(8))` buffer gives the original's `push ebp; and esp,-8` EH frame; add `/GS-` and declare the dealloc helper `throw()` to drop the final EH state store.
      Fix idea: make cmpdis.py accept a chk.py symbol and pick the newest obj by itself; add a name-search helper for `/Od` slots (work/match/scratch_cl1_hill.py does a hill climb over local names). Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s00cae280/s00ee5480] data — card.py callee labels can be actively misleading: 0x0080fee0 "MultiHeapObject::operator_new" is a no-arg getter (the `push 0x5b598fa` before it is the arg of the NEXT thiscall), 0x00435ed0 "KillSetiEffects" is a cdecl (soundID, GetRecorderState()) UI-sound call, and 0x00f3e8a0 looks 2-arg at some sites because the outer cdecl's rightmost arg is pushed before the inner call. MSVC pushes all args before evaluating a call's object expression (`PlanetModel()->F(a,b)`, `WindowManager()->SendMsg(0, WindowManager()->GetMainWindow(), m, false)`), so count pushes from each callee's `ret N` before trusting card/Ghidra arity. Also: `_mm_cvt_ss2si` makes cl add an aligned ebp frame; the frameless cvtss2si/cmovb ceil is the __asm CeilToInt helper.
      Fix idea: card.py could print each direct callee's `ret N` (stack-arg bytes) next to the call. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s007b6570/s00e0df70] codegen — (1) a module with `movss`/`mulss` float math needs `/arch:SSE /fp:fast`; plain `/arch:SSE` still emits x87 for float math and `_ftol2`/fistp for float->int casts, which looks like a source problem but is a flags problem (check docs/matching.md flags list before rewriting float code). (2) Inlined `new T()` with an array local (`int a[2]`) adds /GS cookie code the originals lack: add `/GS-` to the manifest flags. (3) A non-polymorphic base placed BEFORE the vptr stores (e.g. `f10=0` store ahead of the vtable store) is reproduced by giving the intermediate class its own virtual, with the member init in that class's ctor; DSE removes the dead vptr store. (4) The EH-dtor state counts tell you which members have inline dtors: a callee declared `throw()` (operator_delete__) suppresses the state store before it. Fix idea: add (1)-(2) to the flags bullets in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s0097a130] codegen — OnRebuild (WinGrid, 4 KB) emits an inline `fnstcw; or 0xc00; fldcw; fistp qword; fldcw` for `(int)(__int64)ceil(x)`, which no /arch:SSE[2] /fp:fast variant reproduced (cl emits `call __ftol2_sse`). Also card.py hides which stack slots are struct args: a `push ecx` then `mov [esp+N]` stores build an on-stack arg struct, so S-relative offsets (S = esp after prologue pushes) must be recomputed per call.
  Fix idea: document the ceil/fistp inline pattern's source (maybe an EA inline-asm helper) in "Batch-2 lessons". Time lost: ~25 min.
- [s004680c0/s0051afd0 op2_big] codegen — four /Od frame facts for big functions (both functions here got the instruction stream identical; 0051afd0 byte-exact). (1) Holes from DECLINED inline callees: real EASTL `push_back(const T&)` bodies (byte-identical to 0x475130) leave a 2-dword hole per call, `push_back()` of a 0x8c element leaves 37; an inline ctor with a 9-member init list was declined too (6-dword hole = its local array). (2) After ~20 declined attempts cl stops reserving frames for later call sites of the SAME push_back (an inline budget), so later sites must call an out-of-line declaration; I could not trigger the cutoff naturally. (3) Struct temporaries (`TypeID(GetTypeID()).Variant()`) live in the object-temp area right after named locals, while scalar inline temps go below; a plain inline-local copy lands at the end of the frame. (4) Named-local slot order depends on the whole TU (identifier interning), so name sets must be scored ONE variant per compile; N renamed copies in one file give different orders. Also: out-of-line `Reset()` results passed as out-params go through a temp only via an inline conversion (`operator T**()`), and `*_Bits` bitfield writes give the `mov ecx,imm; and ecx,0xff; shl ecx,8; and edx,mask; or edx,ecx` shape.
  Fix idea: od_names.py could score candidate sets in the real TU (one compile per set) and print role order; document (1)-(3) in Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [op2_big s005b2d90/s00726c20] tooling — the dev PDB (work/devbuild/SporeBin/SporeApp.pdb) module streams also hold S_REGREL32 records per function: every local's real name, type and frame offset, plus the full signature (e.g. QuantizeVertices(cMeshData*, float granularity, tQuantizeType)), and shared offsets show which locals the compiler packed into one slot. work/match/scratch_s005b2d90_locals.py <dev-va|name> dumps them (msf.py + a small TPI type namer); scratch_s005b2d90_calls.py <obj> <sym> <va> <size> <src> [mangled-prefix=va ...] compares the ordered direct-call/vcall-slot sequence of an .obj with the original (overloads and template instances need the explicit prefix=va map). Separately, I lost a round by reading a `je` as `jne` from memory when writing a 3-way branch; listing every jcc with its compare (awk over the card) before finishing caught it. Fix idea: add a `--locals` mode to card.py that prints the dev twin's S_REGREL32 list, and ship a calls-compare mode in chk.py. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s004680c0 op2_big] codegen — follow-up: 004680c0 (5152 B) also went byte-exact. Fast recipe for the named-local order of a big /Od scope: use names that ALREADY occur earlier in the TU (member/field names of stub structs); their bucket is fixed, so one compile gives their slot order, then assign roles in that observed order and swap out names that tie (same bucket; order then follows declaration). 7 iterations of 2 single-variant compiles (~3 s each) solved 18 locals (work/match/scratch_s004680c0_fit2.py). Fresh names do not work this way: their order shifts whenever any other new name changes.
  Fix idea: turn scratch_s004680c0_fit2.py into tools/matching/od_fit_tu.py (roles + target offsets in, names out). Time lost: ~0 (found while iterating).
- [op2_big s6 s00f4c300] codegen/tooling — /Od slot order is simpler than "fit K" suggests: cl 15 uses 16 buckets keyed by the local's NAME only (type and scope size do not change it), buckets in a fixed order, later-declared first inside a bucket. A ruler of one int name per bucket (work/match/scratch_s00f4c300_bucket.py) measures any name's bucket in one compile, and a small DP (scratch_s00f4c300_solve.py) then picks names per scope; with ScratchSlots<N> for the declined-inline holes this took a 5816-byte /Od function from 4556 diff bytes to byte-exact in one compile. Also: the original's `lea; push` after a converting-ctor temp comes from an implicit conversion (f(a - b) with a reference param), while an explicit Vector3(a - b) passes the ctor's eax. Fix idea: add `od_names.py bucket <names>` and `od_names.py solve` built on the ruler. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op2_big s6-7] tooling — difftest/equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED when (a) the slice's symbols file still names the VA FUN_xxxxxxxx while the source defines the real method (it looks the symbol up by that name; a `// @ 0x...` marker plus a real name in symbols/slices fixed it), or (b) an extern is declared only in another slice's shared header (the `// 0x...` annotation must be in the slice's own source; redeclaring the extern there works). Havok agents that walk vtables of their inputs get 0 valid random inputs, so equivalence for them rests on static evidence. Fix idea: let resolve.py read annotations from headers the source includes, and have equiv.py fall back to the `// @` marker. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op2_big s00461680] codegen — /Od EASTL frame layout: declaring push_back/ctor/~VectorBase extern (no body) loses the reserved inline holes and even flips which call sites cl inlines (the early-return ~vector got inlined). Giving them their real inline bodies (fixed_vector ctor via a fixed_vector_allocator temp + copy ctor that copies only mpPoolBegin, push_back with placement new, ~vector -> destruct(first,last), ~VectorBase -> DoFree -> allocator.deallocate) reproduced cl's own declines and took a 4950-byte function from 4765 to 30 diff bytes; those helpers then matched byte-exact on their own (0x0041d050, 0x00422380, 0x004209b0). A walk that prints the first differing [ebp-N] per instruction pair (work/match/scratch_s00461680_pairs.py) finds where a hole is missing much faster than cmpdis. Fix idea: ship that walker as a tools/matching option. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00543f00] codegen — /Od frame holes from a declined inline (here vector_map::insert, 30 calls in one function) appear only for the first N calls: cl stops reserving after roughly 720 bytes of reserved inline frames, so the last calls have no hole. Hole size per call depends on the exact inline call chain (insert -> member lower_bound(key) -> eastl::lower_bound gave 28 bytes; calling eastl::lower_bound directly gave 36). Fix idea: add to the /Od frame-layout notes in docs/matching.md: "irregular holes that stop part-way through a run of identical calls = declined inline with real EASTL body; try the EASTL member-forwarding shape". Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s0073f320/s004d34f0] codegen — (1) EH state tracking: a dtor whose later member/base dtors only call `operator_delete__` gets NO per-member `mov [esp+x],state` writes when that function is declared `void __cdecl operator_delete__(void*) throw();` (all remaining dtors are nothrow, so MSVC elides the state updates); without `throw()` every inlined vector dtor adds a state write (container dtor 0x7402e0 went 81 diff -> 0). (2) /O2 functions with a `char buf[N]` local grow a /GS cookie; the original had none, so give the manifest line `/GS-` or use int arrays. (3) eastl vectors with sp_vector_allocator are 0x14 bytes (3 pointers + 2 uninitialised allocator dwords); the zero-init ctor only stores the first 3, and the ctor's unused gap slots at /Od (4d3760) came from `ScratchSlots<2>` in the Key sub-ctor. (4) A base with an inline `virtual ~Base(){}` reproduces the trailing base-vptr store of a derived dtor; a base with `field=0` ctor + derived vptr store reproduces `[4]=0; vptr=...` (first vptr store is dead-store eliminated at /O2). (5) /Od inline helpers that return bool as `return false;` still fold to `mov byte,0`; the original `xor eax,eax; mov [ebp-2],al` temp tag (cSpeciesProfile ctor 0x4d3dd0) was not reproduced. Fix idea: document (1)-(4) in matching.md. Time lost: ~120.
- [op2_big s22-23] docs — two things cost re-derivation on 5 KB /O2 functions: (1) cl 2008 drops the `if (p)` of an inlined AutoRefCount dtor when every path dereferenced p first (unchecked Release in the original is NOT a hint that the dtor has no null check); (2) an inline member that is sometimes inlined and sometimes called (SetClearColor/AddAdditionalRect in cThumbnailManager) reproduces the original's per-site choices on its own when given its real in-class body, and an out-of-line same-TU body (SetCustomParams) gives the "no ecx reload after call" pattern. Also both functions had no /GS cookie (needs /GS-).
  Fix idea: add (1)-(2) to Batch-2 lessons; have chk.py print "cookie present in mine but not original -> try /GS-". Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s0044ae00/s00691680] codegen — (1) A by-value struct return passed straight to a by-value arg (`Place(GetOffsetA(1), GetOffsetB(1))`) is built in the outgoing arg area only if the struct has a user copy ctor; a POD or a struct with only operator= gets a temp plus a 3-dword copy. (2) `x > 0 ? x : 0` gives and-mask code, `int p = 0; if (x > 0) p = x;` gives the original's `cmovg` (needs /arch:SSE). (3) chk.py diff counts are meaningless once a function's frame shifts: use cmpdis (call lines print as `<+0x..>`, so ignore those). (4) /O2 EH modules here are /GS-; without it the cookie prologue hides everything. (5) fixed_hash_set/fixed_vector inlines (count loop, pool-or-delete dtor) can be written as small local structs; the ctor/insert/free calls are plain thiscalls on the set base.
  Fix idea: chk.py could mask rel32 call targets in its diff count; add /GS- to the default flag list for /O2 EH regions. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [op2_big s00efa1b0] tooling — cmpdis.py leaves our object's call/jump targets as `<+0xNN>` and absolute operands as `[0]`/`[4]` while the original side shows `<ext>`/`<addr>`, so every call and global load shows as a diff (1400 noise lines on a 4.9 KB function); also disasm.py's length arg is decimal-only (`0x30` crashes). Fix idea: normalize both sides the same way in cmpdis (work/match/scratch_s00efa1b0_nd.py does it as a post-filter) and parse the length with int(x, 0). Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [cl1_new 63-64] tooling — chk.py reports "(no symbol of similar size)" without saying that the best candidate was filtered by the 12% size window (my first complete 4 KB function compiled to 4864 vs 4004 bytes with duplicated branches and /EHsc adding unwind funclets). Fix idea: print the nearest symbol and its size when the size filter rejects everything. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s006fb5c0] codegen — a spurious `mov [esp+N],-1` EH-state store before an inline dtor (vector temp in swap) vanished only after declaring `void operator delete[](void*) throw();` and the destroy-range helper `throw()`; also a local `/GS-` is needed for old-style EH prologs. chk.py's "best symbol" column often names an unrelated function in the same object (use -a and cmpdis with the exact mangled name).
  Fix idea: note in docs/matching.md that nothrow-declared delete/dtor helpers remove EH state stores. Time lost: ~40 min.
- [s008bb550] codegen — T2K/Font Fusion (EA-UTF, 0x8b0000-0x8d2000) is C: compile with /TC. C's `c ? uint8 : uint8` is int-typed, which gives each branch of the inlined ReadUnsignedByteMacro its own movzx; under /TP no spelling reproduced it. Also, /O2 spill-slot layout follows variable identity: block-scoped temps vs shared function-scope vars (and reusing one var for two jobs) reshuffle every [esp+X] in a 9.5 KB function. A per-switch-case diff (jump tables from both sides) plus a cartesian variant runner took Type2BuildChar from 8600 to 4 differing bytes.
      Fix idea: note "/TC for T2K" in docs/matching.md; have chk.py report code-only bytes (its 9760 size includes 200 bytes of the next functions). Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s006b6920/s00449ed0] codegen — (1) /Od: helpers that return a struct by value construct it in place (no extra rep-movsd/movss copy) only if the struct has a user copy ctor, so give vector types a user copy ctor/operator= (fld/fstp); a stack hole at the frame bottom is `ScratchSlots<N>()` (BBox::Scale needed N=8, a 12-dword hole needed N=12; both then matched). (2) In ~cStringManager-style classes the base vptr store is absent only if the class has no base with a virtual dtor; declare the dtor virtual in the derived class itself. (3) card.py names callees by Ghidra guesses ("~cStringManager", "_Unchecked_idl0<>", "MatrixFromQuaternion") that are really vector::reserve<T> / matrix multiply / in-place orthonormalize, so judge by the asm.
      Fix idea: card.py could print `ret N`/sret hints and say when a callee pops only part of the pushed args (caller pre-pushes the outer call's arg). Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00439110] tooling — the 2008 dev PDB keeps S_BPREL32 local-variable records (real local and parameter names, per scope) for many functions, and no tool exposes them. Using the real names for an /Od function's locals reproduced the original hash-ordered frame at once (SetModelBasedOnSymmetrySign went from 4083 to 518 diff bytes; then a rename search over 60 copies per TU fixed the locals added after 2008). Also: a declined inline's reserved frame appears when the helper's real body contains a delete-expression (cl /Ob1 then refuses to inline it, like `delete this`), e.g. EASTL ~VectorBase/DoFree; an extern declaration gives no hole. Fix idea: add `tools/pdb_locals.py <func>` (work/match/scratch_s00439110_pdblocals.py does it: walk the module stream from S_GPROC32 to S_END, print 0x110B/0x1106 records) and mention it in the /Od playbook. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s004c5200/s0077a160] codegen — four /Od tricks that cost most of the time. (1) A struct local whose address is only passed to a callee (`GetPos(&tmp,i)`) is usually an sret TEMPORARY (`Vec3 GetPos(int)` + `const Vec3&` param), not a named local; making it named shifts the whole slot group, so decide named-vs-temp before searching names. (2) `__forceinline` is needed for inline helpers with loops or several statements (plain `inline` was silently not inlined at /Ob1, e.g. a destruct-range helper or a bitfield key builder). (3) `_InterlockedIncrement(&x)` (not `_InterlockedExchangeAdd(&x,1)`) gives the original `mov r,addr; add r,8; mov r2,1; lock xadd` order. (4) A 3-dword frame hole sitting between an inline's `old` local and its `this` temp was reproduced by a `ScratchSlots<3>()` placed after `old` inside a copy of operator=. Also: random/hill-climb name search over ~8 roles (many variants of one function in one object, scored by chk.py -a) found the /Od slot names in a few minutes once the structure was right; and card.py's byte size for 0x77a160 (6826) hides that the region holds TWO functions (0x77af70 has its own prologue).
  Fix idea: ship a name-search helper (template with $ROLE$ placeholders + chk.py -a scoring) in tools/matching; make card.py/slice_info.py flag a second `push ebp; mov ebp,esp` prologue inside a function's range. Time lost: ~150 minutes.
- [cl1_new 65-66] tooling — chk.py scores a VA only against object symbols within n/8 bytes of its size, so functions whose try/catch funclets (or template COMDATs) are emitted separately show "no symbol of similar size" and out-of-line members that cl dropped (unreferenced inline members, virtuals of never-constructed classes) silently vanish from the list. Fix idea: let chk accept `va=symbol` pairs and score exactly that pair; print object symbols that matched no VA. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s0064c8e0/s0058d430] codegen/tooling — (1) cl 15 drops the null check of a pointer (incl. `this`->base conversion, AutoRefCount dtor `if (p)`) once that pointer was dereferenced on the same path, so `lea eax,[esi+4]` without `test` is plain `CalloutMessageBox(this, ...)`, and an unconditional `p->Release()` can still be an AutoRefCount dtor. (2) `delete[] p` / `operator delete[](p)` emits `??3` (scalar delete) unless `void operator delete[](void*)` is declared in the TU; EA's 0x00f47380 is delete[]. (3) For the "complete" evidence a reusable checker is work/match/scratch_s0064c8e0_calls.py <obj> <sym> <va> <size> <src.cpp> [-c|-t|-v]: size/call ratios, direct-call multiset+order (maps names via `// 0x...` comments), vtable slots, big immediates (orig relocs filtered); capstone prints small offsets in decimal, which broke my first slot tracker.
      Fix idea: promote the checker to tools/matching/evidence.py and add (1)/(2) to the Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s007d56f0/s009245b0] codegen — Three cheap EH/vptr levers cost time: (1) an EH frame in a ctor/dtor with no unwind objects of its own comes from calling an extern helper that may throw; declare it `throw()` (Matrix3::Assign, operator delete, range-destroy helpers) and the frame vanishes. (2) An abstract first base that stores no vptr is `__declspec(novtable)`; a map/tree stored at +0xc after a refcount base is usually a *base class* (vptr store lands after its copy ctor), not a member. (3) The class's own vptr store at dtor entry disappears when the dtor is compiler-generated (no user `~X(){}`). chk.py's "best symbol" column also mislabels near-misses (it picked ??0cSplitManager for an unrelated dtor); use cmpdis.py with the real mangled name.
  Fix idea: add these three to docs/matching.md /O2 levers; have chk.py print the symbol with the same size, not just fewest diff bytes. Time lost: ~40.
- [s00452080/s0050aac0 op2_big] tooling/codegen — for big /Od functions, cmpdis is unreadable because every stack offset differs; a diff with esp/ebp offsets AND e?x registers normalized away, plus a check that the orig<->mine slot mapping is one-to-one (work/match/scratch_s00452080_cmp.py -d -r -m), showed the 4.5 KB BuildNewPhysicsShape was 4 bytes off (then byte-exact) and caught swapped max() operands that the normalized diff alone hid. Codegen: (1) an 8-byte by-value temp (hkPropertyValue{uint64}) under `#pragma pack(4)` lands 4 bytes off; give it pack(8). (2) Havok inline helpers (hkMatrix3/hkTransform::setIdentity, hkVector4::operator=) need __forceinline or cl /Ob1 calls them. (3) EASTL `max(a,b){return (a<b)?b:a;}` compiles to `comiss b,a`, so read max() operand order from the asm. (4) A `T x; x = f();` vs `T c = f(); x = c - y;` choice shows in the push order before the call. Also: PDB name "EditorUtils::GetHavokTransformFromMatrix" on 0x453250 is wrong (it is hkMassProperties::hkMassProperties).
  Fix idea: ship the normalized diff + slot-mapping check as a cmpdis.py option (--norm). Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op2_big s30-31] tooling — for a large non-exact function the "evidence" check (ordered direct-call targets, vtable slots, float constants, original vs obj) has to be re-scripted per agent (work/match/scratch_s005ba320_skel.py), and cmpdis.py reads only len(mine)+64 bytes of the original, so a shorter compile hides the original's tail. Fix idea: ship the skeleton compare as `cmpdis.py --skel` (resolve COFF reloc symbols, map original call targets via card names) and read the original's full length from the function table. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op2_big s00515710/s005d7520] tooling — for big functions the useful completeness check is a frame/register-normalized instruction diff, not chk.py's byte count (3957/2298 "diff bytes" here although both bodies are instruction-for-instruction equivalent; /Od frame slots alone shift every byte). work/match/scratch_s00515710_norm.py (masks [ebp-N], call targets, globals) and scratch_s005d7520_t.sh (masks [esp+N] and registers) plus scratch_s005b2d90_calls.py (ordered call/vcall-slot sequence) and scratch_s005d7520_imm.py (ordered non-address immediates) proved both functions complete in minutes. Also: cmpdis prints large immediates (ids/hashes like 0x2f7d0005) as <addr>, hiding constant mistakes; and new-expression sizes come from stub classes, so every `new` stub needs its real sizeof (the first compile pushed 4/8 instead of 0x120/0x250 and only the immediate comparison caught it).
  Fix idea: ship a `chk.py --struct` mode (normalized diff + call/immediate sequences) and make cmpdis keep non-relocated immediates. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [op2_big s00447150] codegen — a 0x24-byte "hole" at the top of an /Od frame was not a hole: the local was an eastl::fixed_vector<T*,8> (0x38 bytes; ctor sets mpBegin to the inline buffer at +0x18), not a 16-byte vector. Check a local's out-of-line ctor before adding ScratchSlots. The other holes were declined inlines' frames (DoFree = 3 dwords, placed in ~vector before the call). For name-hash slot order, a hill-climb that compiles 40 renamed copies per round and counts pairwise slot inversions converged in ~10 rounds (work/match/scratch_s00447150_climb.py). Fix idea: make it a tool. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00602940/s0074d4b0] tooling — chk.py's size-filtered best-symbol hid AddTab/CollectModels/CollectSweep (shown as "no symbol of similar size" or another function's name), so I had to guess mangled names for cmpdis.py. Also a function with the old EH prolog (`push -1; push <handler>`, no cookie) needs `/GS-`, which a manifest override must spell out. Fix idea: chk.py -a should list every symbol with its diff for the given VA regardless of size. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s005ab7a0/s004f01f0] codegen/data — (1) In the /O2 /arch:SSE editor modules, `movss [m],x; fld [m]; fsqrt; fstp [m]; divss` (SSE math, x87 root through the same slot) is an inline asm helper `float Sqrt(float f){ __asm fld f  __asm fsqrt  __asm fstp f  return f; }`; sqrtf/(float)sqrt/named locals all keep the whole expression on x87 (fld1/fdivrp), which also changes rounding. A sum squared with `fmul st(0),st(0)` before fsqrt is a two-arg asm helper too. (2) Retail cSPEditorBlock+0x33c is mSymmetricBlock (dev 0x334 + 8), not mSocketConnector as s005aad00.h says; 0x438700/0x438a40 link/unlink the symmetric twin. (3) /Od `T** AsPPointer()` result spilled to a temp before the push = inline wrapper `T** AsPPointer(){ return GetAddress(); }` around the out-of-line 0x41d870.
  Fix idea: add (1) to Batch-2 lessons next to the Floor/Ceil asm helpers; fix the s005aad00.h comment. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s008d2590/s009159b0] tooling — chk.py truncates compile errors to the LAST 3000 chars, so the first (real) error is hidden when a header cascades (wasted ~10 min on expat's xmlparse.c `#define atts m_atts` macros clashing with my locals); also chk.py picks the "best symbol" by size, so a function whose size differs (e.g. 1466-byte one) shows "no symbol of similar size" and cmpdis needs the mangled name from `strings -a` on the obj. Idea: print the head of the compile log and a per-VA `--sym` override. Codegen facts found: assigning to the parameter (`id -= 2000;`) is what makes cl emit `sub eax,imm` instead of `add eax,-imm`; `bool r = expr; return r;` gives `and al,1` while `return expr;` gives `and eax,1`; a static/ctor array member loop is inlined only WITHOUT /EHsc (otherwise `__vec_ctor` call); `T x = {0}` (80-byte struct) compiles to `mov [x],0` + `memset(&x+4,0,0x4c)`; `/fp:fast` removes the fstp/fld roundtrip after x87 `fabs(float)` compares; statics get register conventions only when something in the TU calls them (add a tiny extern caller to give an address-taken static its custom convention).
- [op2_big s00736b00] codegen — in a 4 KB /O2 EH function, an in-class smart-ref `operator=` (AddRef/Release swap) stayed out of line (5 extra direct calls, vcalls 9 vs 15) until marked `__forceinline`; the seq script (copy of scratch_s00ee16f0_seq.py) showed it at once. Fix idea: mention "big callers hit cl's inline budget: __forceinline small ref-count helpers" in Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~5 minutes.
- [cl1_new 73-74] tooling — chk.py's per-VA "best symbol" pairs by size filter, so a function whose own symbol is a different size shows a bogus symbol/diff (e.g. SetGridFlags reported as IsCellUsed 75 diff when its own diff was 27); cmpobj's `diff@` list is capped at 32 entries, so there is no way to get a true per-function diff count for a non-matching function. Fix idea: chk.py `--sym <mangled>=<va>` pairs, and an uncapped diff count in cmpobj. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s0049de30/s004e7990] codegen — (1) a POD struct returned by value and assigned (`v = f(v)`) gets an extra temp copy; declare the callee's return type as a derived class with a user copy ctor (`cSPVector3 : Vec3`) while the locals stay raw `Vec3` and the original's direct `mov edx,[eax]` copy appears. (2) Wrapping a whole /Od body in `if (a != b) {...}` is how `ucomiss; lahf; test ah,0x44; jnp end` arises (not `if (a==b) return;`). (3) A discarded `ScratchSlots<N>` placed right after the last use of a value reproduced both the 5-dword eastl-vector-dtor hole (0x4b5440) and a 2-dword hole; try positions with a one-compile variant file (func_v0..vN) ranked by chk.py -a. (4) A temporary passed by const& (`vec.push_back(AutoRefCount<T>(p))`) takes a later (lower) temp slot than a named local with the same lifetime. (5) A user `cSPVector3(const cSPVector3&)` that only chains to the base ctor is NOT inlined at /Ob1 (out-of-line call emitted); copy the members directly in the ctor body. Fix idea: document (1),(4),(5) in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~60.
- [s0070aed0/s00512840] codegen — three re-discovered tricks. (1) /O2+EH: a temporary `*cfg = cLightingConfig();` whose dtor is inlined with NO extra EH states needs a user inline `~T(){}` plus `throw()` on the member-dtor callees (free / Release); without it cl calls the generated dtor out of line, with it but without throw() it adds per-member state stores. (2) /Od float tests `movss xmm0,[0.0]; comiss xmm0,[x]; jb` are a NEGATED `x <= 0.0f` (NaN passes), i.e. `if (a<=0 && b<=0 && c<=0) continue;` — writing `0.0f < x` gives `comiss [x],[0]; ja`. (3) /Od struct copy whose destination address is spilled to a temp slot before `rep movsd` came from an inline copy helper; accessor-method spellings (`acc.Normal(e)` vs `p[idx[e]]`) fix base/index load order. Also: evidence script that resolves my reloc names to original call targets is work/match/scratch_s00512840_evid.py (pass extra name=addr map).
      Fix idea: add (1)-(2) to Batch-2 lessons; ship the call-sequence evidence script as a tool. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s006b37d0/s0074c260] codegen — EASTL quick_sort_impl/get_partition match when written as literal EASTL templates with raw-pointer iterators (iterator_traits<T*>) and the pivot passed as `*median(...)` directly (no `const value_type position` local); a user copy ctor on the 6-float element gives the fld/fstp outgoing-arg copy. A wrapper function around the template never matches; explicitly instantiate `template void eastl::quick_sort_impl<...>` so the instantiation itself is the compared symbol.
  Fix idea: chk.py size-filtered best symbol names unrelated templates here; use cmpdis with a symbol substring. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s009d75b0/s009d9620] codegen — (1) a by-value class arg with a dtor and `ret 0x208` is a `__thiscall` functor `operator()(S a, S b)` (cl emits `lea ecx,[stack]` at the call), not `__stdcall`/`__cdecl`; define `struct Less { bool operator()(S,S); }` and a local `Less pred;` in the sort. (2) A `char raw[N]` stub member in a stack local adds a /GS cookie; use `u32 raw[N/4]` (matched AddElem). (3) `/arch:SSE` alone gives x87 compares for `fa > fb`; `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` gives comiss. (4) chk.py's best-symbol column named the wrong function for most rows here (size filter); use cmpdis with the mangled name. Also zsh does not word-split `$p` in `for p in "a b"` loops, so cmpdis got one arg.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00ee16f0/s00ee4390] codegen/data — (1) SP::cString is 0x14 bytes in retail (dev PDB says 0x1c): with 0x1c the frame of 0x00ee16f0 was 0x14 too big and every slot shifted; 0x14 gave the exact frame (and fits 0x00ee4390, where a 0x1c Message copy shares the slot). (2) card.py/slice_info report 0x00ee16f0 as 4,452 bytes but Refresh ends at 0x00ee279f; the next 180 bytes are a separate function (0x00ee27a0) that Ghidra folded in, so chk.py's diff never drops below ~180 while cmpobj (symbol size) says MATCH. (3) The lever that took Refresh from ~2400 to 0 diff: when the original pushes an outer call's constant args only AFTER a nested call returns (`call Find; push 0; push eax; call GetDrawable; push 0; push eax; push esi; call SetIcon`), the source stored the nested results in locals (`IWindow* b = w->Find(..); void* d = GetDrawable(b, 0); SetIcon(w, d, 0);`); nested-call args make cl push the constants first. Same for sret temps (`ResourceKey key = t->GetImage(); SetWindowImage(img, key, -1);`) and for object-first loads (`g->f74` loaded before the args = an inline accessor `GetData()->F(arg())`). Swapping an if/else (`if (a || b) {B} else {A}`) fixed the last register-allocation diff.
      Fix idea: have card.py flag ranges that contain an inner `ret` followed by a new prologue, and note the cString size in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00983790/s0098a820] codegen — (1) The "vptr loaded before the args, slot loaded after" shape of MSVC virtual calls (and `this->f()` with no explicit pointer temp) only comes out with REAL virtual classes: a generated list of `virtual void sXX();` slot placeholders (one per 4-byte slot, byte offset in the name) plus the real signature at the used slot made 9 more WinSlider functions byte-exact than VSLOT() function-pointer casts did; manual `vt = ...` stores also lose the compiler's base-then-derived vptr store order and dead-store behaviour. (2) `m.src = id1 ? id1 : id0;` matches where `uint32 id = id1; if (!id) id = id0;` leaves an extra store; a single-case `switch (x) { case 2: ... }` gives `sub eax,2; jne`. (3) Two virtual calls to one slot with different arg counts (`GetData()` then `InsertText(GetData(), n, 1)`) are the arg-eval-order trick: right-to-left pushes happen before the nullary call and the callee's `ret` leaves them for the next call. (4) A module can mix fp modes: `(int)(float)(unsigned)` emitting `fstp [m]; cvttss2si eax,[m]` needs /fp:precise-style spill while a float upper_bound uses comiss; per-line manifest flags fix it, `#pragma float_control` did not.
  Fix idea: ship a generator (tools/matching/vslots.py) that emits the placeholder-virtual class text from a {offset: decl} map; let chk.py take a per-VA flags list. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00964190 + s00919620] codegen — (1) A setter that stores a field then runs `if (!mbRefresh) { mbRefresh = true; GetWindow()->SetFlag(8,1); }` only matches (store first, cmp after) when the flag is read through the ADJUSTED full-object pointer, e.g. `char* wb = (char*)this - 0x20c; if (wb[0x210] == 0) { wb[0x210] = 1; ((IWinVt*)(wb+4))->SetFlag(8,1); }`. Reading it as `this->flag` makes cl hoist the cmp above the store (6 setters + SerUpdate fixed). (2) `x ^ y` vs `!=`: `if ((a & 1) ^ cur)` gives `xor; je` where `!=` gives `cmp`. (3) A virtual call whose argument contains another call keeps the vptr in a register only if written `void** vt = *(void***)obj; ((fn)vt[N/4])(obj, a, b, call())`; the plain `obj->f(call())` form folds `vptr+N` early. (4) A by-value 8-byte struct param that is modified and re-passed needs a user copy ctor (`Point2(const Point2& o){x=o.x;y=o.y;}`) to get `sub esp,8; mov eax,esp; stores`. (5) `bool` second params (not uint32) give `sete al; movzx`; an address-taken local (`&ext`) must be copied to a plain local (`const wchar_t* e = ext;`) to stay in esi across calls. (6) `uint32_t i` vs `uint64_t i` for a loop counter compared against a u64 gives `inc ebp` with a constant-zero high compare. Fix idea: add these to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00f88eb0/s00d98140/s00c163f0] codegen/tooling — In big /O2 functions the original inlines the same small helper at some call sites and calls an out-of-line COMDAT copy at others (Transform ctor / Matrix3 copy 0x41cb40 and 0x434040, hashtable::begin 0x594410 vs an inline begin in the same function), so one inline body cannot reproduce the call sequence; I used a declared-only member for the out-of-line sites and a tagged __forceinline twin for the inlined ones. Plain `inline` helpers (RotateVector, NewTrack, Transform ctor) were left out of line by cl in a 4 KB function and the size fell to 0.66x until marked __forceinline. A reusable evidence script (size, ordered call targets mapped by `name=va`, vcall slots, float constants by value) is work/match/scratch_s00f88eb0_evid.py.
  Fix idea: ship that evidence script as tools/matching/evid.py; add the inline-budget note to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op2_big s004ebe20] codegen — /Od frames: od_names fit was not needed. Compile once with neutral names, read each role's slot by aligning orig/mine ebp offsets (instruction streams identical), then hand each role the name that currently sits at that role's original slot (order depends on the name SET, not on which role carries it); 15-local scope matched in 3 compiles. Also: at /Od /Ob1 an EASTL bitset::set(i, bool value=true) is NOT inlined unless __forceinline, and the `lea; mov [tmp]` address temp comes from an inline DoGetWord() returning uint32_t&. Unused /Od hole blocks (0x44 after each GetPropertyAsKey arg) reproduced with ScratchSlots<17>.
  Fix idea: ship the align-and-reassign helper (work/match/scratch_s004ebe20_slots.py + _gen.py) as tools/matching/od_assign.py. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00a158e0/s00a26e10] tooling/codegen — (1) chk.py maps VAs to symbols by size, so with differently-named functions the rows show wrong symbols/diffs; I needed my own loop over cmpdis (obj = newest work/match/chk/<slice>_*.obj; `ls -t work/match/chk | head -1` picks other agents' objects). (2) Flags for this audio region: `/O2 /GS- /arch:SSE /fp:fast` (not SSE2: `(int)(float/uint)` goes through the _ftol2 call only under /arch:SSE; `x * 0.01f` needs /fp:fast to avoid cvtps2pd). A ctor with an EH frame and a 6-element eh_vector ctor needs a base class with a virtual dtor and `__declspec(noinline)` ctor; `new (p) T()` alone (no `if (p)`) reproduces the stores-before-null-test frame. (3) a scalar deleting dtor with only a base vtable store means the body lives in an inline member dtor (FixedBuf with ~FixedBuf), derived class has an out-of-line empty ctor to force the vtable.
  Fix idea: chk.py `--map sym=va` option and print obj path. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [op2_big s00584300/s004d92b0] codegen — (1) Under /O2 ANY volatile access in a function (even one inlined `*(volatile int*)&mnRefCount += -1` in RefCountTemplate::Release) switches off cl's stack packing for the whole function: sibling-scope locals stop sharing slots and only one local reuses a dead param home (frame 0x1c instead of 0x10 in cAppModeEditorBase::Init). The volatile spelling reproduces the original `dec eax; mov [ecx+4],eax; cmp eax,ebx` exactly, but the original packs, so it was not volatile; ~25 non-volatile spellings all give `sub eax,edi` (edi = CSE'd 1, flags fused) and the residual stays. Scratch probes (work/match/scratch_s00584300_t1.cpp) show packing works for sibling `if` scopes, AutoRefCount locals and dead param homes. (2) The dev-PDB locals dump (work/match/scratch_s005b2d90_locals.py <dev-va>) gave the real local names/types (AutoRefCount pRecord, uint64 n64StoredTime) that made the packing line up; a 6-byte `#pragma pack(2)` {int; short} member assigned `= T()` gives `xor eax,eax; mov [m],eax; mov [m+4],ax`. (3) /Od: an inline getter's ternary result is a compiler temp placed after every other slot; the original's in-sequence temp comes from a named local in the helper (`bool bValue; if (..) bValue = ..; else bValue = false; return bValue;`), and block scopes `{ }` around each section reproduced the function's slot groups; slot-map scripts work/match/scratch_s004d92b0_slots.py / _score.py rank renamed copies by stack offsets per instruction.
  Fix idea: put the volatile-kills-packing note and the slot-map/score scripts into docs/matching.md and tools/. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s0098e3a0/s009d29b0] codegen — two levers re-discovered. (1) A tag/comparator struct passed by value (eastl upper_bound `YPosComparator`) must have NO user ctor: with `X() {}` cl stores nothing, the original stores `mov byte [slot],0` (matches the playbook note but is easy to get backwards for comparators). (2) When the original re-reads a member after a bounds check or a size computation (`mov eax,[esi+0x70]` twice) instead of CSE'ing it, wrap the second read in `template<class T> T Reload(const T& v){ return *(const volatile T*)&v; }`: this fixed 4 accessors and ClassifyLimbs in one try each. Also: EAText OTF code has a vendored reference (work/ext/EAWebKitSupportPackages/EATextEAWebKit/.../EATextOpenType.cpp): written verbatim with the real inline create_array/StackAllocator::Malloc and same-TU OTF::ReadUint16 bodies, cl reproduced every inline/out-of-line choice of the original (65/65 calls).

Also: x87 Havok code passes bit-exact only if the source rounds to float exactly where the original spills (named hkReal local = rounded under /fp:precise, sub-expression = extended); /fp:fast compiles of the same math failed on denormal/overflow inputs. - [op3_mid s010f9f30] tooling — difftest/equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED for Havok code: a template callee (`??$quickSortRecursive@...`) is rejected as "special member" even with a `// 0x...` annotation (had to redeclare it as a plain function), and then every input faults on the TlsGetValue-based hkThreadMemory/monitor-stream reads (0/100 valid). Also: an `__declspec(align(16))` member in a class whose first base has the vfptr pads the vfptr to 16 bytes, so `vfptr + uint32 pad[3]` puts the first hkVector4 at +0x20, not +0x10; derive from an unaligned base holding the vfptr instead. - [s007b7ac0] codegen — under /fp:fast, cl 2008 reassociates `360.0f / n * g_Pi * (1/180.f)` (any spelling: /180, inline helper, split statements) into `2*pi/n` or `(k/n)*pi*360`, while the original keeps `((360/n)*pi)*k` with both literals loaded. A 1/180 float literal can't be kept in order; an extern for the literal-pool slot preserves the operands but not the order. Fix idea: note in matching.md that constant-pair folding under /fp:fast is not reproducible from plain source (count it as nonmatching, check value equivalence). Time lost: ~10 minutes.

(2) 0x0116xxxx is EA's RW-audio Layer-3 decoder (mpg123-style dct36/dct32 with doubled COS9 constants, 32x18 hybrid buffers); s01164200's "terrain brush 32x18 planes" is probably that decoder's hybrid/overlap code. (3) In the /arch:SSE mesh module, Vector3 needs a user copy ctor AND operator= to get movss copies instead of int-register copies (frame 0x11c -> 0xe8), and `vector.resize(0)` (not erase(begin,end)) reproduces the original's unfolded `cmp eax,eax` copy loop.

Also in /O2 code, an intrusive_ptr `operator=(T*)` the original calls out of line (/Od COMDAT 0x4b09b0) while inlining the same template elsewhere needs a declared-only operator= plus an inline `reset()` for the inlined sites. - [s0048d010] codegen — At /Od /Ob1, cl declined (emitted out of line) every plain in-class inline that itself calls another inline (hkVector4::setInterpolate4 using operator(), hkTransform copy using hkVector4 copy), even in a 3-line test function; the original had them inlined, so Havok's HK_FORCE_INLINE (__forceinline) is needed on the hkVector4/hkTransform helpers. Forceinlined bodies then do NOT get the param/this slots that /Ob1 inlining gives when the argument is a plain local (setInterpolate4's t/this slots stayed missing). Also: Havok compiler-generated copy ctors (hkRootCdPoint 0x48dc00) match byte-exact as implicit copy ctors. Fix idea: add a Havok-at-/Od note to docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s01073700] codegen — The documented RefCount release spelling `int n = (*(volatile int*)&mnRefCount += -1);` (load / add -1 / store) switched off /O2 stack-slot sharing for the whole function: frame grew 0x48 -> 0x9c in a 3.2 KB cSPUISpace::Init. Non-volatile spellings (`n = x + -1; x = n;`, `--x`, `x += -1`) either fuse to `add [m],-1` or become `sub r,1`/`sub r,ebx`; none gave `add eax,-1`. Left as a 1-byte-shift nonmatching. Also: an extra `{ }` scope around a late ResourceKey local was what let it share a slot with earlier temporaries (frame 0x54 -> 0x48). Fix idea: note in docs that volatile can cost frame packing in large functions; keep looking for a non-volatile spelling. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00fab2b0/s00f5e910] codegen — in 2.7 KB /O2 /arch:SSE functions cl declined to inline plain `inline` helpers (a 330-byte cube-face projection, an x87 RandomRange clamp used 7x) and emitted them out of line with calls, so the first build was 0.85x size with extra calls; `__forceinline` on the helpers restored the original shape (calls 19/19, size 0.997x). A static helper whose original receives args in eax/ecx with a plain `ret` (0x00fab2b0) gets the same register convention from cl only when the TU also contains a caller (add a stand-in caller function); changing the body (e.g. a Vector3 user copy ctor) can flip which args go to registers. Fix idea: mention both in the Batch-2 /O2 levers. Time lost: ~20 minutes.

Also: the earlier scratch call-sequence scripts stop at the first `ret`, so early-return functions look truncated; work/match/scratch_s00d1a2b0_calls.py does a linear sweep of the whole body.
