(2) `shr r,n; test r8,1` needs the inline helper to return bool (a uint return folds to `test [m],1<<n`). (3) wstring length tests: `length() > 0` gives `sar;jne`, `length() != 0` gives `test r,0xfffffffe`. (4) A function-local helper class's members (variant_helper ctor, InitChannel) get custom register conventions; declare them plainly and say so. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [op3_big s12-14] tooling — difftest call trace misses the first `call dword ptr [reg+N]` through a fake vtable that is not mapped yet (_eval_target reads the slot before the fault repair, gets None, records nothing), while our `mov r,[reg+N]; call r` is recorded, so prebuilt-Havok functions (memory-indirect vcalls, e.g. 0x0110b3a0 removeContactPoint) FAIL on "call #2" with identical behaviour; a scratch wrapper that drops non-.text (fake) targets on both sides gives 349 valid / 0 mismatches. Also: resolve.py's "symbols" step beats a correct `// 0x...` annotation when the qualified name exists in pdb_names.json at another address (cSPTimer::Stop 0xbc3110 vs the 0xbc3170 I meant), check resolution_conflicts. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [op3_big s007507e0] tooling — difftest (equiv.py) never sets the x87 control word, so the original runs at Unicorn's default 64-bit precision, not the 24-bit (0x007F) state docs/floating_point.md describes; an /arch:SSE2 build (SSE float sums) then FAILs by 1 ulp against the original's x87 extended-precision sums (lodDist = dist*scale), while /arch:SSE /fp:fast (inline x87 fsqrt on the same expression) PASSes. Also: a void extern "C" function gets its eax compared (no decorated signature), so give the function C++ linkage. Fix idea: set FPCW per docs (or add --fpcw), and treat undecorated functions as void unless asked. Time lost: ~15 minutes.

Writing the callee static plus its only caller (0x01203ee0) in the slice reproduces a register ABI, but cl picks edi for `out` (the register the caller happens to keep it in), so the ABI depends on the caller's register allocation; a float member passed to a 4-byte POD struct param (`Real`) gives the original's integer push. (2) cmpdis.py prints our unrelocated call targets as `<+0x..>`, so every call line diffs; a copy that prints all call/jump targets as `<T>` made the 3.5 KB diffs readable (work/match/scratch_s00e35370_cmpdis.py). (3) MSVC block layout: an early-exit `if (bad) { ...; return false; }` whose code sits at the END of the original means the source was `if (!bad) { big } else-path`; reshaping three such spots made the 51-entry vtable-slot sequence of UpdateCursorStrategy identical.

(2) Named-local order for a 19-local scope: a flat int-local emulation in the SAME declaration order reproduced the real function's order exactly, so a fixed-point search (compile 120 random name sets, reassign each role to the name found at its wanted slot, recompile; converges in 1-2 rounds) found a working set in one minute: work/match/scratch_s00491b40_odsearch.py. Holes after each `push_back(AutoRefCount(x))` (2 dwords) and before the final dtors (16 dwords) came from the slot-mapping script work/match/scratch_s00491b40_map.py (aligns orig/mine streams, prints ebp-slot -> slot). Fix idea: promote both scripts into tools/matching (od_names.py search, slotmap.py). Time lost: ~30 minutes.

- [op3_mid s00587a20/s005bb5a0] codegen — (1) A field store that precedes the base-class vtable/ctor stores (message ID at msg+0x30 stored before the IMessageRC base vptr + `xchg` refcount) comes from a NON-polymorphic base class: MSVC lays the polymorphic base at +0 but constructs bases in declaration order, so `class Msg : public cHeader /*holds the ID*/, public cBehaviorMessage` reproduced it (Deactivate went 17 -> 0 diff). The same message's frame needed one extra trailing dword (size 0x40). (2) `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` keeps sums in x87 when their first consumer is x87 (fabs/fsqrt), which skips the float rounding the original does; computing a value that is also used by SSE first (`float rise = hitTop - minHeight;` before `fabs(a - b) < tol`) or splitting `LengthSquared()`/`Length()` helpers restores the SSE-sum + spill + fld shape. (3) `--*(volatile int*)&mnRefCount` gives the `mov; dec; mov` release (plain `--n` gives `add [m],-1` with -1 hoisted into a register).
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00bc0740] codegen — in /arch:SSE /fp:fast code, `movss [esp+N],xmm; cvttss2si eax,[esp+N]` with a different slot per conversion is a truncating asm helper (`__forceinline int TruncToInt(float f){ __asm cvttss2si eax, f }`), the cvttss2si twin of RoundToInt; a plain (int) cast converts from the register. Also: Vector3 math came out x87 until Vector3 got a user copy ctor (movss loads) and the delta was written `b - pos` (operands already in xmm regs). /fp:precise turns sqrtf into a __CIsqrt call, so an inline fsqrt means /fp:fast.
  Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons next to RoundToInt. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s010ef580/s00ff7530] codegen/tooling — (1) difftest runs the x87 at 64-bit precision, so a Havok x87 port built with /fp:fast FAILs by 1-2 ulp wherever the original stored a float to a stack slot and read it back (cl 15 /fp:fast keeps it on the x87 stack); /fp:precise rounds but turns sqrt into `_CIsqrt`, which equiv.py reports as an extra import call. What passed (202 valid, 62.8%): /fp:fast plus `__forceinline float hkStoreF(double v){ volatile float f=(float)v; return f; }` at every original store-and-reload, and `double` locals for values the original keeps on the x87 stack. (2) A virtual call whose do-nothing fake leaves an out-struct (hkConstraintMotorOutput) unwritten makes equiv compare uninitialized stack garbage (orig slot vs our 0xcccc), a spurious FAIL; `--no-fake-calls` isolates the rest. (3) Under /fp:fast `if (time < e) a=0; else a=1;` / `!(time < e)` flip to `comiss e,time; ja/jbe` (NaN side swapped); `if (time >= e)` kept the original `comiss time,e; jb`.
  Fix idea: have equiv.py treat _CIsqrt/_CIsin as inline (not import) calls and offer an `--fpcw 0x7f` option (the game's 24-bit precision). Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00ca5b10] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" when the compiled function is outside the 1/8 size window (e.g. a helper not inlined), with no size shown; and cmpdis.py leaves our relocated operands as `[0]`/`push 0` but the original's as `<addr>`, so every constant load shows as a diff and drowns structural differences. Fix idea: chk.py should print the nearest symbol's size when nothing fits; cmpdis should normalize relocated operands on both sides to the same token (work/match/scratch_s00ca5b10_cmp.py does this, plus a LOOSE mode that masks esp offsets and xmm numbers to show only structural diffs). Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00b42760/s01205c00] tooling — difftest resolve.py only maps external (COFF class 2) data symbols, but a function-local static of a non-inline function (`?x@?4??Fn@@..@4..`) and its guard (`?$S1@..`) are class 3, so they load from our object and the original's static init writes show up as a .data mismatch even with exact mangled names in symbols/slices. Workaround: put the static in a `__forceinline` helper (its static and guard `??_B?1??Helper@@..@51` become external COMDAT symbols and resolve from symbols/slices). Also: when a real callee leaves an out-param unwritten on garbage input (GetCenter for an unknown shape kind), the result depends on stale stack data, so cl packing that local into a dead temporary's slot FAILs the test; filling the earlier struct component-wise (no temporary) fixed it. Fix idea: let resolve.py map class-3 data symbols named in symbols/ (or by `// 0x` annotation), and have equiv report "unwritten out-param read" separately. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [op3_mid s008ce020/s00d79470] tooling/codegen — (1) cmpdis.py disassembles the original only up to len(mine), so when sizes differ the tail is cut or padded and the diff is misleading; a register- and stack-offset-normalised whole-function score (difflib over both full lengths, `call` targets masked) made variant ranking reliable (work/match/scratch_s008ce020_cmp.py + scratch_s008ce020_var.py batch-apply variants). (2) cl canonicalises `(f & 0x100) >> 8`, `!= 0`, `?1:0`, `/256` to the same `shr;and 1` for any unsigned/int flags; the original's `and 0x100; shr 8` came only from an `int16 flags` with `(flags & 0x100) != 0` (found by a 40-variant grid). (3) A static T2K function taking mem in ECX and the stream in EAX is reproduced byte-exact by a `static` definition plus a same-TU stand-in caller (no /GL needed). (4) T2K's ReadUnsignedByteMacro needs its inner arms typed `int` (slow reader returns int, cached arm cast to int) to get `movzx eax,byte` + outer (uint8).
  Fix idea: give cmpdis an optional original size (from the function index) and a --norm mode. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00fc03c0/s01038410] tooling — the shared evidence script (work/match/scratch_*_evid.py) resolves my callees by NAME from `// 0x...` comments, so overloads (three `SetDestination`s) collapse to one address and show as fake call-sequence diffs; also VC9 compiles `operator delete[](p)` to `??3@YAXPAX@Z`, which no comment can map. Give overloads distinct names (or pass `'??3@=<va>'` as the extra map). Codegen: a 2.8 KB "reset to defaults" function dropped from 1230 to 351 diff bytes once the scalar/vector member stores were written in member-offset order (Vector4 first), plus a user copy ctor on Vector3 (movss copy in a Transform ctor) and an UNinitialized float local whose slot happens to hold a stale 0.1f temp (the original reads it).
  Fix idea: add an `--map name=va` default for operator new/delete and make the evid script resolve by mangled signature. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00b38e50] tooling — the per-agent call-compare scripts (work/match/scratch_*_calls.py) print virtual calls as `ind:r`, so a wrong placeholder-virtual count (3 wrong slots here: 0x2c/0x24/0xac came out 0x30/0x34/0xb4) still shows a "55/55 identical" call sequence. Resolving `call reg` through the preceding `mov reg,[reg+off]` caught them (see work/match/scratch_s0111b170_calls.py). Also, in /arch:SSE modules, sqrtf compiles to a `_CIsqrt` call unless /fp:fast is on; the inline fsqrt in the original means /fp:fast. Fix idea: a shared tools/matching/callseq.py that prints direct targets plus vtable slot offsets for both sides. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s009b2340] codegen — a sparse `switch` whose original compare tree splits one case early (e.g. `cmp 0xc; ja; je; test eax,eax; je end`) needs an explicit `case 0:` with a body cl cannot merge into the exit; a plain `case 0: break;` is dropped and the tree re-splits at the median. `case 0: bReject = bReject; break;` (flag not known-false) reproduced it. Also: comparisons that branch `jae skip` (comiss/fcomp) are negated accept tests, so write `if (!(x >= -r)) reject` rather than `if (x < -r)`. Fix idea: add both to the Batch-2 /O2 levers. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00ade8b0/s00ba0770] tooling — cmpdis.py prints relocated operands in our object as their addend (`[4]`, `[0xc]`, `mov eax, 4`, `[eax*4]`, `push 0`) but as `<addr>` on the original side, so on functions that touch globals at nonzero offsets most of the diff is noise. I wrapped it with work/match/scratch_s00ade8b0_norm.py (maps every absolute/addend operand and call target to R, then re-diffs); work/match/scratch_s00ba0770_evid.py compares ordered direct-call targets (reloc symbol names vs original addresses), vtable slots and constants for the completeness evidence.
  Fix idea: have cmpdis mark operands covered by a COFF relocation as `<addr>` (and fold them into the normalization), and ship the evid script as a chk.py `--evidence` option. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s0116e4f0] codegen|tooling — Under /fp:fast our cl canonicalizes multi-term float sums (all 24 source orders of `IN(3)*c1 + s7 + IN(11)*c5 + IN(15)*c7` give the identical reassociated order), while the LTCG-built RW audio original keeps plain source order, so the IMDCT twins (0x011666d0, 0x0116e4f0) differ by a few ulp in difftest and can never match. Also equiv.py compares esp strictly, so an LTCG caller-pop register ABI approximated with __fastcall (ecx/edx + stack, `ret 4` vs `ret`) FAILs on esp alone; I used a scratch wrapper that tolerates exactly +4. Fix idea: add an `--ignore-callee-pop` option to equiv.py, and note "LTCG RW-audio float sums are not reassociated; /fp:fast cl output is" in Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s008cfd50/s011746c0] codegen — a function whose callees take register args (static readers in T2K truetype.c; the reflection->LPC helper in the audio LPC filter) only matched once those callees' REAL bodies were in the TU (cl picks the callee convention from its body, transitively: New_kernClass needed New_kernSubTable and New_kernSubTable0Data too). cl also keeps caller-saved regs (EDX) live across a same-TU *cdecl* callee whose body it has seen (GetNumGlyphs_sfntClass), so even cdecl helpers need their body, not an extern. Writing them went fast (small readers) and most matched their own VAs as a bonus; 2.7 KB functions then matched byte-exact. Also /fp:fast reorders sums freely: three spellings of a 12-term dot product compiled identically.
  Fix idea: card.py could list each callee's entry-register usage (eax/ebx/edi read before write) so agents see up front which callee bodies they must include. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00e73f60/s009de8b0] codegen — thiscalls on a member of a global (`gspCellGame->mCells.Get(id)`) came out `push arg; lea ecx` while the original computes `this` first (`lea ecx,[eax+0x1c]; push edx`); an inline accessor returning the member by reference (`inline cObjectPool& Cells() { return gspCellGame->mCells; }`) flipped all 8 sites and took sUpdateGfx from 87 to 0. Other levers there: `x == 4 ? 0.0f : f()` float args become `if/else` with two calls (fldz arm), a sum of 4 array floats needs a `for` loop under /fp:fast (unrolled source gets reversed), a cdq'd temp passed as `const Vector3&` shares the Clamp asm slot (frame 0x78 -> 0x6c), `if (!a) return 0; if (!b) return 0;` gave the original's double-zero-store layout, and a const level table defined in-TU lets cl fold `level >= kTable[0].min` to `test ecx,ecx; jl`. work/match/scratch_s009de8b0_reloc.py checks a byte-exact function's relocations map one-to-one to the original's targets (vtables/strings/globals in the right order), which byte diffs can't show. Fix idea: add the accessor lever to docs/matching.md /O2 levers. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s01106490] codegen/tooling — Getting a Havok 3.1 x87 function (hkCollideCapsuleUtilManifoldCapsVsCaps) to pass equiv.py bit-exactly: plain /fp:fast failed 289/400 because the original rounds to float only where it spills (hkVector4 members, some locals) and keeps everything else at 80 bits, while /fp:precise turns every sqrt into a __CIsqrt import call (import-trace FAIL). What passed (3x400 + --nan): /fp:fast for the inline fsqrt, a `Rounded(x){ volatile float m=(float)x; return m; }` at every place the asm does fstp/fst to a dword slot and reloads, unrounded intermediates typed `double` (a cl spill then keeps 53 bits, not 24), and reading each spill point from the asm (e.g. the manifold loop's normalize3 spills |v|^2 to a float, the point-0 copy doesn't). Volatile-double "ordering" barriers against /fp:fast reassociation made it worse (53-bit partial sums), so leave the sums alone. Also: equiv.py cannot tell that an extern "C" function returns void (no mangled signature) and FAILs on eax; test a copy with C++ linkage via --src. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00c104d0/s00ff9390] tooling — card.py labels IAT thunks with wrong library names (0x011e08c2 `jmp [_CIfmod]` shows as eastl::copy_impl<...>::do_copy, 0x010829f0 as UTFWin::InteractiveWinProc::OnWinProcAdd), and the copied scratch_*_evid.py call-sequence script maps symbols to VAs by bare method name, so two stub methods named Refresh/Update/ctor-class-name silently resolve to the wrong address until renamed. Fix idea: card.py should prefer the IAT import name for `jmp [iat]` thunks; ship one shared evidence script in tools/matching that maps by full mangled name + `// 0x` comment. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00b1c8d0/s008e8af0] tooling+codegen — (1) difftest/resolve.py treats ANY `@ 0x...` in a comment as a "names the next definition" marker, including prose in the file header ("LoadStateMachine @ 0x00b1b410 registers..."): it bound `memcpy` (next declaration) to that VA and equiv.py FAILed with an import mismatch; writing "(0x00b1b410)" fixed it. (2) /O2 XHTML layout: the original's two stack copies of `bottom - top` came from writing the height once as a local (`containerHeight`) and once inline as a call argument via an inline `RectT::Height()`; cl CSEs the value but keeps both homes. Using EA RectT Width()/Height() helpers cut PlaceOutOfFlow from 2172 to 778 diff bytes; the remaining 8-byte low-slot hole (4 bytes in SetMargins) is still unexplained (not unused locals, not double rounding temps; `__declspec(align(64))` on the StackingContext local fixes its offset but inflates the frame). Fix idea: make resolve.py require `// @ 0x` at the start of a comment line directly above a definition. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00505850] codegen — an /Od function whose `fabs` shows as the x87 `fabs` instruction plus a `fstp tmp; fld tmp` round trip needs `#include <math.h>` + `/Oi` (per-line manifest flags): at /Od `#pragma intrinsic(fabs)` is ignored without /Oi (it emits `call _fabs`), and a hand-declared intrinsic fabs drops the tmp round trip. Also: a tiny probe function with the same locals (types + names, each used once) reproduces the /Od slot order of a 2.7 KB function and compiles 70 name candidates in seconds, whereas copies of the full function took minutes. Fix idea: mention both in matching.md "/Od frame layout". Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00ff6a50/s00de8ad0] tooling — cmpdis prints every call as `<ext>`/`<+off>`, so checking "same ordered call targets / vtable slots" for a 2.7 KB function needs a custom script each time. I wrote work/match/scratch_s00ff6a50_calls.py (maps our callee symbols to VAs via the `// 0x00xxxxxx` comments on their declarations, prints a difflib of calls, vcall slots and push/cmp immediates) and scratch_s00ff6a50_rdiff.py (register-normalized diff + field-offset multiset check, which caught a wrong pad after an empty member struct). Also: variant copies of a secondary-base override (`this` = sub-object at +8) must override a matching pure virtual in that base, or the copy compiles with `this` = full object and every offset shifts by 8.
  Fix idea: give cmpdis/chk a `--calls` mode with that mapping, and teach try_variants to clone a method under new names in the same base. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s005a33c0/s00f16580] codegen — two re-discovered shapes: (1) `test al,al; jns/js` after a char-returning call is `(r & 0x80) == 0`, not `r >= 0` (that gives jge/jl); (2) an EASTL fixed_vector<int64_t,N> local whose frame has `and esp,-8` needs its buffer as `__declspec(align(8)) char buffer[]` (aligned_buffer), and then /GS- or cl adds a cookie; a plain `int64_t buf[N]` gives no aligned frame. Also MSVC's `cond ? p->mIntrusivePtr : intrusive_ptr<T>()` reproduces the original's conditional-temporary flag register (bit per temp, no AddRef on the lvalue arm).
  Fix idea: add to the Batch-2 /O2 levers. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [op3_mid s00c830f0] codegen — `NounManager()->GetSphere()->mMap[key]` made cl push `&key` BEFORE the two getter calls (the original stores key, calls the getters, then pushes); putting the object chain in a local first (`S* s = NounManager()->GetSphere(); s->mMap[key]`) gives the original order. Also: a switch with 4 cases only becomes a jump table when `case 0:` is spelled out (even if it equals default), and a per-case `push N` table comes from calling inside each case. Fix idea: add both to matching.md /O2 levers. Time lost: ~15 minutes.

(2) The copied evidence script (scratch_s004655e0_evid.py) resolves callees by `// 0x...` comments with a substring regex, so a method named `Transform` captures `?TransformVector@Transform@@` and operators never resolve; pass explicit `sym=va` overrides. (3) s00522800/nonmatching.txt lists 00522a40 as "complete" but the .cpp still has an empty skeleton for it. - [s0057d710/s008be260] codegen — three things that cost rounds: (1) the editor region near 0x57d710 is `/arch:SSE`, not SSE2: under SSE2 `sqrtf` becomes `sqrtss`, while the original has x87 `fsqrt; fstp` then SSE `divss` (that `divss` needs an inline `operator/(float f){ float inv = 1.0f/f; ...}`; writing `1.0f/Length()` directly keeps the divide on x87). The small functions matched under both flag sets, so check a sqrt site before trusting a slice's existing manifest flags. (2) The temporary `T()` passed to an out-of-line `uninitialized_fill_n_impl(first, n, T(), tag)` gets a dtor call unless the helper has a real `__declspec(noinline)` body in the TU (same lesson as "same-TU helper that receives a local's address"). (3) T2K: when the stream is a member expression (`t->in`), the ReadUnsignedByteMacro slow path must also be a macro (the original re-reads `t->in` after `pos++`), and `mov cl, byte` per arm (instead of `movzx ecx, byte`) means the result goes into a `uint8` local.
  Fix idea: add (1) to the Batch-2 flags-by-region list, and the T2K macro and uint8-local notes to the s008bb550 header. Time lost: ~40 min.
- [s00464bb0/s00450f70/s00eff790] codegen/tooling — /Od local-name order is fully predictable and solvable by script: for a 21-local scope cl 15.00 used exactly 16 hash buckets (slot order = bucket rank, later-declared first within a bucket; 0 inconsistencies over 1,800 renamed copies). Method that byte-matched 0x00464bb0 (2.6 KB, 22 loop locals): compile 150 renamed copies of the real function per run (work/match/scratch_s00464bb0_gen.py), map each copy's slots to the original by aligned instructions (…_ana.py), fit bucket classes from pairwise orders (…_fit.py), then DP-assign meaningful names to roles (…_solve2.py). Also: a plain inline `T* operator->() const { return mpObject; }` reproduces the odd /Od "copy uninitialised slot into temp, then overwrite" pattern by itself, and a local AutoRefCount's null checks vanish at /O2 when cl can prove non-null (no custom smart pointer needed).
  Fix idea: turn the four scratch scripts into tools/matching/od_solve.py <src> <va> <func> (generic role extraction from the function's scope). Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00ffe860] codegen — an early `return` that the original emits as its own inline epilogue (`test edi,edi; jne cont; pop..; ret 4`) while cl 15 tail-merges it into the next branch's `je ret` (`test edi,edi; jmp shared_je`) is reproduced by `if (p) goto ok; return;` (1754 -> 52 diff bytes, all later jump offsets fixed). Also: in /arch:SSE code an `__asm` maxss/minss Clamp (params in memory) gives the original's `and esp,-16` frame and stack-slot Clamp args, while the `_mm_*` version keeps them in registers; and `float d = (call() - c) * t; x += (d + c) * dt;` is what gives the x87-then-SSE split `fsub;fmul;fstp;movss;addss` (an inline Lerp(c, call(), t) stores the call result first). Time lost: ~20 minutes.

Codegen: in big functions cl /O2 stops inlining after an inline budget, so one inline helper shows up inlined at its first N sites and as a call to an out-of-line (often /Od, COMDAT-picked) copy afterwards (GetRecorderState 0x435e90 in HandleButton, EAText Atof 0x692700 in PolygonFont::Open); write the helper once as `inline` and cl reproduces the split by itself; helpers that stay inlined everywhere in the original need EA_FORCE_INLINE. (4) Ghidra dropped whole switch cases ("Removing unreachable block") and mislabeled case bodies; decode the jump table from the binary. (5) work/devbuild/symbols.json `va` is decimal; scratch_s01025840_dev.py on it gave EA::Swarm::cParticlesEffect::ApplyEffect with all callee names.

- [op3_mid s0072b630/s004ba150] codegen — Three silent traps: (1) a function *defined* inside an `extern "C"` block under /EHsc gets no EH frame at all (cl assumes extern "C" never throws), so an EH-heavy body looked hopeless until the decl moved to C++ linkage (that also made two dispatchers byte-exact); (2) same-TU helpers that the original calls with stack args but whose callers keep ecx live across the call must be defined non-`static` before use (static ones get register args); (3) `#include <math.h>` makes `fabs(float)` pick the C++ float overload, defeating `#pragma intrinsic(fabs)` at /Od, so declare `extern "C" double fabs(double)` yourself.

- [hk1 s2-3 / 00b45ca0, 0098d630] tooling — card.py's Ghidra decompile names locals `local_N` whose N does not map to the asm `[esp+X]` offsets (N-0x44 for one slot, not for another), so struct/vector slots (e.g. the Vec3 vs Quaternion pair passed to Matrix3FromQuaternion) cannot be traced from the decompile alone; disasm.py also prints every `ret` in a window, so a callee's `ret N` cannot be read unambiguously for a convention check. Fix idea: card.py to emit the esp-offset to Ghidra-local map per function, and disasm.py to stop at the function end (or accept --one-func). Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [hk1 s00bffad0/s00ac5c00] tooling — card.py's Ghidra decompile names locals by its own frame offsets (uStack_38/uStack_7c/MStack_78) that do not match the asm [esp+N] slots, and it types the same slot as float, int* and a message in different places, so the message layout (XformMsg vs MessageBasicRC<5>) cannot be read from the decompile alone. Virtual calls show no arg count at the call site either, so a vtable slot called with 0 args in one place and 3 in another (ObjD vt+0x2c here) is only visible by counting pushes. Fix idea: card.py should print an esp-relative frame map (each [esp+N] with the callee that wrote it), and a per-call "pushes before call" count. Time lost: ~60 minutes, slices left partial.
- [hk1 s0101c780/s004c38e0] codegen — 0x00799320 (a 113-byte SSE normalize helper, returns out ptr in eax) is called 5+ times with NO pushes before the call (e.g. 0101cb5f, 0101ccc7, 00c05523) while the callee reads [esp+4]/[esp+8]; the only pushes on that path are unrelated callee-saved ebx/edi saves (shrink-wrapped after an early-exit jbe). card.py shows the no-arg call but not what the callee reads from the caller frame. Fix idea: card.py should print, for each call, the callee's [esp+N] reads and the pushes still live at the call site. Also: the existing s004c38e0 partial declares UpdatePaintedSkin(3 args) and s004c3230 declares UpdateTorso(1 arg), but the call sites pass 0 and 2 dwords (ret 0 / ret 8). Fix idea: have the decl check compare each callee's `ret N` against the call-site push count. Time lost: ~60 minutes, both slices left partial.

- hk1 s00aa4a10 (0x00aa4e50, cParticleEffectCommand::OnRegister): a placement `operator delete(void*, const char*, int, unsigned, const char*, int)` declaration next to the `new (...)` placement form makes cl emit EH funclets (`__unwindfunclet$...`) and a C4291 warning, none of which the original has. The function came out 16 bytes-to-hundreds off with no symbol of similar size. Dropping the placement delete declaration fixed it and the slice went byte-exact. Fix idea: the match template should not declare placement delete for ArgScript `new (name, 0, 0, 0, 0)` call sites. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- hk1 s0079bfa0 (0x0079bfa0): card.py prints `mov` operands with padding (`mov    ecx, esi`), so naive rg patterns such as `mov ecx, esi` match nothing and `mov +ecx,` is needed. Fix idea: normalize whitespace in the card's asm column so one literal pattern works for every instruction. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [hk1 s00c982a0/s009f2970] docs — two ~2.4 KB functions did not fit one agent's context: card.py prints ~550-730 lines per function (full asm plus Ghidra), so reading the whole thing before writing source exhausted the budget, and the second function's IK solver (~700 asm lines of SSE quaternion math) was left as a signature-only partial. Also: `nullptr` is not available in the MSVC 2008 target (C2065), and macOS `sed -i` needs an explicit suffix (`sed -i ''`). Fix idea: have slice_info.py flag functions over ~2 KB so they are split across agents (or give them ~1.5 KB slices), and have card.py take a --range of asm lines. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [hk1 s0116efb0/s00e1bbc0] codegen+tooling — (1) A struct wrapper `struct F4 { __m128 v; }` returned by value from operators is returned through memory on MSVC x86, which added 309 movs and took the IMDCT from 2245 to 3758 bytes; `typedef __m128 F4;` with inline operators taking `const F4&` returns in XMM0 and matched the original's size (2245 vs 2478). (2) By-value `__m128` or aligned-struct parameters give C2719 ("formal parameter with __declspec(align('16')) won't be aligned"); pass const references. (3) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED ("no marker/name for 0x...") unless the definition has a `// @ 0x<va>` line above it. (4) equiv.py gives WEAK (1.9% coverage, 245 valid inputs) for functions that take a pointer graph (cUIMissionButton plus layout objects) because random inputs do not build a valid object graph; the slice stays unverified. (5) On macOS, `sed -i 'expr'` fails with "invalid command code m"; use `sed -i '' -e 'expr'`. Fix idea: have equiv.py print the marker hint and a "pointer-graph input" warning, and have the template warn on by-value __m128 parameters. Time lost: ~45 minutes.
- equiv.py on space-game slices: (a) names come only from a "// Class::Fn @ 0x<va>" marker, and without it it reports UNSUPPORTED; (b) random inputs reached 15/718 insns for 00bfa660 (the entry checks read this+0x8a and NounManager/player, which the generator does not set up), so the verdict is WEAK even at 3000 inputs. PDB cSPSimulatorSpaceGame (size 0xf0) layout differs from retail offsets used by the asm, so offsets must come from the card, not the PDB. chk.py takes the slice id form ok; the flags needed /EHsc /TP.
- [hk1 s00ebc2f0/s00b55680] docs — the slice dirs for hk1 slices 28-29 did not exist (no match/slices/<id>/ at all), and the 00ebc2f0 `pdb_candidates.json` name (`cModelTestCheat::BakeTest`) is caller-scored with no evidence. The 2436-byte functions mix SSE/x87 with Havok calls and a vector at [esp+0x110] whose slot [esp+0x5c] is read before any write, so a faithful C++ translation needed far more context than one pass. Fix idea: have the orchestrator create slice dirs when it freezes a batch, and give hk-batch prompts a per-function asm-to-source checklist for the x87 blocks. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00da3b90/s00a95d30/s00dd4b20] tooling — equiv.py gives 0/400 valid inputs (UNSUPPORTED) whenever the function's first step is a REAL callee fed a pointer-soup object (FindWindowByID on this+0xc, vtable call returning a position pointer): callees run for real, and fake-method returns are not dereferenceable 75% of the time. Also an unnamed tiny function (0xd9b2b0, a sort comparator) is rejected as "annotation-implausible" because it is not in the start set; defining it locally in the slice worked. chk.py's newest-obj-in-work/match/chk is shared with other agents, so "ls -t | head -1" picks someone else's object (filter by the slice id prefix).
  Fix idea: let fake-method returns be valid pointers to zeroed/soup objects when the next instruction dereferences them, and let equiv.py accept a // 0x annotation for any in-text address. Time lost: ~25 min.
- [hk1 s01170630/s01168520] docs/codegen — 0x01170630 (MmxDct32) and 0x011689e0 (Snd::Dct32) are the same 2441-byte MMX 32-point DCT with only the two coefficient-table addresses different (identical 512-s16 values at 0x015c4638 and 0x015c41f8). Both read 32 int16 lanes from arg 3 (movq/pmaddwd/psubw), but the sibling prototypes (s01169370 Dct32, s01170fc0 MmxDct32) declare `const float*`, which changes the mangled name and the input type. Fix idea: correct those prototypes to `const s16*` (or `const short*`) in their slices and mention the shared routine in matching.md. chk.py also prints "(no symbol of similar size)" with no diff count when a compiled MMX function is 1.24x the original size, so the size filter hides the near-miss. Intrinsics __m64 locals add an `and esp,-8` frame; int64 staging buffers gave an identical object. Time lost: ~20 min.

## s00e6a3f0 (sResolvePartCollision, 00e6a3f0)
- Usercall FUN_00e52960 (reads ESI, no stack args) is not expressible in pure C++. The slice uses a 3-line `__asm` shim (`Usercall_e52960`), which is outside the allow-list (fnstcw/fldcw/cvtss2si). Needs orchestrator review.
- `sqrt` compiles to a `_CIsqrt` call under default flags. The original uses an inline `fsqrt`, so the slice needs `/fp:fast`. Consider a per-slice flags field in the manifest.
- MSVC tail-merges identical emit blocks, so the direct call count is 34 vs 42 in the original. Call-count ratio checks need to allow for this.
- equiv.py gives WEAK coverage (36.7%) for random pointer inputs, so the 0-mismatch result is weak evidence.

## s009a69d0 (009a6e60)
- Not started in source. Partial only. No new tool issues found.
- [s00dc18e0] docs — Ghidra's `param_1` for a thiscall-looking method can be a first stack argument: the 00dc1d70 epilogue read `this` from `[esp+0x94]` with plain `ret`, so it is `__cdecl` member, not thiscall. The Ghidra signature misled the first draft (equiv then failed on esp: orig +0x4 vs ours +0x20). Check the prologue load of `this` before choosing the convention.
- [s00dc18e0] tooling — equiv.py compares the full eax for a non-bool return. A function that returns bool in al (xor al,al) with stale upper eax failed as FAIL on eax (orig 43bd0000 vs ours 0). Declaring the return type as `bool` fixed it, but the card does not say to do that.

## s00d522c0 (00d522c0) and s004af210 (004af260)
- 00d522c0 (SP::cSPDramaManager::Update, 2457 bytes): full body written from the asm card and compiles under the slice flags. Not byte-exact. Left in partial.txt, not nonmatching, because equiv.py reports WEAK coverage (4.1%, need 60%): the mode gate and avatar/stalker preconditions keep most instructions unreached. Needs input shaping before equiv can verify it.
- [s00d522c0] tooling — equiv.py resolves callees by FUN_<va> or mangled names only. A callee declared as a named C function (for example `_PosseInstance`) fails with "unresolved reference", so declare callees as FUN_<va> to get past resolution.
- [s00d522c0] tooling — equiv.py finds the function by the name it is given. A function named `F522c0` gave "no marker/name ... matched", so name the scratch function FUN_<va> to match symbols/slices.
- [s00d522c0] docs — a uint64 stack argument at [esp+4] with the high dword at [esp+8] is the `fild` masked-high-dword pattern: the asm does (hi&0x7fffffff)<<32|lo plus 2^63 for the sign bit, which is plain unsigned 64-bit to float. Write it as `(float)t * 0.001f` on `unsigned long long`.
- [s00d522c0] tooling — on macOS, `sed -i 's/…/…/' file` treats the script as the backup suffix and fails with "No such file". Use python for in-place edits in this repo's scratch work.
- s004af210 (SaveResource, 2446 bytes): not started beyond reading the first 330 card lines. Partial stays as is.
- [s00978df0/s00eb5480/s010d18c0] tooling — cmpdis.py picks the first symbol whose name contains the substring and silently shows a different function (a stale/incorrect 2-line "mine" for `getPenetrations`, where `staticGetPenetrations` has a capital G) and its `ret` early-stop truncates multi-return functions; the call-evidence script copied from s00439110 also needs a real `ret` guard. Also: equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED (0 valid inputs) for pointer-chasing container code (rbtree/SparseMatrix), and `--json` gives only args as ints in a pickle, no readable input dump.
  Fix idea: make cmpdis/chk accept an exact mangled name and print the full normalized diff without the early ret stop; let equiv.py take a prebuilt memory image (e.g. a synthetic SparseMatrix) for container code. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00d964a0/s00ebdc20/s010d0920] docs — three traps when reading stack offsets in x86 cards: (1) arguments of a later call are pushed BEFORE a no-argument call used as one of its arguments (`push &x; push &y; push 1; mov ecx,this; call [vt+0x4c]; push eax; push ecx; ...; call [vt+0x18]` is `t->f18(mag, c->f4c(), 1, &y, &x)`, not a 3-arg f4c); the tell is that `[esp+N]` loads after the call only make sense if the pushes are still outstanding. (2) `push ecx` followed later by `fstp [esp]` reserves a slot for a float argument, not an argument value. (3) chk.py prints "no symbol of similar size" with no diff count when ours is outside its size filter (e.g. 0.83x), so use cmpobj.py <image> <obj> <symbol> <va> --quiet for the byte count and tools/matching cmpdis for the diff.
  Fix idea: note (1) and (2) under "Calling conventions" in docs/matching.md; let chk.py print the best symbol's byte count even when the size ratio is outside its filter. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [cl2 s00eb73f0/s00d2f940/s00d27a70] codegen+tooling — (1) `/FAs` works through cl.sh (`tools/matching/cl.sh /nologo /c /O2 ... /FAs /Fa<x>.asm x.cpp`), so a 20-line scratch TU shows in seconds which spelling gives the original's x87/SSE mix; the SSE-clamp question was settled that way: `if (x <= lo) x = lo` gives comiss, only the `__forceinline` `__asm {movss; maxss; minss}` Clamp helper gives maxss/minss with memory operands, and `acos`/`fabs` need `#pragma intrinsic` plus `(float)acos((double)d)` for a direct `_CIacos`. A float computed straight into that helper (dot product) automatically comes out as fld/fmul/faddp, a float multiplied by `(float)acos(...)` spills dword not qword. (2) A function with an inline fixed_vector local (1 KB buffer, dtor `if (mpBegin && mpBegin[-1]) operator_delete__`) and no fs:[0] frame / cookie needs `/GS-` and NO `/EHsc`; with /EHsc the same source adds an SEH frame and a +0x24 stack, so the flags of the neighbouring slice (`/EHsc`) were wrong for it. (3) The equivalence tester resolves a global only when each `extern` has its own `// 0x...` comment (a shared `extern float a, b, c; // 0x1/2/3` leaves b, c unresolved = UNSUPPORTED), and it needs a `// @ 0x<va>` marker before a function inside an anonymous namespace. (4) Message-id switches and `Instance()`-singleton code give the equivalence tester ~5% coverage (WEAK), because ids and singleton fields are not generated; calls to `Instance()+0x68` style holders read as `add eax,0x68; cmp [eax],0` = a smart-pointer-like member with inline operator->. (5) Ghidra mislabels `push a; push b; call getter; mov ecx,eax; call method` as getter(a,b); the pushes belong to the following thiscall (getter is `mov eax,[g]; ret`). Fix idea: fold (1)-(3) into docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00be88d0/s013894f0/s01353d90 cl2] codegen — (1) 25-record `Simulator::Attribute` table initializers (60-byte temp built on the stack, `rep movsd` into the global; $E-style functions at 0x0138a710 and 0x01353d90) matched byte-exact only after giving each type group (u64, double, bool, short, key, extents) its OWN extern callback symbols: retail ICF folded identical template instances to one address (0xb1fbf0 serves every group), but in the .obj they are distinct symbols, and cl's constant hoisting (edx/ebp/ebx) counts uses per symbol. Using one symbol per address hoisted the wrong constants (759 diff bytes -> 0). A real array initializer `Attribute a[] = {Attribute(..)...}` is instead constant-folded into .data (no code), so write the elements as `gTable[i] = Attribute(...)` in a function, ctor stores in the order name,id,0,0,DefaultOffset,fns...,offset(last). (2) A call whose address matches no object-visible symbol is not a bug: equiv.py is UNSUPPORTED (0/400 valid) for functions that walk singletons (NounManager() etc. are null in the emulator), so evidence has to come from call-sequence/vcall isomorphism (scratch_s00be88d0_seq.py style). (3) cmpdis hunk summaries: normalising registers/offsets and diffing with difflib shows structural vs register-only differences in seconds. Fix idea: add (1) to Batch-2 lessons; ship the isomorphism script as tools/matching/callseq.py. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s0046f2a0/s007bced0/s01315a50 cl2] codegen — five levers found while matching (one Attribute table went byte-exact, DumpZPR 1159 -> 28 diff bytes). (1) /Od inline `strlen` loops (`mov al,[p]; mov [c],al; add [p],1; cmp byte [c],0`) are the `/Oi` strlen intrinsic expansion: add `/Oi` and call `strlen()` with `#pragma intrinsic(strlen)`; no hand-written loop reproduces it. (2) An /Od object of 8+ bytes is 8-aligned unless its type says otherwise: a 0x58-byte fixed_vector local that the original placed at a 4-aligned slot needs `__declspec(align(4))` on the struct (found by seeing B at -0xd8 vs -0xd4 independent of size/name). (3) The dev PDB (work/match/scratch_s005b2d90_locals.py DumpZPR) has the real local names of the editor functions, and the dev names reproduce the /Od hash slot order almost exactly (15 of 17 locals of DumpZPR_M0 fit; only the two retail-only locals need a name search with a probe that compiles `volatile int` locals, e.g. work/match/scratch_s0046f2a0_probe.py). (4) Attribute-table $E functions: which callbacks cl caches in ebx/ebp follows symbol sharing exactly (groups of rows share Write4<T>/ReadText only when they have the same C++ type); and an Attribute ctor that stores a function-local-static default (`GetDefaultValue<T>() = v`, smart-pointer null assign with atexit) must be `__forceinline` or cl calls it out of line. (5) The second Attribute ctor ("field_name" sentinel at table slot N) is built in place with placement new, not via the rep-movsd temp.
  Fix idea: add (1) and (2) to the /Od section of docs/matching.md; make od_names.py accept arbitrary names so it can fit retail-only locals. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00815dd0/s00fb0e00/s01175310] tooling/docs — (1) chk.py prints only the LAST 3000 chars of the compiler output on COMPILE FAIL, so with many C4291/C4996 warnings the actual error line is cut off; I used a 6-line wrapper around cl.sh (work/match/scratch_<id>_cc.py) that filters warnings. (2) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED for externs it cannot name: free functions must be `FUN_00abcdef` / `Fn..abcdef` or carry a `// 0x00abcdef` comment on the SAME line as the declaration (a comment on the line above, or one with trailing text like "(cTerrainMap<unsigned short>)", is ignored); class ctors/methods need that comment per declaration, and a vtable built by an inline ctor with undefined virtuals is unresolvable, so raw `*(u32*)p = 0x14188c4` vtable stores are the practical spelling. (3) A call through an IAT slot is spelled `extern "C" void* (__cdecl* _imp__memcpy)(void*, const void*, unsigned)`; `memcpy` itself is expanded inline for constant sizes and `#pragma function` turns it into an E8 call, neither is `call [iat]`. (4) A shift written `x << (c & 31)` emits a real `and ecx,31`; plain `x << c` matches the original. (5) macOS `sed -i` needs a suffix argument (BSD sed); use python for in-place edits.
      Fix idea: print the first 20 lines of compiler output in chk.py; document the same-line `// 0x` rule in docs/equivalence.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s0048e590/s00f986f0/s010e2270] tooling/codegen — (1) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED "unresolved reference" for a global reached through a class static such as `hkMemory::s_instance` (0x016E4178); declaring it as a plain `extern T* g_x; // 0x016E4178` in the slice resolves it. (2) cmpdis.py only reads len(mine)+64 original bytes, so when ours is shorter the tail of the original (e.g. a second set of stores) looks missing: compare against the card instead. (3) At /Od, `Vector3 v = a * m;` with operators returning a derived `Vector3R` (and Vector3 having an inline copy ctor) reproduces the "sret temp, then movss member copy through a stashed pointer" pattern, and `blocks[i]`-style accessors only get the original's lea+tmp slot with a real inline operator[] chain, which I could not reproduce. Fix idea: card.py could print the mangled return type of operators (cSPVector3R) to hint at this.
      Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s005816f0/s00f47ed0/s008b8f30 cl2] codegen — three reusable findings. (1) `cl.sh ... /FAs /Fa<file>.asm` lists every stack local with its frame offset (`_name$NNNN = -164`), which is the fastest way to compare a local/temp layout with the original's `[esp+N]` slots; sibling-scope locals DO share a slot, but a function with many `if`-nested address-taken locals can silently lose the sharing (not bisected). (2) eastl::basic_string is 16 bytes (3 pointers plus an empty allocator member), so five string locals sit 16 apart; without the empty-allocator member the whole frame came out 0x20 short. An inlined small-literal copy that reloads the destination (`mov edx,[K.b]`) between stores needs typed stores through `*(unsigned*)(mpBegin+4)` from a NON-folded source (extern array) with `mpEnd = p + n` and `sz = n + 1; mpCapacity = p + sz`; a memcpy of a literal folds to immediates. A static helper with a trailing `int start` parameter (always passed 0) gets it in eax, reproducing the original's `xor eax,eax; call` custom convention (FindString). (3) difftest cannot run any function that reaches SteamAPI_Init (import discards all inputs) or tsi_AllocMem (ff_malloc jumps through a null function-pointer global, int3 -> fault on every input), and it reports FAIL on uninitialised-stack reads by a callee when our local layout differs even if the frame size matches. Fix idea: let difftest pre-seed the ff_malloc pointer global / stub SteamAPI_Init, and zero-fill (not 0xCC) callee scratch for the comparison. Time lost: ~40 minutes on frame layouts.
- [s010dac50/s00d10840/s01019090] codegen/tooling — x87+SSE camera math needed three non-obvious fixes before equiv.py went from FAIL to PASS. (1) A clamp spelled `(a<b)?b:a` propagates NaN, while the original `maxss/minss` return the SECOND operand on NaN: write `MaxSS(a,b)=(a>b)?a:b`, `MinSS(a,b)=(a<b)?a:b` and clamp as `MinSS(MaxSS(x,lo),hi)` (value-returning helpers also compile to maxss/minss; the pointer-returning Min/Max do not). (2) `cosf()` left in an x87 register is multiplied unrounded by `qInv`, whereas the original stored cos as float and used `mulss` (1-ulp diffs): `volatile float cMem = c;` for the final multiply (and `volatile float qInvMem` for the 1/sqrt) reproduces the rounding; compare the acos result in double (`double a = acos(c); fabs(a) < threshold`) because the original never rounds it to float. (3) equiv.py reports only the FIRST mismatch (`--max-mismatch` just stops early) and `--ulp 4` also "tolerates" a flags-word difference as a 2-ulp float, so a PASS under --ulp can hide a real branch difference; rerun without --ulp. Also: Havok statics (TLS monitor stream + hkThreadMemory stack allocator) are UNSUPPORTED in equiv.py (fault(read) on every input, thread memory is null), and `rdtsc` temp pattern (`mov [esp+x],eax; mov eax,[esp+x]`) is reproduced by `uint32_t t; __asm { rdtsc } __asm { mov t, eax }` inside the macro.
  Fix idea: equiv.py could seed the hkThreadMemory TLS slot and list every mismatching input; docs/matching.md could carry the MinSS/MaxSS + volatile-cos recipe. Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s0046e450/s00a14a60/s009e7bb0 cl2 s15-17] codegen — (1) /Od: a large class temporary (size 0x8c, `model->mBatches.push_back(Batch())`) that the original keeps at the very TOP of the frame (ebp-0x8c, above every named local) is reproduced by declaring the class `__declspec(align(4))`; without it cl gives the object 8-byte alignment (ebp-0x90 plus an unused dword) and the whole named-local group shifts by 4. I lost ~2 h on hash-name tricks and `Init()/Destroy()` hacks before trying it. Also: the odd 12-dword holes in that frame are `ScratchSlots<N>()` placed after the call whose callee cl declined to inline, and `AllocTag(){}` (user ctor) keeps the empty by-ref temp uninitialized. (2) /O2 /arch:SSE /fp:fast: `(uint32_t)floatExpr` / `(unsigned)` gives the inline `fnstcw/fldcw/fistp qword` sequence the original has, whereas `(int)doubleExpr` calls `_ftol2_sse`; `(int)floatExpr` is `cvttss2si`. (3) the unrolled `add eax,8` / `add eax,0x20` pointer steps in 4x unrolled hand loops come from `p[0]; ...; p += 2; ...; p += 8;` in the source (a single `p += 10` or struct indexing gives different adds). (4) my stub structs overlapped fields silently (`MultiDelayLine mLines[7]` ran into the field at +0x70); equiv.py caught it, but `typedef char Assert[offsetof(S, f) == 0x70 ? 1 : -1];` per field costs nothing and would have caught it at compile time. (5) `if (x >= 0) A else B` and `if (x < 0) B else A` differ on NaN (the original's `comiss 0,x; jbe` goes to the ADD arm for NaN); equiv.py without --nan did not see it, it showed as +inf/-inf.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the matching.md /Od and /O2 levers; chk.py could print the temp/frame slot map (my work/match/scratch_s0046e450_map.py pairs original and compiled stack slots by aligned instructions, which showed the frame mismatch in one line). Time lost: ~150 minutes.
- [s007571a0/s00beeb70/s00d60ce0] codegen — three EASTL/cl shapes found by experiment. (1) Inlined `vector::erase(begin,end)`/`clear()` loops index the per-element IV off `mpEnd` ([ebx-4]=begin, [ebx]=end) only when the helper binds `T*& rend = v->mpEnd;` and uses `rend` for the load, the loop bound and the final `rend -= (e - b)` (diff bytes 1744 -> 1135 in SetCity). (2) A POD erase whose copy loop is dead keeps its two separate IVs and the `cmp eax,eax; je` guard only if the copy result is later "used", e.g. an empty `for (T* q = d; q < v->mpEnd; ++q);` (EASTL DoDestroyValues); otherwise cl folds both IVs into one `[edx+eax]` loop. (3) `_InterlockedDecrement(&rc) == 0` compiles to `lock xadd; jne` with no `dec` (Release at 0x757... keeps an explicit `dec ebp`), and an AutoRefCount member assignment reloads `old` after the AddRef call unless `Sub4* old = p; if (q != old)` is written (still not enough when callee-saved registers run out). Also tooling: equiv.py only resolves a callee/global whose declaration carries its own `// 0x<va>` comment on its own line (a multi-name `extern float a, b, c;` or a one-line `struct S { void f(); }; // 0x..` leaves it unresolved), and it needs the `// @ 0x<va>` marker directly above the function definition.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons and the equiv.py usage text. Time lost: ~40 minutes.

(3) equiv.py needs '// 0x<va>' comments on every extern global (redeclare them next to the function) and a '// @ 0x<va>' marker directly above the member definition; a function reading nested pointer structures (model lists) stays UNSUPPORTED with 0 valid inputs.

(2) A hand-written clamp/branch on a float must reproduce comiss NaN semantics (`if (0.0f > b) neg else pos`, `if (!(r > 0)) return 0`), or equiv's float-special inputs diverge. (3) /Od loops with `++it` at the bottom are `for (;;++it)` in the original: the increment call appears first in the text, so `while` + trailing `++` gives a different call order.
  Fix idea: add these three bullets to docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~30 minutes.

- [s004a7c30/s00fac5c0/s00cab550] codegen — three re-found traps. (1) Pushes that look like a callee's args can belong to the OUTER call: `push 1.0; push const; call [vt+0x4c]` (ecx=obj) with no `add esp` and a later `ret 0x10` callee means the vcalls take no args and the pushes are the outer call's right-most args (RecordFoughtEnemyEvent(a,b,c,d)); likewise `push A,B,C,&v; call [vt+0x98]; push eax; ...; call f; add esp,0x1c` is f(…, vf98(), &v, …) with 7 cdecl args, not a caller-pops thiscall (cl cannot express that; a variadic member becomes cdecl+this-on-stack). Count stack bytes across the whole expression before declaring a variadic. (2) A function that is a secondary-base virtual (this = cVehicle+0x508, `[ebp-0x508]` is the vptr) takes its field offsets from the ModAPI absolute table minus the base offset; the 2008 PDB cVehicle offsets looked right by coincidence (0x5e8/0x618 matched PDB names but are ModAPI AF0/B20). (3) A /Od editor-region function with 16 locals matched only with `/GS-` and NO /EHsc (the slice manifest default had /EHsc for the other functions); per-row manifest flags solve it. Also: `float`-returning callee whose result is discarded leaves `fstp st(0)` after the call, so declare the stub as returning float. equiv.py gave 0 valid inputs for a method reached through nested vtables (fake-arity discards) and 13% coverage for a strand-vector loop; neither can validate the body.
  Fix idea: card.py could print "stack bytes outstanding" per call site and flag `fstp st(0)` after a call as an x87 float return; equiv.py could build a strand-vector style input from the nonmatching note. Time lost: ~60 minutes.

- [s00bb4f30 s00bee450 s00c19b60] tooling — card.py/Ghidra labels of tiny shared callees are wrong and misleading (e.g. 0x5c65e0 "SP::cCivData::GetCities" is `lea eax,[ecx+0x3c]; ret` = cStarRecord::GetPosition; 0x8e7f80 "PFIndexModifiable::GetFileCount" is cCombatant::GetDamageState; 0x00c87040 "GetItemInstanceID" returns flags at +0x5c), and tiny getters must be declared extern (not inline) or the call disappears. Also `push eax; call X; mov ecx,eax; call Y` shows Ghidra dropping a stack arg of Y. Fix idea: have card.py print each callee's first instructions + ret N inline. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s010154f0/s010ec0a0/s01034d40 cl2] codegen/tooling — (1) cmpdis.py prints a unified diff with n=2 context, so counting calls/vtable slots from its output silently drops the identical stretches; a full normalized listing of both sides (cut the original at the function size, strip trailing int3 on ours) was needed for call-sequence evidence. (2) `fld [x]; fistp qword [esp+N]` with no fnstcw/fldcw plus an `and esp,-8` frame is NOT a plain `(unsigned)f` cast (that gives fnstcw/fldcw, or a `call` under /arch:SSE): it is an `__asm { fld f; fistp result }` helper with an `__int64 result` local (EA FloatToInt64, also in s00ce6af0); `__declspec(align(8)) __int64 result` reproduces the aligned frame, and passing the float local itself (not an expression) makes it live in memory like the original. (3) Vector3 copies from a global with `/arch:SSE` give fld/fstp; adding `/fp:fast` gives the original's movss pairs. (4) A thiscall virtual-slot tail call (`add esp,N; mov [esp+8],K; jmp edx`) is just a last-statement call with the same signature as the enclosing function. - [s00e2adb0/s010fc700/s008f16c0] data — Ghidra attaches stack args to the wrong call: a getter that is just `mov eax,[ecx+N]; ret` (no `ret N`) still shows up as `GetVehicleSpecialty(city, idx)` because the caller's `push idx` precedes it; that push is really an argument of the NEXT call (`f(spec, idx)` pushes idx, calls the getter, then pushes eax). The same effect makes a trailing `add esp, 0x1c` look like a variadic callee: it is one outer call whose right-hand args were pushed before an inner no-arg vcall. Also `card.py` hides which register `call [reg+N]` reloads. - [s00eede20/s00612270/s00dbe140] tooling — (1) Ghidra's decompile of big cdecl functions with deferred stack cleanup mislabels stack args: it printed `param_1` for three different stack loads (args 2/3/4) and swapped param_5/param_6 stores, so arg roles have to be re-derived from `[esp+N]` minus the outstanding pushes. (2) equiv.py cannot resolve a method of a class in a nested namespace with a repeated name (`EA::DateTime::DateTime::Set` mangles as `?Set@DateTime@1EA@@`, which the resolver reports as unresolved); naming the method `FUN_0092e3d0` fixes it. Globals need a `// 0x...` comment on the extern or equiv says unresolved.       Fix idea: card.py could print the stack-arg index (arg k = [esp+0x34+4(k-1)+outstanding]) next to each `[esp+N]` load; equiv resolver should handle mangled back-references. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s004ea310 cl2] codegen/tooling — byte-exact 2.4 KB /Od function (FUN_004ea920) took ~4 h; what cost time and what finally worked. (1) cmpdis/chk byte counts are useless once the frame shifts; a diff that renames every `[ebp-N]` slot by first use (work/match/scratch_cl2_norm.py, plus a slot table orig-vs-mine) showed the instruction stream was already identical and only the frame layout differed. (2) `od_names.py fit K` is measured with zero params and no hidden temps, so its name order did not hold in the real function: the hash has only ~14 buckets, names in one bucket sort by REVERSE declaration order, and POD locals can be declared earlier or later to break ties. Reading the real order from `/FAs` listings (`_name$ = -N`) of many renamed copies of the function in one object (work/match/scratch_s004ea310_search.py, _cand2.py) found a full match in ~30 s. (3) Frame holes: a VSet is 0x14 bytes (extra word, a hole above every vector local), an out-of-line `~VectorBase` needs its real body inline-declined (3-word hole per dtor), `vector::erase` is a 4-word hole (`ScratchSlots<4>()`), and temps for `begin()/end()` only appear on BOTH call args when the accessor has a local (`T* it = mpBegin; return it;`). (4) Inline-ctor param temps: with plain `mpBegin`/`mpEnd - mpBegin` args the count/data slots came out swapped; giving the container `data()` and `size()` accessors fixed it. (5) difftest says UNSUPPORTED (0/400 valid) for functions that need deep structured inputs. Fix idea: add the slot-renaming diff (--slots) to cmpdis.py and a `/FAs`-based name-order search to od_names.py; note in matching.md that names must be fitted in the real function (K includes params and temps), not in a flat K-local probe. Time lost: ~120 minutes.
- [cl2 s007e6210] docs — the shared header match/slices/s007e5220/s007e5220.h declares `int cAppSystem::Init()` without the CommandLine* parameter (the retail function is `bool Init(CommandLine*)`, ret 4), and difftest/equiv.py only finds a function through a manifest row, a symbols/slices entry or a `// @ 0x<va>` marker directly above the definition. I had to define Init in a derived stub struct plus a marker comment, so the mangled class differs from the original.
  Fix idea: correct the signature in the shared header at the next integration; a "// @ 0x" marker must sit on the line right above the definition. Also: `new("App",0,0,0,0) T()` under /EHsc needs a matching 6-arg `operator delete` declaration to reproduce the EH state stores. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00482670 s010938e0 s008d5340 cl2] codegen — three reusable /Od tricks from a 2.3 KB editor function that went from 1980 diff bytes to byte-exact in ~25 compiles. (1) Diff the two instruction streams with ALL stack displacements masked (`[ebp-N]`/`[esp+N]` -> `S`, branch targets -> `T`): when only 10-20 lines differ, only the frame layout is left, and a slot-map script (align the two streams with difflib, pair the stack operands, print orig->mine in first-use order with the delta) shows exactly where the original has extra dwords. (2) Holes in the 4-byte-temp region are `ScratchSlots<N>()` calls placed right after the declined-inline call that precedes the jump in the delta (VectorLength/Apply/Normalize each reserved 1-5 dwords). (3) `f(&Vec3(a - b))` (functional-cast temporary, address taken; cl 15 allows it) is the source for "sret temp -> slot -> movss x3 into a lazily-allocated 12-byte temp -> push its address": a named local or a by-value inline param put the copy elsewhere or used fld/fstp. Named-local slot order is a hash BUCKET per name (16 buckets at K=12 plus declaration order inside a bucket, later-declared first): classify candidate names against ~15 representative names with pairwise compiles (name vs rep in both declaration orders), then pick real names so the buckets increase along the target order; random sampling of 300 name sets never hits a 12-local order. Fix idea: ship the classify/slot-map scripts (work/match/scratch_odclass.py, scratch_slotmap.py, scratch_normdiff.py) as tools/matching/ and mention them in matching.md "/Od frame layout". Time lost: ~60 minutes before finding the bucket trick.
- [s008d5340] codegen — a 2.3 KB /O2 parser (EA::Input::TriggerConfigParser::Parse) compiles to 0.94x the original size with the same call multiset but 1882 diff bytes: the original keeps `this` on the stack and the token type in esi across calls, and calls the out-of-line vector/reader dtors at 5 of ~20 return sites (cl's inline budget); no source rewording I tried (do-while inside if, strlen intrinsic, forceinline comparei, memcpy for erase) moved the register assignment. Also equiv.py coverage is only 8% for text-parsing functions (random bytes rarely reach the grammar and half of the inputs die in the isalnum import). Fix idea: let equiv.py take a seed corpus of valid inputs (a few real strings) for pointer-to-text arguments. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00845370/s005e7830/s0046c000 cl2 72-74] tooling — chk.py never prints the .obj it built (work/match/chk/<src>_<md5(src+flags)>.obj) so every cmpdis.py/cmpobj.py follow-up needs `ls -t work/match/chk/<id>_*.obj | head -1`, and cmpdis.py dies with "ambiguous" when a class has an out-of-line helper ctor with the same substring. - [s005e7830] codegen — a retail function taking a `Key` BY VALUE (3 dwords on the stack) only matched after declaring the parameter as a struct: with three scalar params cl cached the middle one in ebx (frame and every later stack offset shifted), with a struct param it re-reads `[esp+N]` at each use exactly like the original. Also, cl /O2 reuses dead incoming-arg stack slots for spilled locals, so the frame size (sub esp,0x20) tells you how many locals were NOT merged. Fix idea: add both to docs/matching.md under /O2 levers. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s0046c000] codegen — /Od name-hash slot search (od_names.py fit) only converges when ALL scopes' names are searched together and the scoring is instruction-level (difflib on normalized lines), not byte diff (byte diff is flat at ~1930 whenever the function length differs). A 2 s/30-variants generator (one object, N renamed copies, difflib score) is in work/match/scratch_s0046c000_search.py. Fix idea: promote a generic version into tools/matching (rename-search with an instruction-level score). Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00af5b40/s00cbe640/s0101bd10] docs — three re-discovered traps. (1) A call with pushed args but no `add esp` after it is NOT necessarily callee-pop: when the later call needs those args (right-to-left evaluation of an outer call such as Launch(..., speed, &pt, ctx->GetSpeed()...)), the early pushes belong to the OUTER call and the inner thiscall takes none (count the pushes against the outer callee's `ret N`; 0x00cbda10 ret 0x20 = 8 args settled it). (2) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED for `extern float g...;` globals unless each is declared on its own line with a `// 0x<va>` comment (runtime .bss globals at 0x16dd... are zero in the image, so they cannot be read back). (3) equiv FAILs that sit only in a callee-written object (e.g. viewer+0x40.. copied by 0x7c4940 from uninitialized locals) are stack-residue noise from our different frame, not a source bug; check that the call trace and the `this` pool agree.
  Fix idea: put (1) and (2) in docs/matching.md; have equiv.py print which pool region a mismatch is in (this vs argument object). Also macOS `sed -i` needs `-i ''`; Edit/python is safer. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s0103e8e0] tooling — equiv.py reported "2 unresolved reference(s)" for callees declared as `struct cEmpire { uint32_t* GetStars(); };  // 0x00c308d0`: a '// 0x...' annotation is only matched to a member when the member sits on its own line inside the class body; one-line struct declarations are not resolved.
  Fix idea: have resolve.py accept the annotation for single-line class bodies (or print which identifier it failed on). Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s010c9c70/s00a3bc70/s01071a00 cl2] tooling+codegen — (1) equiv.py cannot seed globals, so functions that start with `if (!GetApp()) return;` or a `GetElapsedSeconds() > x` gate come out WEAK at 2-5% coverage; a `--set 0xADDR=value` option (or reading `// seed: 0xADDR=val` from the source) would let agents reach the body. (2) For Havok x87 code the tester compares exact float bits, so a candidate that keeps a value in an x87 register where the original spilled it (fstp/fld through a stack slot) FAILs by 1 ulp: a `static __forceinline float hkRound(float v){ volatile float t = v; return t; }` helper at exactly the spill points (found by diffing the tester's stack-store trace of both sides) fixed it. Also read NaN behaviour from the asm: `fcomp; test ah,0x41; je X` / `test ah,5; jnp X` continue on NaN, so write the C++ as `!(a > b)` / `!(a < b)`, not `a <= b` / `a >= b`. (3) A local copy of a vector (`rayDir = dir`) gets forwarded and CSE'd with later uses of `dir`; the original keeps a real memory copy, which a copy through `volatile` loads reproduces. (4) `__asm { rdtsc } __asm { mov t, eax }` reproduces Havok's `mov [esp+N],eax; mov eax,[esp+N]` timer stamp. (5) chk.py with `--flags` must repeat /fp:fast etc.; macOS `sed -i` needs `-i ''`.

- [s0108b2f0 s00bdc350 s00e9a7c0] tooling — chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" both for a compile that produced nothing and for a function whose size is outside orig +/- 1/8, so a first draft that is 14% too small looks like a missing symbol (`-a` then lists no symbols either). Fix idea: with `-a`, always list every object symbol with its size and best diff, ignoring the size filter. Time lost: ~5 minutes (wrote work/match/scratch_cl2_sizes.py to list sizes).
- [s00c80ce0/s0097f390/s00694210] codegen — (1) In s00c80ce0 an inlined `~KeyList` loop (free nodes of an on-stack circular list) makes cl emit `push ebp; mov ebp,esp; and esp,-8` while the original is esp-based; the same loop as a noinline function, or no dtor at all, gives no frame (bisected over ~15 spellings, not solved). (2) `mov bl,1 ... mov al,bl` epilogues (result kept in bl through a switch) were not reproduced by `bResult = ok && f()`, a char/bool result, or per-case returns; cl folds to `mov al,1`. (3) s0097f390: `_ReadWriteBarrier()` after plain float member stores reproduces the original's reload of a pointer member (cl otherwise hoists the load above the stores); a function-pointer-before-args `mov edx,[ecx]; mov eax,[edx+0x10]` ahead of float math came with inline `Rect(l,t,r,b)` ctor args evaluated right-to-left into temps. (4) A 1956-byte "function" span can hold two functions (0x694bd0 is a separate tiny helper): check the tail of the card. (5) difftest: a char-returning function whose original leaves `ah` dirty (mov eax,0xfff2 mask) FAILs on `eax&0xffff`; declaring it bool avoids the false positive. Fix idea: card.py could flag trailing `ret` + `int3` padding followed by more code. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00fe0570/s00f17f70/s00d28900] tooling — ad-hoc "newest obj in work/match/chk" helpers pick another concurrent agent's object (cmpdis then says "not found" or silently diffs the wrong file); glob on the source basename (`ls -t work/match/chk/<basename>_*.obj`). Also chk.py's printed diff count stays identical across edits that change the frame, so trust cmpdis. equiv.py needs a `// @ 0x<va>` marker line above the definition (not `// 0x<va>`), and vtable/constant addresses must be extern globals with a `// 0x<addr>` comment or it reports "map that symbol" (a really-polymorphic stub class stores its own vtable).
  Fix idea: chk.py should print the obj path; equiv.py/docs should state the marker form. Time lost: ~20 minutes.

  Fix idea: add (2)/(3) to the EASTL notes in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00f17f70] codegen — `eastl::min/max` as `const float&`-returning inlines reproduce the `fld [eax]; fstp [edi]` x87 copy and lea-of-spill pattern, but only with `/arch:SSE /fp:fast /GS-` (plain /arch:SSE emits fld/fucompi compares; any `wchar_t buf[32]` local adds a /GS cookie). A `float a[3]` with only [0] and [2] assigned reserves the 0x34/0x3c slots exactly like the original's partly-used rect copy. Local float slot order (t/v1/q) could not be steered by declaration order.
  Fix idea: note "uninitialised partial arrays reserve slots" in the /O2 levers. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00d28900] codegen — a 230-store constructor matched the original store ORDER only after (a) giving the class real base structs (vptr+ctor) with a first mem-initializer that rewrites the derived vptrs, (b) using user-copy-ctor Vector2/Vector3 (init list => movss) vs POD SPVector3 (assignment => dword copies), (c) building the inline Transform/Matrix3/LocalInputState members by placement-new in the body so they are not hoisted ahead of the body stores. Constant hoisting into xmm1-xmm4 at function top is scheduler-driven and was not reproducible.
  Fix idea: add to Batch-2 lessons "ctor store order: init-list members vs body". Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00df6db0/s0072d5a0/s00c6d7c0] codegen — three reusable tricks. (1) A stub class with a vptr followed by `char padNN[off - 8]` is 4 bytes off (the vptr occupies +0..3, so the first pad is `off - 4`); every field lands 4 low and cmpdis shows only wrong displacements. (2) Passing a call result directly as an argument (`Spawn(pos, sp, n, GetFrac(), this)`) keeps the original's `push this` before the call, while a named local forces the call first; likewise an inline `size()` accessor on the vector stub (`mHerd.size()`) reproduces `mov ecx,[end]; sub ecx,[begin]` and cut a 635-byte diff to 44. (3) `equiv.py` is UNSUPPORTED on a 6-arg EA `operator new` unless its declaration carries a `// 0x00f473a0` comment, and the `// Flags:` source comment (or `(flags ...)` in the nonmatching reason) must be present or the default /EHsc flags are used.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(2) to the Batch-2 lessons; make chk.py print a pad-offset warning when a class has a virtual and a first `pad` array starting at 8. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00c015e0/s00e9d300/s010ad3b0] codegen+tooling — (1) cl 15 x87/SSE tricks that moved s00e9d300 from 1290 to 28 diff bytes: a `Vec3POD` (no ctor) by-value arg or local gives the original's integer-register copies, a Vector3 with a user copy ctor gives movss/fld pairs; `f(effect, p.x, p.y, p.z)` from a local `Vector3 p = *ptr` loads x,y,z in order (loading through the pointer directly loads z first); args evaluated before the call target when written `o->Set(&v, o->Get())` (that is how the original caches the vtable pointer in a spill slot); two `Matrix3` out-temps in disjoint scopes do NOT share a slot, declare ONE at function scope (saved 36 bytes of frame); `__forceinline Clamp` with `__asm maxss/minss` reproduces the stack-slot Clamp pattern; a float literal arg to a `float` param compiles to `fldz; fstp [esp]` / `fld [const]; fstp [esp]` not `push imm`. (2) equiv.py: UNSUPPORTED for functions that walk nested pointer structures (ctx->vector begin/end, obstacle arrays; 0/1500 valid inputs, 9 minutes), WEAK (8% coverage) for large view-update methods; no way to feed a typed memory image. (3) symbols/slices/<id>.txt must also name every unresolved callee/global (e.g. `0041cb40 Matrix3::Assign`, `014a23c4 _HK_REAL_EPSILON`) or equiv says "unresolved reference". (4) Havok x87 clamp `x = (x > eps) ? x : eps` with `fcom dword ptr [eps]` (memory operand, constant NOT hoisted) was not reproducible with extern/static/const/literal eps or any if/ternary/helper spelling (cl always loads eps first or hoists it across the six clamps). - [s00b4a720 s00ae73e0 s00edbfc0] tooling — Ghidra (and card.py) attribute argument pushes to the wrong call whenever an outer call's arguments are pushed before an inner call is evaluated (`push 1; push id; call vt[0xf0]; push eax; call f(...); add esp,8`): it invents extra parameters for the inner virtual (e.g. a 7-argument UseToolStart plus vt8(1)) and drops real ones (a 4th cdecl `char` argument at [esp+0x60]). The only reliable check is stack balance: the outer callee's `ret N` / the caller's `add esp` must equal the pushes, so inner virtual calls take whatever is left over. Fix idea: have card.py print a running push-depth next to every call and the `ret N` of statically known callees. Time lost: ~40 minutes over three functions.
- [s00b4a720] docs — chk.py's "diff" column is the number of differing bytes, not the compiled size, so "2328 1911 diff" reads like a 18% short function; size has to be taken from the object (e.g. last offset of a dump). Fix idea: print `ours=<size> orig=<size> diff=<n>`. Time lost: ~15 minutes chasing a non-existent missing block.
- [s00edbfc0 s00ae73e0] tooling — equiv.py cannot exercise UI/manager code that first reads a real global that is zero in the image (`mov ecx,[g_16c7aa4]; mov ecx,[ecx+0x74]`): the original faults, every input is discarded and the verdict is UNSUPPORTED/WEAK at 0-2% coverage. Fix idea: allow `--global 0xADDR=<pointer-soup object>` seeding. Time lost: ~25 minutes of reruns.
- [s00edbfc0] docs — appending to nonmatching.txt/partial.txt with `echo >>` glues the new row onto the last one when the file has no trailing newline (the existing files have none), which silently makes equiv.py read the wrong flags. Fix idea: have run_all/equiv warn on a row with a second VA inside it. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [s00687c20/s010f62e0/s00eccaf0] codegen — (1) compiler-generated `vector_constructor_iterator` (??_H, stdcall, call 0x401930 with the element ctor pointer pushed) is always inlined as a loop by cl at /O2 /Ob2 (even with `/Ob1` or `#pragma auto_inline(off)`), so a member array of class type never reproduces the original's `call 0x401930`; declaring the iterator as an extern stdcall function and calling it explicitly with the extern ctor address (arrays held as POD storage) reproduced the call sequence exactly. (2) A UI function that builds 0x1c0-byte descriptor structs from inlined ctors is easy to recover from the ctor store pattern: a dword store followed by a 16-bit zero store at +2 means a default ctor (`d0c=0; w0e=0`) overwritten by `d0c=flags`. (3) equiv.py needs the `// 0x...` address comment on the same line as each method declaration inside a stub class (a one-line `struct S { void* F(); }; // 0x...` leaves F unresolved). (4) chk.py prints "(no symbol of similar size)" for any compiled size outside +-12%, which for a short-by-15% function hides the real symbol; scratch_*_sizes.py (work/match) lists every symbol's size from the object. Fix idea: print the best symbol regardless of size and note the size ratio. Time lost: ~45 minutes.
- [s009e5f50/s00674640/s006f54a0 cl2] codegen — (1) cl 15 /O2 unrolls simple loops by four itself (trip count `(n-4)>>2 + 1`, scalar remainder); hand-unrolled source gets unrolled twice and ends 40% too big, so write plain loops. (2) float code that mixes `fabs()` (double, x87 fcompi) with float math only becomes SSE arithmetic with `/arch:SSE /fp:fast`; plain `/arch:SSE` made the whole 1.7 KB solver x87 (size 0x653 vs 0x6f1) and equiv.py then FAILed on sign-of-zero (extended-precision product-sub). (5) equiv.py false FAIL: a real callee (cViewer::SetRaster -> FUN_007c4a60) reads an uninitialised stack rect that holds the return address of an earlier fake call, and original and our object have different code addresses, so the written word differs on every input that reaches it (values 0x888f vs 0x33ec, same at any address); nothing in the function source can fix it.
  Fix idea: equiv.py could seed the stack below esp with the fill byte after each fake call (or mask words equal to either side's code addresses); a note in docs/equivalence.md that FAILs whose differing dword is derived from a callee's uninitialised locals are expected. Time lost: ~25 minutes on (5), ~15 on (1).

Rule that worked: sum the pushes vs the `add esp,N` that follows several calls, and read the callee's `ret N` / which [esp+k] it reads (tools/matching/card.py on the callee); a plain-`ret` callee that receives pushes at its call site does not own them. (3) equiv.py cannot resolve an `operator()` or a mangled name without a hex name or a `// 0x...` comment on its declaration line (reported as UNSUPPORTED); name callees FUN_xxxxxxxx or annotate every declaration. (4) /O2 float code with x87 sqrt needs /fp:fast, otherwise cl aligns the frame (`and esp,-16`, ebp frame) and any inline `__asm` (maxss clamp) forces an ebp frame too.
  Fix idea: card.py could print for each call the stack bytes still outstanding (pushed minus popped) so hoisted args are visible; equiv.py could fall back to the n-th-call alignment for operator() symbols. Time lost: ~60 minutes.

(5) A hand-written MMX routine (IDCT at 0xfd0f10) became a correct 1.5 KB function with <mmintrin.h> intrinsics: pass big __m64 args as `const __m64&` (C2719), and loop the two halves so cl does not inline four copies (2.9 KB vs 1.5 KB). equiv.py coverage stays at 10% for /Od loops whose count field the generator leaves at 0.
