  Fix idea: card.py could flag calls whose preceding setup writes esi/edi/eax without ecx/stack so agents know a callee is usercall; equiv.py could seed pointer-typed globals with pointer soup. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00be2070/s00b47600/s00d239a0] codegen — three recurring shapes. (1) Fully unrolled zero stores (14 `mov [esp+N],eax`) come from plain `for (i<14) a[i]=0;` loops (cl unrolls small constant loops and even unrolls `for (j<14)` search loops by 2); `= {0}` gives memset calls instead. (2) `std::min/max`-style `const T&` helpers (`(b<a)?b:a`) reproduce the original's `lea eax,[slot]` pointer-select code, and an `__asm {maxss/minss}` clamp needs its lo/hi as named locals; `cl` is a reserved register name inside `__asm`. (3) A cdecl sret chain `(out*, in*) -> out*` (normalized_safe, quaternion helpers) deferring `add esp,N` over several calls looks like a stdcall at first; check callee `ret` before trusting the card's push arithmetic. Also: the frame of all three kept coming out 0x0c-0x30 bytes larger than the original (original shares sret/temp slots across scopes; cl only shares across sibling blocks), which blocked byte-exact on all three; equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED (fault(read)) for functions that read a global manager pointer.
  Fix idea: a card.py option that tracks esp across pushes/cdecl pending cleanups and prints locals as `[F+off]`; an equiv.py option to pre-seed global pointers. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00585d40/s00ba1590/s00f7bf90 cl2_393-395] codegen — (1) a callee pushed args that are NOT popped by its own `ret` are the args of the OUTER call: `vcall(a,b)` followed by `push eax; call X; add esp,0x20` means X(vcall(a,b), 7 more args) with the 7 pre-pushed args evaluated first; Ghidra attributes them all to the vcall, so count `ret N` before trusting its arg lists. (2) /GS cookie appears for functions with a big local array (fixed_vector buffer); the original had none, so such slices need `/GS-` in their flags (put it in the source-comment flags line too, tools/difftest/slice.py reads it from there). (3) `T* x = Mgr(); x->F(args)` vs `Mgr()->F(args)` differ: the call to the accessor is emitted after the arg pushes in the latter; assign the manager to a local first when the original calls it before the pushes.
  Fix idea: add (1) and (3) to docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s006b9510/s00fa9d20/s00cacf40] codegen — (1) cl /O2 /arch:SSE unrolls simple counted loops 4x with a remainder loop by itself; hand-writing a 4x body plus remainder doubled the code (3.8 KB vs 2.2 KB), a plain `for (i<count)` gave 2.2 KB. (2) `sqrtf` compiles to a `_CIsqrt` call unless /fp:fast, even when the original does x87 fld/fsub/fsqrt (so the module is /arch:SSE /fp:fast with x87 distance math). (3) A header comment containing `@ 0x<va>` is read by equiv.py as the annotation for the next declaration (it resolved `_memset` to the function VA); write "(VA ...)" instead. (4) A null-checked `static_cast` to a secondary base (`lea eax,[ebx-0x34]; test; je; lea eax,[ebx+0x4d4]`) is `R() ? &mGauge : 0` with `R()` = this-0x34; and `eastl` `AutoRefCount::AsPPVoidParam()` is inlined in these callers as `if (p) { o = p; p = 0; o->Release(); } return &p;`.
      Fix idea: add (1) and (2) to Batch-2 lessons; equiv.py should only honor `// @ 0x` annotations placed right before a function. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s00d626d0/s00c6d0f0/s00701a00] tooling — (1) equiv.py finds the function by the `// @ 0x<va>` marker, which must sit directly above the function definition; a marker in the file header before `#include` makes it try the next helper's name ("names tried: ['Sub']"). Externs must be named DAT_<va> or equiv reports unresolved references. (2) An evidence script comparing ordered direct-call targets (original rel32 targets vs our COFF relocation symbol names via libresolve.obj_functions) plus vtable slot offsets and big immediates took 10 minutes to write and settled call-order questions at once (work/match/scratch_s00d626d0_ev.py). (3) Ghidra attributes `push` args made before a no-arg getter call (PlanetModel(), FUN_00b3d310()) to the getter; the args belong to the following thiscall (DirectionToSurfacePosition ret 8, FUN_00b45410 ret 4). A `Vec3(a,b,c)` ctor with `a->x-b->x` args evaluates y,z,x in the loads; `SubTo(d,a,b){d.x=..;d.y=..;d.z=..}` keeps x,y,z order.
  Fix idea: ship the call-sequence checker as tools/matching/evidence.py <slice> <sym> <va>. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s0059f510/s00b86500/s009ecac0 cl2 105-107] docs/codegen — (1) Before writing a big function from scratch, search the neighbours: s00b872c0 (BuildPathRibbon) was a near-twin of 0x00b86a10 and s0059e360_b already held the retail cSPEditorVerbIconData (0xC4) and s0059d300 the Property/cPropertyList stubs; adapting them took minutes, but their sum orders had to be re-derived from the asm (x87 sums are NOT in Ghidra's order: c2 = (z2+y2)+x2, l0 = (x2+z2)+y2). (2) A user `operator=` on a Vec3/Quat gives the original's fld/fstp per-float copy; plain component copies give movss. (3) cl keeps the same inline-vs-call split per site only if the helper's source order matches; the verb-icon dtor was inlined at 1 of 2 original sites in BlockGetAbilities and I could not steer it. (4) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED for vtable-heavy functions with no valid random inputs and prints a "resolution conflict" when the compiled block order differs from the original's call order; fixing the order of if/else arms (put the arm whose call comes first in the original first) makes the call sequences identical. (5) 0x009ec360-style callees (eax/esi register args) are modeled as leading args, as in s008b3ae0.
      Fix idea: card.py could list near-twin functions (same callee set) from other slices; add the x87 sum-order caveat to matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s010c2890/s00fddba0/s00d48010] tooling/codegen — (1) difftest cannot see the `/vc71` pseudo-flag for NONMATCHING Havok functions: FLAG_RE in tools/difftest/slice.py has no `/vc71`, so `flags /vc71 ...` in a reason or a `# flags:` manifest note is silently dropped and the function is compiled with VS2008 (sqrt becomes a `_CIsqrt` call and every run FAILs on the first import). Only manifest rows (byte-exact) carry it, so equiv needs `--flags "/vc71 /O2 /MD /Gy /TP"`. Fix idea: add `vc71` to FLAG_RE/EXPLICIT_RE. (2) Float-rounding lesson for x87 Havok: wherever the original does `fstp [local]` then reloads, our source must keep the value in a memory-resident struct field (hkVector4 local), because `const float` scalars stay unrounded in the x87 stack; making the segment point / normal / cap point hkVector4 locals turned castRay from 5/139 mismatches (8 ulp) into PASS. (3) `if (a < b)` compiles in cl 13.10 to `test ah,5; jp`; the original's `test ah,1; je` is `if (!(a >= b))`. (4) a float-typed global constant like 0.001f read as `fmul dword [mem]` against a double needs an `extern const float` variable; a literal `0.001f` is folded to a double constant (`fmul qword`). Time lost: ~40 minutes.

Fix idea: have chk.py print the best candidate's byte size whenever no symbol passes the size gate, and note the copy-ctor trick in the struct-by-value lesson. Time lost: ~15 minutes. Also: hk2 slice 20 (EAT_CARCASS_Tick, 1811 bytes) sits in a batch described as 150-500 byte functions; batch metadata should be checked before fan-out.

(3) The calls-evidence script copies stop at the first `ret` for OUR object and then compare against a 63-byte data symbol when the substring also matches a keep-alive global; use "?Method@" as the substring and bound our bytes by the next symbol. (4) Codegen: `dest = V(src)` (V with a user copy ctor, default operator=) gives the "movss temp; mov r,temp; mov dest,r" copy of the Swarm writers; `Vector3` rows written as `out->v = row * s` (operator* returning a Vector3) give the stack-temp output stores; x87 compare `fld dword [const]; fcompi st(1); fstp st(0)` needs /arch:SSE /fp:fast (plain /fp:fast gives fcomp+fnstsw); an inlined-then-not-inlined split of the same string helper (first site calls operator=/~string out of line) can be reproduced with a twin type that declares those two out of line.
      Fix idea: log the unreferenced-internal-linkage case in docs/matching.md and have chk.py list the object's symbols when no size match; document the equiv.py marker/annotation rules in docs/equivalence.md. Time lost: ~40 minutes.

- [s00e62b80/s0066afd0/s00d5a260] tooling — (1) difftest marker lookup needs a `// @ 0x<va>` comment directly above the definition, and `equiv.py` takes compile flags from the source header comment, so a missing `/GS-` there makes every input hit `import:_decode_pointer` (UNSUPPORTED 0/400); (2) a shrink-wrapped `push ebx` in the middle of a function (callee-saved reg first used late) shifts every later [esp+N] by 4 until the matching pop, which cost time when reading Ghidra's stack names; (3) the earlier partial for 0066b480 was a 2 KB function of 16 identical `new("Sporepedia",0,0,0,0) T; AutoRefCount push_back` blocks: an `AutoRef` struct plus `DoInsertValueEnd(pos, const AutoRef&)` (arg is the address of the ref) is the right shape, but cl still keeps the new-result in an EAX/ECX temp where the original keeps ESI.

A `wchar_t buf[N]` local also adds a /GS cookie (use `uint32_t store[N/2]` + cast). (2) equiv.py reads `// 0x...` annotations only from the slice's own `<id>.cpp` and its `*.h`, so code in a second `.cpp` (even `#include`d) is UNSUPPORTED; put shared stubs in a `.h`. (3) The second MP3 scalefactor reader (0x0115b910) was the sibling of the byte-exact 0x01159cc0 in slice s01159630; a search of symbols/slices for "ISO"/"scalefac" would have found it before reading any asm. Time lost: ~30 minutes on the 53-byte record fill.
- [hk2 s00bcd2a0] tooling — equiv.py reported UNSUPPORTED 0/400 valid (fault(read) 309) for a function that calls through vtable slots on a sub-object (`mov eax,[ecx+0x34]; call [eax+0x5c]`). The fake-method path never got a valid this/vtable, so the check could not run. Fix idea: have equiv.py accept per-slot fake targets from the source header, or document the fake-vtable setup in matching.md. Also: a thiscall with `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` needs `--flags` passed explicitly, because equiv.py otherwise falls back to default flags silently. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s01042450/s00b46660/s00fa5610] codegen — /fp:fast x87/SSE mixing (fsqrt on an unrounded x87 sum next to SSE float math) flips ulps and zero signs vs the original; equiv.py flags 1-ulp diffs and the structure shifts with every source edit. A `volatile float` temp for a 1/sqrt result forces the store-then-reload the original shows. Also equiv.py needs `// 0x...` comments on every extern global (not just functions) or it reports UNSUPPORTED unresolved reference.
  Fix idea: document the volatile-temp lever and the global-comment requirement in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~40 minutes.

- [slice-id mapping] Functions were first placed in the wrong slice directory: 00a43a50 belongs to s00a42cb0 and 00bdf600 to s00bdee20, not s00e5a430. Check slice_info output before writing a slice's .cpp.
- [equiv.py marker] equiv.py needs a `// @ 0x<va>` marker above the definition (or a FUN_<va> name) to find an object symbol; a bare C++ name is not enough (UNSUPPORTED "no marker/name ... matched an object symbol").
- [equiv.py callee VAs] Callee declarations need a trailing `// 0x<va>` on the declaration line itself. A `// 0x...` comment on the line above is ignored, which leaves "unresolved reference" UNSUPPORTED.
- [float rounding] x87 vs SSE: a float intermediate can stay unrounded in x87 registers. Forcing 32-bit rounding at the original's store points needs `volatile float` temps; the un-volatile version differed by 1 ulp on an equiv input.
- [equiv coverage] equiv on ARCVectorTuning::Assign returns WEAK (117 valid inputs, 42.9% coverage): random vector pointers mostly fault on read, so a clean run does not prove equivalence.
- [s00496d30/s005e4db0/s01159630] codegen — (1) a `switch` where several case ids return the same constant as `default`: stacking the labels onto the default body makes cl DROP those compares from the binary tree; give each such case its own `return K;` statement and the tree matches (orig keeps all 64 ids). The orig still has two dead `cmp` instructions before fallthrough copies of the default block that no source variant reproduced (22 bytes short). (2) /Od "returned-by-value then copied into a local" needs the operators to return a BASE-class type (`struct W` with the out-of-line copy ctor) while the local is a derived `V3 : W` with an inline `V3(const W&)`: this also gives the extra copy of a temp when it is bound to a `const V3&` argument. (3) By-value args copied by an out-of-line ctor in the original store `esp` in a hidden slot first (`mov [ebp-N],esp ... mov ecx,[ebp-N]`); none of noinline / declared-only / inline_depth(0) / auto_inline(off) variants reproduced the slot. (4) equiv.py for a nonmatching function with no flag list in its reason falls back to /O2; put a `// Flags: ...` comment at the top of the .cpp.
  Fix idea: document (1)/(2) in matching.md. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s008f7690 s00d25ac0 s00bfcc80] tooling — (1) chk.py prints "no symbol of similar size" with no sizes, so a 26-byte size gap (172 vs 198) looked like a bad candidate; the real cause was a 4-byte member pointer where MSVC's 16-byte unknown-inheritance form was needed (fixed by a class member of the PMF type declared before StyleState). Fix idea: print our size next to the target's in that message. (2) Without `/GS-` in the flags, chk output carries a stack cookie (`mov eax,[0]; xor eax,esp`) that the original lacks; the default flags should carry `/GS-` for this module. (3) equiv on ParseBoxHeight returned WEAK at 43.1% coverage (1488 valid inputs): random token/ident states never reach the "auto" compare or the MatchLength path, and there is no per-slice input hint. Fix idea: an optional per-slice hint (e.g. token value / string seed) in symbols/slices. (4) equiv on IsDestroyed returned UNSUPPORTED (0/600 valid, fake-arity 595) because the object vslots and FUN_00bfca80 have no arity info; the 3-arg form was inferred from `add esp,0xc`. Fix idea: let symbols/slices/<id>.txt give arities for callees. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s0109b490/s008a5700/s010554c0] codegen — (1) a function whose body is only `static T x;` registrations (28 bits in one init mask) is byte-exact by copying the `STATIC_REGISTRATION(N)` macro + `cSimRegistration<N>` template from s00b60d80; grep for the callee VA (0x00692f60) in match/slices first. (2) cl keeps a POD struct returned by hidden pointer as a copy; giving it user ctors (`Sym3x3(){}`) makes the call use the caller's slot with `lea` (no copy), and putting a named temporary in an inner `{ }` scope lets its stack slot be shared (frame 0x88 -> 0x6c). (3) A ctor with a virtual-dtor base under /EHsc gets an EH frame the original lacks: drop /EHsc. (4) Batch loops `for (p = a; p < a + n; ++p)` that contain calls reload `n` and `a` from memory each pass; hoist `end = it + n` into a local to get the original's single `lea`.
  Fix idea: chk.py's size filter prints "(no symbol of similar size)" whenever the function is >12.5% off; a scratch script that disassembles both sides and diffs with esp offsets masked (work/match/scratch_s008a5700_var.py, scratch_s0109b490_var.py) was far more useful for iterating. equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED for constructors that store a vtable address. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00dadce0 s00bc1690 s00eaffc0] tooling — (1) equiv.py on 00dadfb0 returns WEAK (295 valid of 400, 21.0% coverage, need 60%): random SpawnHost/RegionOwner pointers fault on read (96 of 400 discarded as fault(read)), and there is no per-slice pointer hint, so the run cannot confirm the region path. (2) equiv.py on 00bc1940 returns UNSUPPORTED "unresolved reference ??0TreeArg@@QAE@ABV0@@Z" because an out-of-line copy ctor declared without a body cannot be resolved; there is no way to point equiv at the original copy ctor 0x00af0d40. Fix idea: allow symbols/slices/<id>.txt to bind a declared callee to a VA (like `// 0x<va>` but for members). (3) Ghidra names 0x009a9600 "rbtree DoNukeSubtree" but it is also the tail of other functions and the 0x00bbfbf0 median helper; the card's names for shared destructors are misleading, so check the caller's arguments before trusting a name. Time lost: ~45 minutes.
- [s00dadce0] codegen — the vtable call `host->Attach(region->target, true, false)` (slot 0x84) takes the vptr in edx in the original but cl loads it into eax when the lea of `region->target` is pushed first; about ten spellings (param/return types, local copies, const/volatile, alias forms) left the 14-byte diff unchanged. Fix idea: document the lea-before-vptr ordering case in matching.md with the working variant. Time lost: ~50 minutes.
- [s007c1c10/s01135a30/s00f6b9a0] codegen/tooling — (1) a frame 4 bytes too big at /O2 was two same-typed locals declared in sibling scopes (`RectID tmp` in each `if` block): cl gave each block its own slots, while the original shares one function-level local; declaring it once at function scope took 1583 -> 193 diff bytes. Look for "same temp stored twice at the same [esp+N] in different blocks" in the original. (2) `static float x = kExternConst;` reproduces the `test byte [guard],1 / or [guard],1` function-static init; the constant needs a `// 0x<va>` comment on its extern or equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED (unresolved reference). (3) equiv.py reported "0/400 valid, fault(read)" for a SEH-prologue function (`mov eax, fs:[0]`) -- the emulator probably has no TIB, so such functions cannot be difftested. (4) symbols/slices/<id>.txt may already hold PDB names: append with `>>`, don't overwrite (I clobbered one and restored it from the file's git HEAD copy).
      Fix idea: tell agents to `cat symbols/slices/<id>.txt` first; give equiv.py a fake fs:[0]. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s004ef880/s010414c0/s00fa0f90] codegen — three re-usable /Od and /O2 findings. (1) /Od inline-callee slots: a by-value scalar param gets its own slot, allocated after the other params, only when its address is taken (e.g. a free inline `operator==(const Elem16&, const uint32_t&)` in the loop test); otherwise an lvalue arg gets an arg temp that flips the temp order. A discarded scalar accessor result gets no slot, but a discarded constructed object holding the loaded global does (a late-region slot when inside a nested inline). Top-level locals are pre-allocated in hash order, while first-level inline params and return-value temps are allocated in code order and interleave with each other. `ScratchSlots<17>()` at the map operator[] call reproduced a 64-byte hole. (2) `__declspec(novtable)` on an all-pure-virtual primary base drops its vptr store, so a secondary RefCountVTemplate base stores first (matched an inlined job ctor). (3) A module with `/arch:SSE` still needs `/fp:fast` for mulss (otherwise x87 fld/fmul); `chk.py` only matched once both were given. Also: my variant harness compiled `scratch_*.cpp` but cmpdis read a stale `s004ef880_*.obj` (chk names objs `<basename>_<hash>.obj`), so glob `*<slice>_*.obj` when looking at scratch variants.
  Fix idea: chk.py could print the .obj path it just wrote (and cmpdis take it); add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00f32880/s00a954b0] tooling — chk.py with default flags (/EHsc) on a function whose locals have destructors prints only "(no symbol of similar size)" and `-a` lists nothing: the EH state table makes the function bigger than the original's, with no hint that EH is the cause (the original had no EH frame, so the right flags were /O2 /MD /Gy /TP). Also equiv.py reports WEAK at 11.6% coverage for a lock-buffer streamer (fake Lock call returns 0, so the particle loop never runs); there is no option to script a callee's return value.
  Fix idea: chk.py could print the size of the best-named symbol and a note when __ehhandler/__unwindfunclet symbols exist; equiv.py could accept `--fake-ret N`. Time lost: ~10 minutes.
- [cl2 s00bb7940/s005a1920/s00cffd50] tooling — three time sinks. (1) scratch ordered-call checkers (scratch_cl2_seq.py) lose most indirect calls because capstone prints small offsets without `0x` (`[eax + 4]`); a regex needs `(?:0x)?[0-9a-f]+` or it silently reports 2 vcalls instead of 6 (it hid a wrong vtable slot 0x70 vs 0x74 until fixed). (2) card.py's pretty "x87 float->int" is invisible: the original often has `fstp [m]; cvttss2si eax,[m]` or inlined `fnstcw/fldcw/fistp qword` where cl gives `__ftol2_sse`/`cvttss2si xmm0`; float-in-memory (phi-merged or global float) is what produces cvttss2si [mem], a bare `(int)call()` never does. (3) in a multi-variant scratch file the inline/outline choice of shared inline dtors depends on the other variants, so compare each variant in its own file (try_variants ranks every function in one object and cmpdis line counts shifted by 60 lines). Fix idea: a shared tools/matching/calls.py (ordered direct/vtable call list + size/call ratios) and a `--each` mode for try_variants. Time lost: ~60 minutes.

- run_all.py --manifest takes a path argument (not a flag). Pass match/slices/<id>/manifest.txt for a single slice; a bare --manifest errors with "expected one argument".
- [s005fc330/s010409b0/s009b5ce0] docs — (1) a vtable stub with PV(n) padding is easy to get off by one: count slots as offset/4 (GetProperty at +0x24 is slot 9, so 9 pads before it, not 7); chk.py showed 32 diff bytes, all `[eax+0x20]` vs `[eax+0x24]`. (2) `float` literal 3.4028235e+38f gives C2177 "constant too big" in cl 15; use FLT_MAX from <float.h>. (3) equiv.py resolves callees only from a `// 0x<va>` comment on the declaration line itself, a comment on the previous line is ignored (UNSUPPORTED unresolved reference), and a file-static empty-string array avoids the unresolved eastl::gEmptyString16.
  Fix idea: mention (3) in docs/matching.md playbook next to the equiv.py paragraph. Time lost: ~15 minutes.

(4) Two branches that both start with PlanetModel() get that call hoisted by cl; the original kept one per arm. - [s00db0130/s00eb92d0/s007bfaa0] tooling — three things cost real time. (1) cmpdis diffs are unreadable when the frame size differs by 4 bytes, because every `[esp+N]` line differs; masking all `esp + 0x..`/`sub esp`/`add esp` operands (a regex pass over the normalized lines) cut a 600-line diff to ~100 and exposed real source bugs at once (two text-zoom holders swapped, a `cFilterChainJob` int member at +0x24 that is +0x28 in the original; s007c0780's copy of that struct has the same wrong offset, not mine to edit). (2) `cl.sh ... /FAs /Fa<file>` prints the stack equates (`_task$ = -52`, `_r$ = 28`, `_st$ = 28`), which shows directly which locals share a slot and which dead parameter homes cl reuses; a single-use stack parameter keeps its home live unless it is copied into a local at the top (`ParamObj* pp = prm;`), after which the home is reusable like the original's. (3) another agent overwrote work/match/scratch_cl2_seq.py (a shared, non-slice-id name) with a different script mid-run, and its regex ignores single-digit vtable offsets (`[eax+4]` shows up as a stale slot).
  Fix idea: add `--mask-esp` to cmpdis.py, an `--asm` listing option to chk.py, and fold "copy a single-use stack param into a local to free its home slot" into the /O2 levers. Time lost: ~60 minutes.

(2) Havok TLS functions (hkThreadMemory stack alloc, monitor-stream timers) are UNSUPPORTED in equiv.py (0/N valid, fault(read)): the emulated TlsGetValue block is zero so the stack-allocator vtable is null; and views/handlers that bail out on a soup byte (`if (!obj->flag) return`) stay WEAK at 2-5% coverage. (3) codegen: a module without a security cookie / EH frame needs `/GS-` and NO `/EHsc` as soon as an inline helper has a dtor (EH state setup otherwise); a derived->base `lea ecx,[eax+0xc0]` stays materialised (`add eax,0xc0; cmp [eax+0x74]; ... mov [eax+0x74],0`) only when ONE inline method of the base does the test-and-clear (`bool ConsumeDirty(){ if(m){m=0;return true;} return false;}`), plain field access through the derived pointer folds the offset and a local `Base*`/`Base&` adds a null check; a temporary `Vec3(0,0,1)` passed by const ref and a `Vec3(*p)` copy passed to an inline setter share one stack slot, a by-value class parameter is never inlined; the movss-vs-dword copy of a Vec3 is "user copy ctor vs implicit assignment"; `(v>>n)&1` testing needs `u8 b=(u8)(v>>n); return b&1;`. (4) Not solved: the load order of three `G.k - p->k` subtractions (original z,x,y; every declaration/sum order tried gives x,z,y or z,y,x), and the Havok "12-float skip-w stack copy of the transform + 4x unrolled x87 transform" (same miss as s010dac50). - [s00fabd60/s00f59370/s00e97bf0] tooling/docs — (1) equiv.py reads `// 0x...` annotations only from the slice's own source, and its struct scanner mis-parses `struct __declspec(align(16)) Name` (scope becomes `__declspec`), so methods of such a struct go unresolved; put the alignment on a member (`__declspec(align(16)) char mData[..]`) instead. A static callee that is defined in another slice must be copied into the TU (with `// @ 0x...` above it and `__declspec(noinline)`) to get cl's register convention AND an equiv annotation. (2) `/GS-` is needed when a function with a local array shows no cookie check; `__security_check_cookie` as an extra call is the tell. (3) cl fully unrolls a constant 3-iteration matrix loop; a do/while with pointer increments keeps the loop and matched the original's `and esp,-16` frame. (4) equiv --fake-calls leaves sret buffers uninitialized, so Velocity/Normal-style virtual calls returning Vector3 give -0.0/garbage FAILs that are not real. - [s00e74a20] codegen — MSVC shares stack slots between locals declared in disjoint nested blocks (a `{ Vector3 tmp; ... }` per use got the original's single 0x18 slot and shrank the frame from 0x58 to 0x4c), and a loop cursor/end/predicate packed in one local struct whose member is called thiscall reproduces `it`/`end` living in memory and reloaded after each call (s00d8bd30 FilterIter). - [s00b76f10/s00f392b0/s0137ef90] docs/codegen — (1) chk.py prints `<va> <orig-size> <diff-bytes> diff <symbol>`: the number before "diff" is the differing-byte count, not our size (easy to misread; our size is the COMDAT length, e.g. via parse_coff). (2) The 2008 PDB's SP::cString is 0x1c bytes but retail's is 0x14 (ctor 0x006b5770 writes +0..+0x11 only); using the PDB size changes the caller's frame by 8 bytes. (3) cl's stack-slot sharing between disjoint-lifetime locals follows the textual order: a `goto existing;` that jumps over the create block keeps `res` live across it and blocks sharing; write `if (found) {...} else {...}` instead. (4) `(ms/1000)/60` and `(ms/1000)%60` written as two separate expressions give the original's single `cdq; idiv ecx` (0x3c), while `int s=ms/1000; s/60; s%60` gives two imul sequences. - [cl2 441-443] tooling — a stub struct with only methods (e.g. `struct IdPool { void Init(); }`) has size 1, so `Gap<>` padding computed as `(next_off - prev_off)/4` silently shifts every later field; the equivalence checker then faults at `eip=3000006c` with no hint. (3) x87-sqrt-in-SSE-module: `(float)(1.0 / sqrt(x*x+y*y+z*z + 1e-8f))` under /arch:SSE /fp:fast gives the fld/fmul/fsqrt/fld1/fdivrp sequence, but only if the helper is `__forceinline` (plain `inline` leaves a register-convention local call); same for a push_back with a slow path.

(3) /Od vector classes: `n = -x` / `n = x * s` (slot holding the returned pointer + movss triple) and `n = Normalize(n)` (dword copy straight from eax) come from TWO types: a result type with a user copy ctor that the local's user `operator=(const X3&)` copies member-wise, and a local type whose implicit operator= does the dword copy; `Scratch<N>()` (`unsigned pad[N]` in a __forceinline template) reproduced the declined-inline holes (5 dwords per Normalize call) and a named `unsigned hole[11]` the by-value Matrix3 arg frame. - [s00c54110/s00934260/s00cf3bf0] docs — constant stack args are pushed EARLY by cl (before the call that computes another arg), so `push 0,0,x,key; call [vtbl+N] (no args); push eax; push ctx; push ebx; call f(ret 0x1c)` is ONE 7-arg call to f whose inner vcall takes no arguments (the same goes for `push 1; call [vt+0x2c]` before a `ret 0x14` callee). Counting `ret N` of the later callee settles it; my first reading gave the virtual call 4 phantom args. Also `lea ecx,[esp+X]` offsets taken after pending pushes must be re-based to the post-prologue esp to map locals (strings/Keys) to frame slots. The slice dirs for frozen batch cl2 did not exist yet (slice_info only prints the id), so mkdir match/slices/<id> first.
  Fix idea: card.py could annotate each call with its callee's `ret N` and mark pushes consumed by it; also let chk.py accept a VA list without a work/batches lookup. Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [s004c3230/s00a478b0/s0086d090] codegen — three things cost the most. (1) At /Od a local struct of 64+ bytes is placed on an 8-byte boundary relative to ebp (leaving a 4-byte hole under the first int), unless the type is declared `__declspec(align(4))`; the retail eastl::fixed_vector was exactly that (fixed_vector<T*,40> at -0xbc next to an int at -4). (2) An inlined dtor with `for (T* p = mpBegin; p < mpEnd; ++p) {}` plus `ScratchSlots<3>()` reproduces the tail loop slot and its hole, and a by-value empty-tag call (`assign(first,last)` -> `DoAssign` -> `DoAssignFromIterator`) needs two dead empty-tag locals in the inline body (`TagFalse a; TagFalse b;`) so the pushed tag lands on the third byte; the begin()/end() accessor temps (not raw pointers) fix the first/last slot order. (3) Compiling `/Z7` and reading the S_BPREL32 records (type 0x110B, null-terminated name) out of the .obj gives every local's ebp offset by name; a 60-variant-per-compile search over meaningful local names scored on that found a 12/12 /Od slot order in under a minute (work/match/scratch_s004c3230_dbg*.py, *_s3.py).
      Fix idea: ship a tools/matching/locals.py that prints name->ebp offset from a /Z7 compile, and let od_names.py fit use it. Also libpng's png_build_gamma_table (0x870860) stays 590 bytes off only because cl spills `i` in the 16-bit loops where the original spills `ig`; no source rewrite tried (about 40) changed the allocation. Time lost: ~120 minutes.
- [s01092e10/s00b981a0/s00e87cf0] codegen/tooling — (1) Havok x87 difftest 1-ulp FAILs came from two places where cl keeps a just-stored float unrounded in the x87 stack but the original reloads it from memory: world-space vectors (read them back through a `const volatile hkVector4&`) and an angle that is stored and then re-read in a sum (read it via `*(volatile float*)&field`). Both cost me ~40 minutes; a volatile read is the cheap lever and does not move the rest of the frame. (2) `if (a < b)` under /vc71 gives `test ah,1; je`; the original's `test ah,5; jp` could not be reproduced by `!(a>=b)`, `b>a` or `!(b<=a)`, but the NaN behaviour (no change on NaN) means plain `a < b`. (3) A function that wraps coordinates with a do/while over an out-param call (`while (WrapCubeFace(n,&f,&c,&r,0,0));`) must set the locals BEFORE the loop; assigning inside the do-body resets them each retry. (4) difftest reports UNSUPPORTED/WEAK (coverage 7%) for functions that need a valid pointer graph (grid of cells, nested vtables); the static evidence (reloc order, vslot offsets) is all one can give. Fix idea: let equiv.py accept a hand-written input builder file per slice. Time lost: ~60 minutes.

(2) A first equiv FAIL (eax) in a 2 KB float function came from declaring bool-returning callees as `int` (original tests `al`, the fake-call returns have garbage in the upper bits): check `test al,al` after each call and declare `bool`; the 64-bit mask test `mov ebp,[a]; mov edx,[a+4]; or eax,edx` is a `uint64_t` (`#pragma pack(4)` struct), not two ints. (3) equiv.py of a 2 KB function with loops takes 10 min (time-limit 600) and prints only the first mismatch; run it in the background with `--inputs 800` and keep compiling elsewhere. (4) A by-value struct arg whose copy is `movss` x4 needs a user copy ctor on the struct while a plain struct copy (member assign) stays integer moves; cl hoists the identical default-arg copy of an if/else above the compare only when the callee args are plain calls (wrapping them in an inline helper or caching `AssetBrowser()->p` in a local loses the hoist).

(3) macOS `sed -i` needs `sed -i ''`; a bare `sed -i 's/..//' file` silently treats the file as the backup suffix and edits nothing (cost a wrong-offset recompile).

(2) A `// 0x...` annotation is needed on EVERY overload of a callee that shares one address, otherwise equiv reports a bogus call-trace mismatch on the copy ctor.   tooling — equiv.py's annotation scanner mis-scopes `struct __declspec(align(8)) X {` (the `)` of align(8) breaks the class regex), leaving every member annotation unresolved; and addresses in the `.bind` section (e.g. a type tag 0x018c43e8) are "annotation-implausible", so pass such tags as `(const void*)0x...`. (2) `pow(0.1, x)` vs `pow(0.1f, x)`: the retail constant is the double of the float 0.1f (0x1447a10 = 0.10000000149); using the double literal 0.1 gives a 1-ulp equiv.py FAIL, and the (float,float) overload of pow changes codegen, so write `pow((double)0.1f, (double)dt)`. (5) A callee that ignores/doesn't pop a pushed arg (93ba70 GetBuffer: plain ret) is just `rc->Read(req->GetBuffer(), size)` with size pushed first, not a 2-arg call. Fix idea: let equiv.py take globals from the original image (.data) when our object references them. Time lost: ~20 minutes.

- [s00509f50/s00667c80/s00b4ed50] codegen — the /Od frame of a 1.7 KB function (0x50a0a0) went from 1130 to 0 diff bytes in about an hour once these were known. (1) Slot order is per lexical scope (function scope first, then each nested block in order; for-init vars after their block), sorted by name hash with K = that scope's own count, so each scope can be searched alone: compile ~150 one-scope functions per object with random natural names (declaration order fixed by the code order) and keep the ones whose slot order equals the original; K=8 needed ~5k samples, K<=4 are instant. (4) MSVC pads the vptr of a polymorphic class with an aligned (hkVector4) member out to 16 bytes, so address Havok vectors in such stubs by offset. (5) Also: s00509f50's 0x509f50 "InsertEdge" is wrong (the original calls a method on arg 3 + 8 and has 2 calls; it is not a slot-table edge insert).
      Fix idea: ship my work/match/scratch_s00509f50_{ndis,slots,names,search}.py as tools/matching/od_scope.py (frame-offset-masked instruction diff + per-scope name search). Time lost: ~60 minutes before the scope rule was found.
- [s00508400/s00bb6a30/s0077d800 cl2] codegen — (1) /Od named-local slot order is NOT fixed by names alone when ties exist: declaring all of a scope's locals up front, in a declaration order found by compiling ~100 shuffled flat copies of the scope (same types, one distinct constant store per local, read the ebp slot of each store), got 46/46 slot offsets right where od_names.py fit lines alone failed; a struct-typed local with a user ctor can swap with a scalar neighbour otherwise. (2) The `mov [slot],ecx; mov edx,[slot]; mov eax,[edx]` after `lea` is an inline `T& operator[](i) { T* q = b + i; return *q; }` (named local pointer inside the accessor); `return b[i];` or `*(b+i)` folds with no slot. (3) A second copy temp such as `movss x3 into [ebp-0x13c]` before a call means the callee's param class (rw Vector3, out-of-line copy ctor 0x4098a0) is constructible from the operator's result class: give it an inline converting ctor `V3Out(const Vec3&)`; named locals of the param class then pass with no copy. A dot product whose z/y/x products load in that order is an inline `Dot(a,b)` helper, not inline source. (4) Holes below the last temp are `ScratchSlots<N>()` at the end of the branch that expands the declined inlines (5 and 10 dwords here; the this-slot lands below them). (5) cl's zero-in-ebx-from-entry (`xor ebx,ebx`, `push ebx`, `cmp r,ebx`) triggers with about four LIVE zero stores (dead stores overwritten before any call do not count); I could not find the original's source for it in 0x00bb6a30 (StarSystem::Init, 2075 B, same size and call order, 1592 diff bytes). Fix idea: a tool that bisects statements to find the zero-hoist trigger. Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s007689a0/s01074bb0/s00cbcde0] tooling — (1) tools/difftest/resolve.py `source_annotations` reads a `// 0x...` comment on a one-line `struct X { R Method(); };  // 0x...` as a free declaration (the class scope opens after the identifier is recorded), so the method stays "unresolved" and equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED; the same declaration spread over three lines resolves. (2) equiv.py cannot exercise functions that walk deep pointer graphs (WriteResourceGameModel: 0/600 valid inputs, every run faults), so such functions get no differential evidence beyond call/vtable-order comparison. (3) A callee that takes its argument in EAX (0x01066720 = static helper, `call` right after a function returned the pointer in eax) can only be reproduced by defining a `__declspec(noinline) static` copy of its body in the slice; a same-TU body also fixes the "ecx reloaded after the call" difference (0x01065e20 -> 0x01065e40 reuse ecx) and byte-matched at once.
  Also re-found: `std::swap`-like register choices flip with trivial source tweaks (an inline accessor `GetStride()` returning uint8_t gives the original's `mov al,[m]; movzx x2` pair; an inline `PutU32(s, v)` by-value helper gives "vtbl load, then store the temp, then push"). A float walk that overwrites its own running vector (`cur = normalize(cur)*R` in place) must not be written with a separate temp: it changed 5/63 differential inputs. Fix idea: resolve.py should pre-split one-line struct bodies; chk.py should print byte diffs against the VA-named symbol only. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00e2b9d0/s01123850/s00b08670] codegen — one slice went from 1399 diff bytes to byte-exact in ~6 compiles once the flags and four source shapes were right: (1) a mix of movss/ucomiss for plain float compares and x87 fcompi for float returns means `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` (the same module as s00e2adb0 needs /fp:fast; without it ucomiss compares come out as fld/fucompi; sqrtf also needs /fp:fast to be inline fsqrt, not __CIsqrt); (2) per-arm `push 0; push IMM` for a text id needs a separate `cString line(...)` + `text += ...` in every if/else arm (cl tail-merges them), not an `id` variable or `c ? A : B`; (3) `cmp ebp,eax; jl A` layout is chosen by which arm falls through, so swap the arms to flip it; (4) a float returned in st0 that is only compared (cost vs money) must be an unnamed temporary: write `civ->Cost(..) <= civ->GetMoney()` (callee order matches left-to-right) instead of `float cost = ...;`, which moves the spill slot. Also: `cmpobj.py ... diff@ [...]` lists the real differing byte offsets (cmpdis.py output is mostly masked-call noise). Evidence script work/match/scratch_s00439110_calls.py still stops at the first `ret` of OUR function (papercut already logged; fixed in scratch_s00e2b9d0_calls.py). Another: equiv.py reports only UNSUPPORTED/WEAK for UI/Havok functions with pointer-heavy arguments (0 valid inputs), so a "complete" claim rests on the call-order evidence there.
      Fix idea: fold (1)-(4) into docs/matching.md "/O2 levers"; make chk.py print cmpobj's `diff@` offsets. Time lost: ~0 (found while iterating), but ~30 minutes would have been saved by knowing (1) at the start.
- [cl2 468-470] tooling — /O2 functions with `sub esp,N` and no frame pointer are hard to map to locals: card.py/cmpdis print raw `[esp+X]`, whose meaning moves with every push and with callee-popped `ret N` calls (an `lea edx,[esp+0x2c8]` turned out to be the end of an on-stack fixed buffer, i.e. `[entry_esp+0]`). I wrote work/match/scratch_s00af7c60_stk.py (rewrites `[esp+X]` as `[E+-k]`, offsets from the entry esp, with a callee-pop table) and it made the frame, the fixed_vector header and the 16 call args readable at once. Fix idea: add an `--entry-relative` mode to card.py/cmpdis.py that tracks esp (push/pop/add/sub/ret N of known callees). Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s01019f50/s00d73090/s00db5e80] codegen — (1) under /arch:SSE /fp:fast cl canonicalizes the ORDER of a float sum (`a*a+b*b+c*c` compiles identically for all 6 spellings), but the order of the operand LOADS follows the declaration order of the temporaries (`float dz=..; float dx=..; float dy=..;`), so brute-force the three declaration orders, not the sum. (2) `-(unsigned)bit & MASK` drops the `sbb` when bit is known 0/1; the original's `neg; sbb; and` is `(bit ? MASK : 0)`. (3) A callee that returns a matrix by value (hidden out pointer) plus an out-of-line 0x41cb40 "Assign" copy lets cl share the temp slot with the later product temp; a named `Matrix3 tmp` local never shares, so declare the callee as returning the struct by value. (4) `(int)f` through memory (`movss [esp+N],xmm; cvttss2si eax,[esp+N]`) is an `__asm cvttss2si eax, f` helper, same as the cvtss2si RoundToInt helper.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(4) to Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00de7200/s004e6c60/s01030b10] codegen — (1) `#define _CRTIMP` (empty) before `#include <math.h>` makes `floor()` a plain extern, so calls go through the local CRT thunk 0x011e0906 like the original, and the C++ overload floor(float) -> floorf -> floor(double) inlining at /Od reproduces the arg-temp + `fstp/fld` return temps exactly; `1.0f + floor(x)` gives `fld1; faddp`, `floor(x) + 1.0f` gives `fadd [mem]`. (2) x87 `sqrt` inline `fsqrt` (no `__CIsqrt` call) needs /fp:fast, which also reassociates sums. (3) At /O2, bool locals are laid out by descending reference count (ties by IR order), so a mismatched extra reload shifts every byte-slot; names and declaration order do not matter at /O2 (test in one object with chk.py -a). (4) Ghidra shows stack args reused as locals (`cStack_c`, `unaff_EDI`); tracking push depth by hand per instruction was needed to see which [esp+N] is the arg slot.
  Fix idea: card.py could annotate [esp+N] with the current push depth and mark arg slots; od_names.py fit could accept a role list directly. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s008a47f0/s0061ecf0/s00cb7e50] codegen — (1) under /arch:SSE /fp:fast, cl reassociates `acc += p*w` loops into even/odd lanes; the original's sequential chain (and 1-ulp-exact equiv.py PASS) came only from accumulating through an inline helper returning a POD Vec3 by value (`mean = AddScaled(mean, p, w)`), and `(d*w)*d` stayed un-reassociated only via a `Scale()` helper returning Vec3. (2) A function with a local `char buf[N]` needs `/GS-` when the original has no cookie (the default manifest flags add one). (3) equiv.py needs a `// @ 0x<va>` marker comment on the function or it reports "our symbol not found".
  Fix idea: mention (1)-(3) in docs/matching.md. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [cl2 201-203] tooling — (1) equiv.py's source-annotation resolver misses a callee declared on a one-line `struct S { T f(); };  // 0x...`; put each member declaration on its own line with the `// 0x...` comment. A member named like a symbol the DB knows at another address (MainArguments 0x838020 vs the 0x838320 overload) raises a "resolution conflict" and UNSUPPORTED; rename it. (2) The call-sequence evidence script work/match/scratch_cl2_calls2.py (copy of scratch_s00439110_calls.py with the break-at-first-ret fixed) works for multi-return functions. (3) A getter-then-method pattern `push arg; call getter(); mov ecx,eax; call method` is `Getter()->Method(arg)`; MSVC pushes the args before calling the getter.

- [s00bf9e70/s00adbca0/s00a9bf20] tooling — cmpdis.py shows only 2 lines of context, so any script that rebuilds the full original/mine instruction lists from its output (call-target or vtable-slot sequences) silently drops unchanged runs; and it decodes the original past the function end. Also card.py/Ghidra show `push -1`/`push 1` before a thiscall as an argument of the wrong callee (they were args of the later call). Fix idea: add a `--full` / `--calls` mode to cmpdis.py that dumps both normalized streams and the ordered call/vtable-slot sequence, stopping the original at ret+padding. Time lost: ~25 minutes.

  Fix idea: promote the normalized-diff and variant-ranker scripts into tools/matching; make equiv.py accept /vc71 in notes; remind agents in the prompt to prefix scratch names with the slice id. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00fa86c0/s009ea370/s010cfcf0] docs — (1) a sibling slice's string-literal annotation was copied into a new Havok function and silently pointed the timer name at the wrong literal (processCollision uses "TtMultiSphereTri" 0x014a4570, getClosestPoints uses "TtMultiSphereTriangle" 0x014a4584); equiv.py's "resolution conflicts" line caught it, so read that line even on PASS. (2) equiv.py FAILs on a local int array indexed by an unchecked arg (`levels[a]`, a >= 6 reads stack garbage that differs per frame layout), and its coverage stays ~8% when the tail needs real objects; treat such a FAIL as a checker artifact only after confirming the mismatching input has an out-of-range index (--json shows `args`).

- [s0097cea0/s00e0d100/s00b48110] codegen/tooling — (1) A thiscall virtual call whose pushed args are NOT popped and are immediately reused by the next call (`push pos..; call [vtbl+0xc]; push eax; push out; call f; add esp,0x1c`) is `f(&out, obj->Getter(), pos, ...)`: args evaluate right to left and the arg-less getter is called after the trailing pushes. It is not a caller-clean virtual with 5 args. (2) A float-heavy module needs `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` for fsqrt (no `call _CIsqrt`) and no `/EHsc` when a local with an inlined dtor has no unwind table in the original (EH prolog + cookie otherwise). (3) `x = floor(f)` on a float must use the C++ float overload (x87 `fadd; fstp qword; call floor; fstp dword`), and a hoisted-by-original per-outer-iteration floor needs the call placed before the inner loop. (4) equiv.py: a callee with a static init guard in the image (0x168e7e0 -> `_decode_pointer`) makes every input discarded (UNSUPPORTED), and a global annotation `// 0x...` on a data address in .rdata is "annotation-implausible" (use a literal pointer cast instead); early-return gates (`len^2 <= 0.1`) leave coverage at ~10% with random inputs.
  Fix idea: let equiv.py model `_decode_pointer`/`_encode_pointer` and allow --poke addr=val to preset image globals/guards. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00d18200/s00fbe7d0/s0132cde0] codegen — (1) /O2 stack slots of address-taken locals are ordered by reference count (more references = lower address; declaration order and variable names do nothing). A property read written as one shared `Property* prop` ends up at the bottom of the frame, while the original's per-use `prop` (an inline helper `ReadFloat(list, id, dst)` with its own local) sits at the top; writing the helper fixed a 600-byte diff in cTerrainStateMgr::Initialize. (2) Passing the AutoRefCount member itself (implicit `operator T*()`) instead of `member.mpObject` to a call changes the argument register choice (eax vs ecx for the list pointer); try both per call. (3) An out-of-line EASTL helper such as basic_string::AllocateSelf must be given its real body (`__declspec(noinline)`), or cl cannot drop the dead mpEnd store / forward the field loads after the call, and the inlined string copy comes out with reloads and a different frame (and the vtable-load register order eax/edx flips for the rest of the function). (4) An empty eastl tag passed by value with a user constructor reads stale stack, as in the original (`push [slot]`); a tag without one gets value-initialized to 0. (5) Simulator::Attribute table initializers: copy the s0137ef90 template; the 17-row tCultureTargetInfo table matched on the first compile. Residual unsolved: `sar eax,1; je` for a string-size test always folds to `test eax,0xfffffffe` in my source (any int/unsigned/ptr-diff spelling), and the relative order of three 16-byte locals (rect/string/string) could not be matched in cGameEditInputStrategy::Init.
      Fix idea: add (1)-(4) to the Batch-2 lessons; a tool that prints each local's reference count would end the slot-order guessing. Time lost: ~120 minutes.
- [s0063d9e0/s005d4630/s011ea070] codegen/tooling — (1) A shared tail that differs only in one pushed constant (three CalloutMessageBox keys, two OpenBrowser URLs) matched byte-exact only as separate calls per arm followed by `goto` to a shared tail (callout arms each call CalloutMessageBox then `goto tail`; the URL arms call OpenBrowser then `break` to the final `return true`); a `const T* key`/`url` variable makes cl load the constant into a register first. (2) Out-of-line callees with `lea ecx,[reg]` before the call but no use of ecx (0x5ce9b0, 0x733030) are thiscall members that ignore `this`; slice s005ce9b0 declares 0x5ce9b0 as a free __stdcall, which the difftest will not catch. (3) Static helpers with register conventions (eax/edx/esi/edi/ebx args, e.g. the rw printf parsers) DO compile to the same register conventions here if they are `__declspec(noinline) static` in the same TU, so a Vsnprintf-style function can be written whole; `VA_ARG(ap,T) = (*(T*)((ap += sizeof(T)) - sizeof(T)))` reproduces the original's ap-4/ap-8 induction pointers, but `VA_ARG(ap,char)` advances 1 byte (must be `(char)VA_ARG(ap,int)`). (4) The difftest's random inputs reach only ~20% of a printf-style function; a driver that imports tools/difftest/equiv.py, builds Input objects with pool_over strings (work/match/scratch_s011ea070_drive.py) and compares run_side(orig) with run_side(ours) found two real bugs the WEAK verdict could not. Its `call #N (depth 1)` mismatches are artifacts when our static helpers call externs that the original's helpers call at depth 2. (5) cl cannot keep a local vector's mpEnd in a register across calls unless the callees are visible in the same TU (original: ebp holds mpEnd across ctor/next calls); `#pragma intrinsic(memset)` does not inline a variable-length memset, use __stosd/__stosb for the original's rep stosd/stosb.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons; ship the crafted-input difftest driver as tools/difftest/drive.py. Time lost: ~120 minutes.
- [s00b7a4a0/s00b3bef0/s00b0a590] tooling — `equiv.py` finds our symbol only through a `// Cls::Fn @ 0x<va>` marker comment before the definition or a `symbols/slices/<id>.txt` entry with an unmangled `Class::Method` name (a mangled `?...` name was rejected), the nonmatching.txt reason needs the words `flags <list>` for non-default flags, and a `// 0xVA` address comment only counts when each callee declaration sits on its own line (one-line `struct S { void f(); }; // 0x..` leaves "unresolved reference"). Also `equiv.py` is unusable (0/400 valid) on singleton-chasing UI functions without `--repairs 80 --inputs 800`. Fix idea: document the marker/name/own-line rules in docs/equivalence.md "Running it". Time lost: ~20 minutes.
- [cl2 219-221] docs — Ghidra mis-assigns stack args when a cdecl callee's cleanup is merged with a later `add esp,N` and when a thiscall virtual takes 0 stack args: the "extra" pending pushes become the 3rd/4th args of the NEXT call (e.g. 0x00da1330: `push 0; call vf; push eax; call vf; push eax; call d00a10(ret 0xc)`; also a `fldz; fstp [esp+N]` that pre-fills the 4th arg of af9cf0). - [cl2 s00ab0590/s00a0e4c0/s00ac4ff0] codegen/tooling — (1) `movss [slot],xmm; cvttss2si eax,[slot]` with a fresh slot per site (6 in one function) is the module's `__forceinline int TruncToInt(float f){ __asm{ cvttss2si eax, f } }` helper, not a cast: plain casts, named float locals and ref-taking inline helpers all fold to `cvttss2si eax,xmm0` (a local only spills if its address escapes to a call), and `(unsigned)` goes through fistp/fldcw. (2) `cmp n,3; jb` followed by `test n,n; jle` means `uint n` for the early return but `(int)n` loops; written as unsigned loops, equiv.py FAILs with a read fault on garbage vector sizes (replay of the saved .pkl does not reproduce it because repairs are not replayed; work/match/scratch_cl2482_dbg.py re-creates the live run and hooks instructions). (3) equiv.py stays WEAK (4.9% coverage) for stream readers: ReadInt32 goes through a soup vtable whose fake methods never fill the buffer. work/match/scratch_cl2481_stream.py drives Runner with `runner.fake_override` (an IFile fake: slots +0x10/+0x14/+0x30 via `inp.pool_over`, stream reset in a run_side wrapper because repair() re-runs the original, bswap-aware words) and gets 57.9% coverage, 0 mismatches. (4) A frame of `push ebp; mov ebp,esp; and esp,-16` in cParticlesEffect::Update (two copies, 0x00ab0d90 and 0x00f60f00) is reproduced by any `__declspec(align(16))` local (the true cause is unknown; double spills, float arrays, SSE2 and /Oy- do not trigger it); without it cl tail-jumps the final Stop() call and equiv.py reports a spurious fake-arity FAIL. Fix idea: add a `--stream`/scripted-fake hook to equiv.py and a TruncToInt note to docs/matching.md "/O2 levers". Time lost: ~90 minutes.
- [s004b3e10/s00d3c970/s011503e0] tooling/codegen — (1) equiv.py without --flags uses default flags (no /arch:SSE /fp:fast), so /O2 SSE functions are UNSUPPORTED (__CItan) or WEAK; pass --flags explicitly. Its one FAIL here (mf5c after the float->int call) is likely a fake-call artifact: ours calls __ftol2_sse while the original calls the inlined CRT helper 0x11e0810. (2) /Od sret temps: a `T x = a - b` with an out-of-line copy ctor only keeps the temp+copy call if the operator returns a derived struct (`struct R : T {}`); the temp then sits in the named-local zone unless it is 0x24 bytes. A tree/map local's object size matters (0x1c, not 0x18) for the whole /Od frame. (3) /O2: `movss [esp+N],xmm0; cvttss2si eax,[esp+N]` (float spilled before a cast) was not reproducible by any plain spelling; only an address-taken float local gives it.
  Fix idea: let equiv.py read flags from nonmatching.txt/chk; document the derived-struct sret trick. Time lost: ~60 minutes.
- [s00df3be0/s00b64470/s00c67560] codegen — three re-found tricks. (1) `and esp,0xfffffff0` in an /O2 /arch:SSE prologue means a local of a `__declspec(align(16))` type (hkVector4, an aligned Vector3 temp) exists even if cl later scalarizes it; an aligned 12-byte temp copied with a user copy ctor gives the `movss [esp+N]` x,y,z staging and the frame alignment at once. (2) `static float k = (float)cos(c)` inside a function reproduces `test byte [guard],al; fld qword; or [guard],eax; fcos; fstp` (guard bit set before init), and a float 3-array/struct built as a call argument stays "stores first, lea after" with an inline ctor; the original's "lea/push, then stores" order for a zero-vector arg was not reproducible (145 B left in s00df3be0). (3) cmpdis.py shows every call as a diff (same as the b003 s24-26 entry); I used a copy that masks call targets plus a script that lists ordered call targets (orig VA vs reloc symbol) for the evidence check (work/match/scratch_s00df3be0_seq.py); chk.py's size-filtered best symbol still works for 2 KB functions.
      Fix idea: ship the ordered call-target comparison as a tool (tools/matching/callseq.py <obj> <sym> <va>) so agents can quote it as evidence. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00f1d430/s00ddbe40/s004928d0] codegen — (1) a `static` callee that takes outputs in esi/edi (0x00dd9130 key split, 0x00dd93d0 random offset) is expressible by re-implementing its body as `static __declspec(noinline)` in the TU; equiv.py then tests our body, not the original. (4) A by-value Vec3/float push before a `call` that has a later nested call is the arg block of that later thiscall (FUN_0044d9e0(Vec3, float) ret 0x10), not args of the outer call; count the outer callee's `ret N`/`add esp` to split them. (5) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED (all inputs fault(read)) on functions that walk 3-level object chains (Swarm spawner).
      Fix idea: card.py could print the callee `ret N` next to each call so the push-block split is automatic. Time lost: ~40 minutes.

- [s00b87dc0/s005c7500/s0047e4b0] codegen — (1) 0x011e0744 must be declared as a plain extern function, not `memcpy`: with the intrinsic cl assumes the vector members are unchanged across the "erase" and folds away the following resize shrink branch (call count drops by one). (2) `__forceinline` on EASTL-like `resize`/`clear` is needed to inline a second resize that cl otherwise leaves out of line. (3) MMX loops: `<xmmintrin.h>` + `__m64` locals (`m64_u32[]` for halves, `_mm_empty()` at the end) reproduce the original's punpck/pcmpgtd stream and the `and esp,-8` prologue almost exactly. (4) Out-of-line vs inline AutoRefCount::operator= at different sites of one function: give the object an extra declared-only `SetOol(T*)` method for the out-of-line sites. (5) The difftest only resolves a declared-only callee when its declaration line carries a `// 0x<va>` comment; templates and class operator new need it too. Also: disasm/card labels such as `GetPlanetKey`, `FUN_00641770` on 0x641770 are plain thiscall field getters on the item (ecx), not singletons.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(5) to the Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~30 minutes.

(3) The helper work/match/scratch_calls.py / scratch_seq.py (ordered direct-call VAs vs reloc symbol names, vtable slot offsets, constants) made the "call sequence identical?" check cheap; cl tail-merges two identical-shaped `Assign` calls in sibling branches that the original keeps separate, so +/-1 call count remains. (4) A stack object whose ctor zeroes only 3 fields but whose frame reserves 5 words (eastl vector with allocator fields): add two uninitialised int members. Also: switch case code is laid out in SOURCE order of the case labels (the jump table order is irrelevant); `if (a < b) fail else ok` vs `if (a >= b) ok else fail` changes jb/jae and the fxch. Fix idea: fold into the "/O2 levers" list. Time lost: ~60 minutes.

(2) `if (a == 0 || (b = f(a)) == 0) return false;` (one `||` condition) reproduces the shared tail of early returns; two separate ifs do not. (3) A local that is reassigned from a second struct (`rc = main->GetRealArea();` with a user operator=/copy ctor on Rect) reproduces the shared fld/fstp frame slots, and scoping an array in `{ }` lets cl reuse its slot (SPUICommScreen::Init went from 940 to 0 diff bytes this way). Also: card.py names a function by its PDB name even when the ctor/dtor callee names are misattributed (cConnectionDialog::cConnectionDialog was really cSPUILayout's ctor, `new ("UI",0,0,0,0)` size 0x18); and equiv.py needs `// 0x<va>` comments on extern declarations to resolve them.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons list. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00be6f90/s00fe8630/s0080c5d0] codegen/tooling — (1) float code in /arch:SSE2 modules needs per-function `/fp:fast` (without it cl promotes float add/mul to cvtps2pd/addsd/cvtsd2ss); put it in the nonmatching reason as `flags /O2 ... /fp:fast` so equiv.py uses it. (2) equiv.py resolves callee names only from a `// 0xVA` comment on the SAME line as the member declaration; `struct X { void __thiscall F(); }; // 0xVA` on one line gave "unresolved reference". (3) A `static inline` helper is not inlined where the original inlined it: use `__forceinline`; and macros named like a member (`city`) break `->city`.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to Batch-2 lessons; chk.py could print the obj path so cmpdis/call-trace scripts need no "newest obj" glob. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00b07980/s006b8680/s00c9c1b0] tooling — (1) Ghidra's decompile mis-assigns pushed args around getter calls: `push X; call GetFoo(); mov ecx,eax; call Bar` (GetFoo is `mov eax,[g]; ret`, no args) means X belongs to Bar, and a `ret 0` callee with no `add esp` after it means the pushes feed a LATER call (interleaved right-to-left evaluation, e.g. the 6 args of a thiscall with a virtual/getter call in the middle); card.py could mark callees that are one-instruction getters and print each call's ret N next to the call. Also 0x11e073e is memset (Ghidra labels it operator_new[]), and a push of an odd constant (0x18eb4b7) into a cdecl cast helper was an ID, not an address. (2) tools/difftest marker lookup ("// @ 0x<va>") binds to the NEXT definition, so an inline helper/template placed between the marker and the function makes equiv.py compile-resolve the wrong name; keep the marker directly above the function. Fix idea: have equiv.py skip `inline`/`template` definitions after the marker, and let it fall back to the manifest/nonmatching symbol. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s00e29c80/s00c41360/s005b4170] codegen — three re-discovered shapes. (1) A callee with `ret 0xc` whose receiver comes from a vcall that has *no* pushes of its own (`push 0; call [2c]; push eax; push ecx; call [6c]; mov ecx,eax; call X`) takes the earlier pushes as ITS args: the vcall is a plain getter and X is a thiscall with sret (`Vector3 f(const Vector3&, int)`); counting pushes per call from the card hides this. If the original then copies the result from eax into a named local (`mov ecx,[eax]; mov [slot],ecx`), declare the callee as `const POD& f(Vector3& tmp, ...)` with an explicit shared tmp, not a by-value return (which cl constructs in place). (2) In x87-heavy UI/city code the original spills the three difference components and runs sqrt after a vcall: write `d = a - b; w = obj->GetX(); len = sqrt(...); if (len < w->r + k)` (call hoisted into its own statement after d); writing the call inside the compare moves it before the squares. (3) A `wchar_t buf[40]` function in a UI module also needs `/GS-` (a stack cookie appears otherwise); equiv.py reads flags from the source comment, so write `/GS-` there too. Fix idea: card.py could print "vcall getter + following thiscall consumes pushes"; add (2) to the Batch-2 lessons. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s005567e0/s00e9b2b0/s0072f830] codegen/tooling — 0x5567e0 (1573 B /Od) went byte-exact in about 2 h once these were found. (1) The dev PDB lists real local names for the retail function (scratch_s005567e0_pdblocals.py "<Class::Method>" prints S_BPREL32 names; pdbmod variant lists a whole module): names like editorResource/stats/resourceBBox/width/depth/height/partTotalVolume/fTotalValue reproduce the /Od slot order directly, and 10 of 15 locals were then fixed, leaving only 5 to search. (2) Name search: scratch_s005567e0_bucket.py (measures every candidate's hash bucket with a forward and a reversed declaration compile; the list must contain names from all 16 buckets or bucket numbers are not absolute) + scratch_s005567e0_solve.py (picks one name per role so buckets are non-decreasing toward lower slots, ties = later-declared nearer ebp). (3) Frame holes: a 20-byte `vector` whose out-of-line ctor only zeroes 12 bytes leaves the 8-byte allocator as a hole (add the allocator member); `VSet v((Alloc()));` keeps the tag byte in the temp area while `Alloc a; VSet v(a);` makes a named local; `switch (obj->InlineAccessor())` reads an uninitialised return slot first (dead `mov ecx,[slot]; mov [tmp],ecx`); declined-inline callees (a same-TU `inline float F(...)` too big for /Ob1, or an accessor with a big body) each need `ScratchSlots<N>()` at the call site, N = the callee's local count, found by shifting one hole at a time and watching scratch_s005567e0_map.py (orig->mine ebp-slot map). (4) `fabs(float)` through `extern "C" double fabs(double); #pragma intrinsic(fabs); #include <math.h>` gives the fld;fabs;fstp tmp;fld tmp;fstp x form, a hand-written Abs() does not. (5) __m128 by-value parameters are rejected (C2719) for structs; 12 distinct movaps row copies + movss reads came from a user copy ctor `Matrix44 m(src)` inside an inline helper. Also: `chk.py ... | tail -3` hid COMPILE FAIL (the actual error is above the warnings), so I measured an old object for several rounds; always read the head. Fix idea: promote the pdblocals/bucket/solve/map scripts into tools/matching and have chk.py print the error first. Time lost: ~25 minutes.
- [s01317d30/s00bfbbf0/s00ca6630 cl2] codegen/tooling — (1) card.py/Ghidra attribute pushes made before a vtable call or getter to the wrong callee: `push X; push Y; ... call [edx+0x2c]` followed by pushes and `call cf90a0 (ret 0x1c)` is ONE 7-arg call whose later args include `obj->GetPosition()` (slot 0x2c is `lea eax,[ecx+4]; ret`); read the callee's `ret N` and the slot target before deciding who owns the pushes. (2) A virtual called with `this` pushed on the stack and `add esp,N` after is a `virtual void __cdecl F(...)` member (cl 15 accepts it and passes this on the stack; by-value 0x24-byte Constraint args copy with `rep movsd`). (3) Unsigned float->int with a bare `fld; fistp qword [m]` and no fldcw is an `__asm` helper (/QIfist conflicts with /arch:SSE); a user copy ctor on a 3-float Vec3 turned the by-value zero vector from integer-reg copies into direct stores. (4) difftest annotation resolution needs the `// 0x...` comment on the member's own declaration line (a one-line `struct T { void F(); }; // 0x..` stays unresolved), and equiv reports UNSUPPORTED (fake-arity / fault(read)) for functions that call through vtables of generated objects (cVehicle::Init, civ tick). Fix idea: let equiv resolve one-line structs; let it seed fake vcall arity from the vtable slot. Time lost: ~30 minutes.

(2) equiv.py returns UNSUPPORTED "0/4000 valid" for UI fill-in functions (every random input faults in a real callee such as GetRootWindow/ClearEntries); the only evidence left is the ordered call/vcall/constant comparison (work/match/scratch_seq.py). - [s00466320/s0110cec0/s004582b0] codegen/tooling — (1) /Od name-hash slots: copying another agent's bucket ruler (work/match/scratch_s0040f4a0_bucket.py, 16 buckets, bucket 0 = highest address) and splitting the original's slot order into maximal runs of strictly decreasing declaration index gave exactly 16 runs = 16 buckets, so the name->bucket assignment was forced; renaming 24 locals in one go took a 1936-byte /Od function from 1459 to 94 diff bytes. A shared `od_names.py bucket/solve` would save ~40 min. (2) The /Od "mov [tmp],ecx; mov edx,[tmp]; push edx" temp for a pointer arg is a free inline wrapper with ONE by-value param (`inline void PrepareModel(unsigned m){ PrepareModelImpl(m); }`), while the same wrapper as a member adds a `this` slot copy, and a named local puts the slot in the wrong (named) region. (4) equiv.py aliases outPtr args onto input objects (normalOut == verticesA) and reports FAIL on write-order differences that real callers never trigger; its 400-input default reaches only 35% of a switch-on-dims function, `--inputs 4000 --seed 3` found the alias case. - [s004a6690/s00f52df0/s00575270] codegen — three shapes that cost the most rounds. (1) A serializer whose by-value scalar writes (`WriteUint32(s,&tmp,1,0)`) all land on ONE stack slot (the dead home slot of the `o` param, `entry+8`) is written with tiny inline helpers `PutF32(IStream*, float v){ WriteUint32(s,(uint32_t*)&v,1,0); }` (by-value param, address taken); plain per-write locals each get their own slot (frame 0x88 vs 8). Two consecutive Vec3 writes with `lea ebx,[o+X]` ... `add ebx,0xc` need an explicit `const Vec3* p=&a[0]; Write(p); p++; Write(p);` (a for-loop is not unrolled). (2) /Od: `if (A && B) jmp END` emitted as `je L; je L; jmp END` (not `jne END`) only comes from `if (A && B) {} else if (C && D) {} else body;`, and a bool that is copied from an inline bitset::test's return slot (`mov cl,[ret]; mov [local],cl`) needs the test to go through a second inline wrapper (`bool IsFlagSet(i){ return mFlags.test(i); }`). (3) `return a || b` of comparisons gives the shared `mov eax,1` tail, `return true;` in switch cases gives the shared `mov al,1` tail; per-branch fields reloaded from `this` (no hoisted locals) is what frees esi right after a `push esi ... pop esi` mid-function region. Also: `od_names.py fit 1` never terminates (cannot sample 300 unique 1-name sets), and the K-local order is measured per scope size, so a 3-role scope needs a K=3 compile; I used a small combo script (work/match/scratch_s004a6690_combo.py) that compiles all name combinations at once.
  Fix idea: add (1)-(3) to the Batch-2 lessons; make od_names.py accept `combo` roles; guard fit against k<2. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
- [s00c5c860/s0047d450/s00d7f790] codegen/docs — (1) On a frameless cdecl function the first `mov r,[esp+N]` can run before all prologue pushes are done; count the pushes executed SO FAR when mapping N to an argument (here arg0/arg1 were swapped for the whole function until equiv.py showed the original passing arg1 to the first callee). Fix idea: card.py could print "arg k = [esp+X]" per instruction. (2) A virtual call needs a real polymorphic stub: build vtable slots with a `template<int N,class B> struct VPad : VPad<N-1,B> { virtual void vpad(VPad<N,B>*) {} }` chain (the parameter type must differ per level or the pads override each other), then `call [reg+off]` matches; casting a function-pointer-from-array gives `call dword ptr [mem]` instead. (3) equiv.py needs a `// @ 0x<va>` marker above a function in a slice without a manifest, else "our symbol not found". Time lost: ~30 minutes.

(2) difftest equiv needs a `// ... @ 0x<va>` marker comment before the function or reports "our symbol not found". (3) macOS `sed -i 's/..//' file` treats the script as the backup suffix; use python for edits.

(2) A function-local static with an empty ctor inlined from a helper only resolves in difftest when the statics live in a `__forceinline` helper and its guard symbol (`??_B?1??Helper@@YAXXZ@51`) is listed in symbols/slices/<id>.txt with the original guard VA; otherwise equiv reports a bogus .data mismatch. (3) Stack-arg reuse: the original pushed `(noun, bool, 0.0f, 1)` for one cdecl call, then `add esp,4` and reused the remaining three pushes as args 5-7 of the next cdecl call; cl never does that without LTCG, so such sites cannot match. (4) A tail loop the original inlined from a standalone function (StopAndClearSoundList 0xd5adf0) compiled worse when written as a static inline helper than as inline code. Fix idea: document (2) in docs/equivalence.md. Time lost: ~15 minutes.
- [s0102cd90/s009e5070/s00be10d0 cl2] codegen — four reusable tricks that cost rounds. (1) `/arch:SSE` alone still emits x87 `fld/fsub/fstp` for plain float math; the `movss/subss` code of these modules needs `/arch:SSE /fp:fast` (the sibling slice s0102df20 records its flags without /fp:fast). (2) A struct whose float members are copied by the implicit copy ctor gives `fld/fstp` per member, but floats inside a nested POD sub-struct (GaitC.d) are copied with integer `mov`s, and a user copy ctor `Item(const Item&): ab(o.ab), c(0)` makes cl forward the constant 0 into the copy (`mov [eax+8],zeroreg`). (4) A two-case `if (ctx==0) {..} else if (ctx==1) {..}` that the original emitted as `sub eax,esi; je A; sub eax,1; jne end` is a `switch`; and a static helper that must stay inline needs `__forceinline` (chk.py then showed 83=83 calls instead of 61). (2) An empty same-TU stub (`void F(a,b,c){}`) is dropped at its call sites even with `__declspec(noinline)`; call it through a differently named extern declaration. (3) Block-scoped locals share frame slots (key kept at function scope, rect/pk in separate blocks reproduced the 0x24 frame). (4) The stack-arg bool of a cdecl function is invisible in Ghidra's signature: `cmp byte [esp+0x38]` right above the saved regs + `ret` means one cdecl arg.
  Fix idea: note (1)/(2) in matching.md /O2 levers. Time lost: ~40 minutes.

(3) cSPUI-style vcall args: `sub esp,8` + two float stores before a ternary means the compiler allocated the outgoing slot before the branch; none of ~12 spellings (local h, inline helpers, if/else, pointer) reproduced it for a `SetSize(ternary - a.x1, a.y2 - a.y1)` call, so it is still 5 bytes off (00e16090). (2) Twin functions across slices (s00cb2480 vs s00c2cac0) save most of the work; the layout of `if (a||b||c) {A} else {B}` followed the original only when the fallback was assigned to the parameter itself (`dir = &local;`). (4) equiv.py FAIL on .data written by real callee code (camera apply 0x11f3d40) is a stale-stack read: the callee reads an uninitialised local that holds the previous callee's return address, which differs between original and any recompile; the doc lists uninitialised-stack reads, but a hint to check `--replay` + a UC_HOOK_MEM_WRITE on the differing address would have saved ~30 minutes. - [s0091c640/s00b43d50/s00b52690] codegen — (1) x87 compares are NaN-sensitive and the difftest's "smallint" inputs reach them: the original's `fcompi st(1); jb exit` (st0 = sumR just loaded, st1 = dist) is `if (!(sumR >= dist)) return;`; `if (sumR < dist)` compiles to `fxch; fcompi; ja`, which does NOT leave on NaN and made equiv.py FAIL (ours went on into garbage). Likewise `xorps x,x; maxss x,[v]` is a helper `MaxZero(v)` with an __asm maxss, not `if (0<=v) x=v`. (2) equiv.py reports UNSUPPORTED (0 valid inputs) for functions that call PlanetModel() and then use Havok objects (b52690), and WEAK (3% coverage) for file parsers that call FileStream::Open (MapFileMSVC::Load): no way to reach the bulk of the body; review the asm by hand. (3) A struct member that is 16-aligned (hkTransform inside a class with a vptr) moves the following offsets (+0x10): declare the later fields with raw `u8 pad[]` instead of the aligned member. (4) macOS: `echo "== x"` in zsh expands `==`, and `sed -i` needs `-i ''`; use python for edits. Fix idea: none needed beyond docs. Time lost: ~45 minutes.
- [s00ed37c0/s00728c90/s01070290] codegen — (1) `/GS-` and `/fp:fast` are easy to miss: a stray `call __security_check_cookie` or x87 `fld` where the original has SSE `movss` is just a missing flag (chk.py's diff count does not say so). (2) chk.py diff bytes are meaningless once registers shift; normalizing registers to `R` in the cmpdis output (and comparing ordered call/vtable-slot sequences) was the useful signal. (3) Evaluation order of `PropertyManager()->M(&smartptr)` is args-first in cl 15, so a dead "reset smart pointer" test survives only when the object expression is hoisted into a local before the `&`; also a by-value 12-byte struct passed through a template forwarder copies twice (use a macro/direct function-pointer call). (4) callees in Ghidra/other agents' stubs are often mis-typed (names like `reserve`/`swap` for unrelated functions, `ret` with pushed args that really belong to the next call), so check `ret N` and the pushes before each call.
  Fix idea: card.py could print `ret N` for each direct callee next to the call, and tell the agent which flag set (/GS-, /fp:fast) makes cookie/x87 calls vanish. Time lost: ~40 minutes.
- [s00b99ed0/s010021a0/s00f1faa0] tooling — equiv.py cannot exercise functions that start by calling globals-backed singletons (NounManager(), CivModeStrategy::Get(), SpaceGameGet()): every generated input faults on the null singleton, so 00b99ed0 is UNSUPPORTED (0/1500 valid) and 010021a0 only reaches 4% coverage. Also `chk.py` byte-diff counts are dominated by shifts; a register-normalized instruction diff would show which block actually differs. Fix idea: let equiv.py seed well-known singleton globals (symbols/) with pointers to generated objects. Time lost: ~30 minutes.
